# The notes plan: builds decided on 5 November 2026

On 3 November 2026 the owner wrote eight points and three questions in their notes (trainees who sign up without a
coach, checking coaches' certificates, a trainee's goal for Max, two screen fixes, "+X kg" from what was logged, a paid
custom design, founding coaches, WhatsApp) and asked for a plan for each. The plan became a decision page of 38
decisions for the two owners, the tech partner and the trainer partner; the trainer partner answered on 4 November and
on 5 November the tech partner recorded every clear answer as a decision: 21 decided, 4 with one question left, 11
still to explain, 2 unanswered. This file is the build plan for what those decisions settle. Section 0 lists the rows
in order with their status, section 1 says how a row is built, section 2 says what each row builds and which decisions
it follows, and section 3 collects the choices the rows made on their own, for the owner. The decision page and the
plan it came from are private (the plan holds names and prices); the rows carry everything a chat needs.
On 7 November the two owners answered the rest, in three rounds: rows N10a to N10c and N11a and N11b were added, and the rows those answers settle were opened. On 11 November the owner changed row N9 (Free became an exercise pool with no programs, and Max was confirmed as TB Gym's own coaching) and a critique's fixes went into its outline, written in on the 12th; no row was opened. AGENTS.md, DOMAIN-RULES.md and UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md apply to every row.

## 0. Build status

One row is one chat, in this order. The runner (`scripts/start-plan-runner.ps1 -Plan notes`) starts the next row once
a row is Done. A chat builds the first row whose status is Open, passing over rows marked "Waits" (the owner opens
them once the decision they name exists) and "Open, not blocking". The third column is the owner's word as the
decisions shaped it; an item it leaves out is not built. A status reads Open, Done · date · commit, Waits: <decisions
and why>, Open, not blocking (with why), or Built, not committed (with what failed). What a row left out or chose on
its own waits for the owner in section 3.

## 1. How a row is built

**Where.** On this laptop, by the plan runner: `.\scripts\start-plan-runner.ps1 -Plan notes`, one fresh chat per Open
row on Fable 5.2 at extra-high effort since 12 November (the owner's word that day; Opus 5.5 at max effort before),
with ultracode on: the chat orchestrates its understanding of the area, the build and an
adversarial review of its own diff with the Workflow tool (the owner's word of 5 November), within the owner's limits of
10 November: at most four readers, and one review of at most four lenses with one skeptic a finding, two on security,
tenancy or money (the review's agents had been half of every row's tokens). A chat builds the first Open row in section
0's order, commits on main once its checks are green, and the runner pushes each row that ends Done.
Nobody watches: the owner reads the reports and the screenshots afterwards.

**What every row keeps.**

- The rules of AGENTS.md: the backend is the source of truth and an invariant never lives only in Angular; every
  tenant-owned row carries its tenant and its tests include the unauthorized and wrong-tenant cases; history that
  AGENTS.md names append-only stays append-only; no money rule, discount figure, price or legal wording is invented
  by a chat. docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md gives the kit, the words and the definition of done for every screen.
- The words. A coach is never called "verified", "certified", "licensed", "qualified" or "approved" anywhere on a
  screen, in a notification or in a document the app writes. The badge's words are "Founding coach". A certificate
  line names what was shown and when; its wording is one constant, because the lawyer may reword it.
- Nothing sensitive (health answers, documents, identity data) reaches logs, analytics, exceptions or notification
  payloads. Certificate records hold text only: never a copy, a photo or a screenshot of a document.
- The first download has a 520 kB error budget (510 kB warning): a web row measures the production build's initial
  size before and after and names both numbers in its report.
- Where a row changes a rule, it updates DOMAIN-RULES.md, the ADR or ARCHITECTURE.md in a few lines, as AGENTS.md
  asks. No new docs, reports or checklists.

**Checks**, in proportion to what changed (AGENTS.md), and never the whole backend suite or the whole of Vitest inside a
row (the owner's word of 10 November: the full backend suite had taken 1.6 to 2.1 hours of every row). While a row is
built it runs only the tests it is writing or changing. Once at the end, after the review's fixes, a row that changes
server code builds the backend and runs the domain and architecture tests whole (seconds) and the integration test
classes it added or changed, plus every class that calls an endpoint, service or table its diff changed, with
`scripts/TestPostgres.ps1` and a container name of its own; a failure an earlier row left is fixed first, in its own
commit, and named in the report. A web row runs `format:check`, `lint`, the production build, the Vitest spec files
of the code it changed and `git diff --check`, plus the mocked Playwright specs of the screens it changed. When a run
has built its rows, the runner itself runs the full backend suite and Vitest once; what fails there, and fails again
when rerun alone, goes to one fix chat that commits the fix on its own, and the check runs once more. Every new or
changed screen gets a live pass against a fresh demo in a
throwaway database of the chat's own (`ConnectionStrings__Database` pointed at it, built with the `demo-workspace`
command), never against the owner's API (port 5134), web (port 4200) or the `tbgym` and `tbgym_demo` databases: 390 px
and 1440 px stills in light and dark into `artifacts/notes-plan/<row>/` (git-ignored), axe clean, no sideways scroll,
200% text still readable. The chat drops only the database it created and stops only the servers it started.

**Choices.** A technical question is the chat's (AGENTS.md). A product choice the row and the docs do not settle (the
words, a layout, a default, an edge case) is built the way the row's "Open" part names, or the way closest to the
owner's earlier choices, and listed in section 3, "Waiting for the owner" (the row, the question, what was built, the
other way, a recommendation). A new money rule, legal wording or work outside the row is left out and listed there
with a recommendation; the rest of the row is built. No chat stops the run for a decision. A row too big to finish well
in one chat is split into parts (N4a, N4b), each with its scope: the first is built and committed, the others stay
Open for the next chat.

**Commits.** A chat stages only the files its row changed, by explicit path, commits once its checks are green, then
records the hash in its status row in a second small commit. No co-author or "Generated with" lines. It never pushes;
the runner does, once the row ends Done.

## 2. What each row builds

### N1 · The visitor-count fix

**Follows.** Point 5 of the plan ("the homepage feels separate from the app"), the problem found along the way: visitor counting on the website is off today, and switched on as it stands it would also count the app's pages people open after signing in. The plan does this fix before counting is switched on, whatever else is decided, and the fix needs no decision of its own, so the row is Open. Decision 23 (5 Nov 2026), which would add one count of the sign-up page to the numbers, is not understood yet by the trainer partner: that count is left out here (one call to add once it is settled). Nothing in this row waits on N2.

**Builds.** Everything is in the web app; the server does not change. The rule: Plausible counts only the website's own pages, and only for a visitor who is not signed in. The pages are two, the ones that load the script today: the homepage (`/`) and What changed (`/changelog`) when nobody is signed in; one count each time one of them is opened. Nothing is counted on any page of the app, nothing after sign-in in the same tab, nothing on sign-in or the sign-up page, on the forgot/reset/confirm pages, on an invitation (`/invite`, whose address carries a token) or on a legal document. Today `WebsiteAnalytics.start` writes Plausible's queue stub with `init({ endpoint })` and adds the script once per tab, and leaves the counting to the script; Plausible's script counts a page on every address change the app makes, so once loaded on the homepage it would count every page a person opens after signing in; and `Changelog` loads the settings, and so the script, for a signed-in person too (`changelog.ts` line 41). The fix: `WebsiteAnalytics` tells the script not to count pages on its own (`autoCapturePageviews: false` in `init`, an option of Plausible's current script, on its "Script extensions" docs page together with the `transformRequest` hook; the chat re-reads the page, and uses the hook to drop any event whose address is not one of the two pages, as a second guard) and gains a `pageview()` that the two pages call once per opening, through the queue stub that already exists (a call made before the script lands is queued, as `website-analytics.spec.ts` shows; `pageview()` writes the stub itself when it comes first, and `start` keeps a stub it finds). `Home` counts on `/` alone, read from the page's own address, so a second address for the same component (N2) counts nothing unless its row says so. `WebsiteSettingsStore.load()` keeps loading the script when the deployment names it, since loading sends nothing by itself. `Changelog` reads the settings and counts only when `signedIn()` is false: the embedded page draws no website header or foot, so it needs nothing from them. `RegisterCoach` is untouched. An event carries the page's address alone. No screen changes.

**Rules.** No cookie, no banner, no third-party host, the CSP as it is (ADR 0037, WEB-003). No event ever carries a name, an email, a form field or a token (WEB-001's last sentence). A signed-in person is never counted: the pages decide from `AuthStore.user()` after `initialize()` (the homepage route is matched for a visitor alone by `publicHomeGuard`; `/auth/*` is a visitor's alone by `signedOutGuard`; `Changelog` reads `signedIn()`); no page counts unless it calls `pageview()`. The build-time render still holds no script (`check-prerender.mjs`; `e2e/built/home-prerender.e2e.ts` line 42). Counting stays off until the owner names the script (`Website:Analytics:Script`, `TB_GYM_ANALYTICS_SCRIPT`, validated as `pa-….js` by `WebsiteOptions.Validate`). DOMAIN-RULES WEB-003 gains the counting rule in two lines; ARCHITECTURE's R6.2g paragraph and GO-LIVE's analytics paragraph a line each (what the numbers mean: people on the website's two pages, nobody signed in).

**Where.** `src/web/src/app/core/website/website-analytics.ts` (the queue stub and `start`; `init({ endpoint })` only today). `core/website/website-settings.ts` (`load()` reads `GET /api/website/settings` once per visit and starts analytics, line 51). `features/home/home.ts` (the homepage's component, which gains the one call) and `features/home/home-talk.ts` line 256 (the homepage's settings read, in `watchForTheSection`). `features/changelog/changelog.ts` (loads the settings for everyone, line 41; `signedIn` exists; its foot, drawn for a visitor alone, uses nothing from the settings). `src/web/nginx.conf` lines 124–149 (the `/stats/` proxy to plausible.io; unchanged). `e2e/home.e2e.ts` line 621 (the analytics test with a stub script at `/stats/pa-e2e.js` that records `window.plausible.o` and `q`) and line 603. `e2e/support.ts` (`mockSignedOut` answers the settings with `NO_WEBSITE_SETTINGS`; `mockSession` has no settings handler). Specs: `website-analytics.spec.ts`, `website-settings.spec.ts`, `features/changelog/changelog.spec.ts` ("inside the app of a signed-in person it keeps just the releases"). Backend, read only: `src/backend/Modules/Website/TB.Gym.Modules.Website/WebsiteEndpoints.cs` (`GET /api/website/settings`, anonymous), `WebsiteContracts.cs` (`WebsiteSettingsView`, `WebsiteAnalyticsView`), `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/WebsiteOptions.cs`. ### N3a · Founding status, the server

**Follows**

- Decision 15 (5 Nov 2026): a founding coach earns the badge once at least 10 of their clients have an active plan and they have stayed past the trial month and at least one more month; kept after the discount ends; removed only for cause. Whether the wait is 2 or 3 months was left to the build: both thresholds are published terms the admin changes in a click, 10 clients and 2 months at first, and the row says so in section 3.
- The plan's Point 8, founding coaches from your list: the words are "Founding coach", never "verified", "certified" or "qualified"; cause means misrepresented qualifications, harm to clients, or the coach asks; the platform admin marks a space as founding, with the date, and removes the badge with a reason, history kept; certificate lines (decision 7) are a separate build, so a coach whose certificate nobody can confirm can still be a founding coach; the badge changes no bill.
- Decision 10 (5 Nov 2026) fixed the founding places, so the two docs that still say "first 10" are aligned in this row.
- What exists today: the admin gives a space a percentage off with dates (`WorkspaceDiscount`: granted, revoked once, kept) on the space's page under Platform billing; nothing marks a space as founding; the only platform-wide settings are the price plan's immutable versions, whose first version the ManualPlatformBilling migration seeds with an INSERT.

**Builds**

Server only: no screen changes in this row. Every screen is N3b, built on this row's API, the way B8a built Energy v2's server and B8b its screens.

1. Founding status. One row per mark in the platform billing module, `billing.FoundingStatuses` (`FoundingStatus : TenantEntity`): `FoundingSince` (a UTC day, like every billing date), `Note` (what it is for, required, 200 characters, as the discount's), `MarkedAtUtc`, `MarkedByUserId`; `EarnedOn`, `EarnedUnderTermsVersion`, `EarnedClientCount` (written once, never cleared); `BadgeHidden` (the coach's own switch, drawn in N3b); `EndedAtUtc`, `EndedByUserId`, `EndReason` (required, 500 characters). One open status per space (a partial unique index on `TenantId` where `EndedAtUtc` is null); a space may be marked again after an ended one, so the rows are the history. A database trigger refuses deleting a row, changing its mark, clearing or changing the earned fields once written, and touching an ended row, the way `protect_workspace_discount` guards discounts.
2. The terms. `billing.FoundingTerms` (`FoundingTerms`), global, immutable, versioned like `PlatformPricePlan`: `VersionNumber`, `ClientsWithActivePlan` (1 to 1,000), `MonthsSinceStart` (0 to 24), `Note`, `PublishedAtUtc`, `PublishedByUserId`, append-only by trigger; the latest version is in force. Version 1 is 10 clients and 2 months, an INSERT in this row's migration, the way the first price plan is seeded (the ManualPlatformBilling migration, lines 471-479). Neither number is a constant in code. N8 adds the price guarantee's months to the same table later, as a new version.
3. The rule, named and versioned: `FoundingBadgePolicy` v1 in the module. On a UTC day a space's badge is NotFounding (no open status, or `FoundingSince` after today), Waiting (clients so far of clients needed; whole months since the space's creation day of months needed), Earned (its day) or Ended (its day and reason). Months are whole calendar months from `Tenant.CreatedAtUtc`, as the trial counts from it (31 January plus one month is the last day of February). Clients with an active plan are counted the way Billing counts clients with a plan (`PlatformBillingReader.CountAsync` with `BillableClientCounter`: paid or free coverage, a pack's too, unpaused), but over a window of this instant rather than the month so far, so a plan that ended earlier in the month does not count; the Billing page's month-so-far figure can read higher, and the admin's panel (N3b) says it counts right now. Once Earned, the state stays Earned whatever the counts do later; only an end for cause changes it.
4. The check. `FoundingBadgeCheck` in Infrastructure: for every open status not yet earned, count, evaluate, and write `EarnedOn` (today), the terms version and the count, once; safe to repeat. `PlatformBillingWorker.RunOnceAsync` runs it after the invoice run and the reminders on every sweep (every 15 minutes by `SweepIntervalSeconds`; there is no daily schedule, and a repeat finds its work done); the admin runs it on demand.
5. API, the admin's (the existing `/api/platform-admin` group: the platform-admin policy, antiforgery, the sensitive-write rate limit):
   - `POST /workspaces/{tenantId}/founding` `{ foundingSince, note }` → the status; 404 for an unknown space, 400 for a day after today or an empty note, 409 `founding_exists`.
   - `POST /founding/{foundingId}/end` `{ reason }` → the status; 404, 400 for an empty reason, 409 `founding_ended`.
   - `POST /founding/check` → `{ checked, earned }`.
   - `GET /founding-terms` → every version; `POST /founding-terms` `{ clientsWithActivePlan, monthsSinceStart, note, expectedCurrentVersion }` → 409 `founding_terms_changed`, like `price_plan_changed`.
   - `GET /workspaces/{tenantId}` (`AdminWorkspaceDetail`) gains `founding`: the open status with its live reading (state, clients so far and needed, months so far and needed, earned day, hidden) and `foundingHistory` (the ended ones); `GET /workspaces` (`AdminWorkspaceSummary`) gains `isFounding` and `badgeEarnedOn`.
6. API, the owner's and the client's, so N3b is web only: `GET /api/billing/founding` (owner; 200 with the reading, state NotFounding included); `PUT /api/billing/founding/visibility` `{ hidden }` (owner; antiforgery; the routine-write limit; marked `AllowedWhileWorkspaceReadOnly`, a personal setting, so the pinned list gains its name on purpose); `GET /api/billing/access` gains `foundingCoach` (`Waiting`, `Earned` or null; null for a coach); `OwnCoachView` gains `foundingCoach`, true only when the client's assigned coach is the space's active owner and the open status is Earned and not hidden. Regenerate the web API client (`npm run api:generate`); no screen reads the new fields yet, and the web build stays green.

What the coach and the client see: nothing yet; N3b draws every screen. The words are fixed here for it: "Founding coach" and "One of the first coaches on TB Gym. Not a check of qualifications". Nothing this row adds, an error message, a note or a log line, says "verified", "certified" or "qualified".

**Rules**

- Only the platform admin marks, ends, checks or changes the terms (PBL-006: the global role, re-read from the database on every request, no workspace header). The owner reads their own space's status and sets the hide switch; a coach, a client and anyone outside the space read nothing of it.
- Tenant-owned rows: `TenantId`, the global query filter and write guard through `ConfigureTenantEntity`, a tenant-aware unique index, a foreign key to the space; the admin enters the space with `tenantContext.SetTenant` as `GrantDiscountAsync` does.
- Append-only history: a status is never edited or deleted; ending is recorded on it with who, when and why; `EarnedOn` is written once and survives an end; terms are immutable versions; database triggers refuse the rest.
- Earned under the terms in force that day, recorded with their version and the count, so every badge is explainable.
- The badge changes no access, no bill and no other rule; marking or ending founding status never touches the discount, and revoking a discount never touches the badge.
- A client reads the flag only through `ClientProfiles.CurrentFor(userId)` and their assigned coach; a released or departed client reads as today. Infrastructure composes the client's read from the two modules' tables; the Clients module references nothing of PlatformBilling.
- The hide switch is the only new state-changing staff route open on a read-only space, and the pinned allow-list test says so.
- Never "verified", "certified" or "qualified" in any string this row adds.

**Where**

- `src/backend/Modules/PlatformBilling/TB.Gym.Modules.PlatformBilling/PlatformInvoice.cs:235-335`: `WorkspaceDiscount` (granted with dates, revoked once, kept, `ApplicableTo`), the model for the status. `PlatformPricePlan.cs`: immutable global versions (`PlatformPricePlan.Publish`, `PricePlanTerms.Validate`), the model for the terms; `BillingText.Required`. `PlatformBillingRules.cs`: the named policies (`TrialPolicy` at 61-93, `BillableClientCounter` at 175-249, `PaymentSchedule.Today` at 328). `PlatformBillingContracts.cs`: `IPlatformBillingAdminService`, `IWorkspaceBillingService`, `AdminWorkspaceSummary` (144-158), `AdminWorkspaceDetail` (169-173), `WorkspaceBillingAccessView` (136), `GrantDiscountRequest`, `BillingCommand`. `PlatformBillingEndpoints.cs`: the admin group (`GrantWorkspaceDiscount` 144-160, `RevokeWorkspaceDiscount` 162-176) and `GET /api/billing/access` (22-27, the coach policy).
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/PlatformBillingAdminService.cs` (`GrantDiscountAsync` 270-324, `RevokeDiscountAsync` 326-358, `RequiredAdmin` 377, `Summary` 380-394 with its window of now at 392, `PublishPricePlanAsync` 67-110 for the terms' `expectedCurrentVersion`), `PlatformBillingReader.cs` (`WorkspacesAsync` 54-89 finds the active owner; `CountAsync` 95-167; `StateAsync` 245-272; `ToView(WorkspaceDiscount)` 388-395), `WorkspaceBillingService.cs` (`GetForOwnerAsync` 22-42, `GetAccessAsync` 46-69, which already tells an owner from a coach), `PlatformInvoiceRun.cs` and `src/backend/TB.Gym.Worker/PlatformBillingWorker.cs:79-107` (`RunOnceAsync`: the invoice step and the reminders on every sweep; the check sits after them), `WorkerDependencyInjection.cs:128` and `DependencyInjection.cs:160-161` (the registrations), `ClientProfileApplicationService.cs:468-480` (`GetOwnCoachAsync`: `CurrentFor` → `AssignedCoachUserId` → `Users`; Infrastructure composes the reads, no module references another).
- `src/backend/Modules/Clients/TB.Gym.Modules.Clients/ClientContracts.cs:141` `OwnCoachView(string Name)`; `ClientEndpoints.cs:241-250` `GET /api/client-profile/me/coach`.
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Persistence/GymDbContext.PlatformBilling.cs` (the `billing` schema, the checks, `ConfigureTenantEntity`; `WorkspaceDiscount` at 179-204); `Persistence/Migrations/20260924125021_ManualPlatformBilling.cs:363-460` (the append-only triggers and `protect_workspace_discount`) and `:471-479` (the first price plan's INSERT, the model for the terms' version 1); a new migration.
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Security/TenantAuthorization.cs:85-95` and `src/backend/BuildingBlocks/TB.Gym.SharedKernel/WorkspaceReadOnly.cs` (the read-only rule and its metadata); `SharedKernel/ExecutionContext.cs` (`RateLimitPolicies.SensitiveWrite` and `RoutineWrite`).
- `src/backend/Modules/Tenancy/TB.Gym.Modules.Tenancy/Tenant.cs` (`Tenant`, `TenantMembership.IsActiveStaff` at 286, `TenantRole.Owner`); `SharedKernel/AuditableEntity.cs:12` (`CreatedAtUtc`).
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Initialization/DemoWorkspaceCast.cs:19` (`WorkspaceCreatedDay = -77`: the demo space is created 77 days before the day it is built).

**Tests**

- Domain (`tests/backend/TB.Gym.Domain.Tests/PlatformBillingDomainTests.cs`): NotFounding; waiting on months with enough clients; waiting on clients with enough months, the counts said; earned on the day both hold; kept when the clients later fall; ended keeps the earned day; month arithmetic at a month's end; 10 and 2 come from terms version 1, never code (as `EveryNumberComesFromThePlanNotFromCode` proves it for prices); the refusals (a future day, an empty note, an empty reason, terms out of range).

- Web: no screen changes; after `npm run api:generate`, `format:check`, `lint`, the production build and Vitest stay green.
- Live, through the API, since no screen changed (no stills): a scratch stack of the chat's own (a throwaway database built with the `demo-workspace` command, a scratch API port; never the owner's API on 5134, `tbgym` or `tbgym_demo`); the admin role by the `platform-admin grant` command; the demo space is created 77 days before the build day (`DemoWorkspaceCast.WorkspaceCreatedDay`), so its trial is over and two whole months have passed: mark it founding, run `POST /founding/check`, read Earned if its clients with a plan meet version 1, or publish terms of 1 client and 0 months first and say so in the report; end for cause and mark again; the owner's read and the hide switch; a client's `GET /api/client-profile/me/coach` before and after hiding. The full backend suite with `scripts/TestPostgres.ps1` and a container name of its own, since server code changed.

**Left out**

- The badge image for Instagram and WhatsApp, the printed certificate and the announcement: outside the app.
- Certificate lines (decision 7): a separate build after go-live.

**Open** (the chat settles each, builds the default and adds the line to section 3)

- Who holds the badge: the space's active owner, the person who signed and pays. Other way: the admin names the member.
- Whose clients count: the space's clients with an active plan now, counted as Billing counts them. Other way: only the clients assigned to the holder (`ClientCoachAssignments`), or Coach Today's active clients (`CoachOverviewReader.LoadMonthAsync`, CLI-020).
- When "earned" is written: by the sweep's check and Check now, dated the day it first finds both met. Other way: evaluated and written on the owner's or admin's read.
- Months: whole calendar months from the space's creation day, as the trial counts. Other way: from `FoundingSince`.
- The end reason: free text; N3b offers the three causes as presets in the sheet. Other way: a fixed list of three.
- The hide switch's home: on the founding status, written by the owner. Other way: on the account like theme and weight unit (`PUT /api/auth/me/theme` and `/me/weight-unit` in `IdentityEndpoints.cs`, outside the tenant handler).
- The Settings row's signal for N3b: `foundingCoach` on `GET /api/billing/access`. Other way: a read of its own from the Settings page.

### N3b · The badge on screen

**Follows**

- Decision 15 (5 Nov 2026): the badge, earned as N3a records it, kept after the discount ends, removed only for cause.
- The plan's Point 8, founding coaches from your list: in the app it shows to the coach in their own account and to their clients under "Your coach", with one line of explanation, "One of the first coaches on TB Gym. Not a check of qualifications", and a setting to hide it; not in the space-wide sidebar, where every team coach would see it; the platform admin marks a space as founding with the date and ends the badge with a reason, history kept; the words are "Founding coach", never "verified", "certified" or "qualified".
- N3a built the server: the admin's founding routes and terms, `founding` and `foundingHistory` on the space's detail, `isFounding` and `badgeEarnedOn` on the list, `GET /api/billing/founding`, `PUT /api/billing/founding/visibility`, `foundingCoach` on `GET /api/billing/access` and on `GET /api/client-profile/me/coach`, and the regenerated web API client. This row builds after N3a and changes no server code.

**Builds**

Web only, three screens' worth, in this order, so the admin can mark a space before the coach's page is tried.

1. The admin's screens:
   - The space's page (`/admin/billing/:tenantId`): a "Founding space" panel after "Where it stands". Not founding: "Not a founding space." and **Mark as founding**. Founding and waiting: "Founding since 12 Jan 2027 · <note>", "Badge: 7 of 10 clients with an active plan right now · 1 of 2 months since the space started. TB Gym checks this on its own; Check now looks at once.", **Check now** and **End founding status**. Earned: a "Founding coach" pill and "Badge earned 3 Mar 2027", with "Hidden by the coach" when it is. Ended: "Ended 5 May 2027: <reason>" in a history list, and **Mark as founding** again. Every number and state is the server's reading.
   - The mark sheet: "Founding since" (today by default, no later than today), "What it is for", the sentence "The badge is earned once 10 of the space's clients have an active plan and the space is 2 months old (the terms in force). The discount is separate: grant it on this page too." (the numbers from the terms), **Mark as founding**. The end sheet: "Why?" with the three causes offered as presets over free text ("Kept on record. Only TB Gym's admin sees it."), the sentence "The badge leaves the coach's account and their clients' Me page at once. The discount is not changed: revoke it separately if it should end.", **End founding status**. A refusal stays on screen in the server's words, as the discount sheet keeps it.
   - The list (`/admin/billing`): a "Founding" pill beside the status pill of a founding space; under Price plan, a "Founding coach badge" panel: "Earned once 10 of a founding space's clients have an active plan and the space is 2 months old. Version 1 · since <day>" and **Change**, a sheet with the two numbers, a note and "What changes", publishing the next version with the version it saw, so a stale one shows the server's refusal.
2. The coach's page. Settings › Your account gains a row "Founding coach" (`/account/founding`), shown only when the access read says the space is founding (`foundingCoach` is `Waiting` or `Earned`); a new page `features/account/account-founding.ts/.html/.scss` in the settings frame, built like Appearance. Heading "Founding coach". Earned: the "Founding coach" pill, the line "One of the first coaches on TB Gym. Not a check of qualifications", "Earned 3 Mar 2027", and a checkbox "Show the badge to your clients" with "They see it under Your coach on their Me page. You can hide it any time.", ticked unless hidden; a change saves at once through the visibility route (the CSRF token read first, `getCsrfToken()` at `api-client.ts:68`), "Saving your choice…" while it goes, and a refusal puts the box back and says "Couldn’t save your choice. Try again." (the Appearance pattern). Waiting: "Your coaching space is one of the first on TB Gym, founding since 12 Jan 2027." and "The badge is earned once 10 of your clients have an active plan and your space is 2 months old. So far: 7 of 10 clients, 1 of 2 months.", every number the server's. Ended or not founding: the row and the page are gone; opened by address, the page says "Nothing here" with a link back to Settings.
3. The client's card. On Me (`client-account.html`, the `coachBody` template labelled "Your coach"), when `foundingCoach` is true: the "Founding coach" pill after the coach's name and, under it, the small line "One of the first coaches on TB Gym. Not a check of qualifications", inside the Message link and the plain card alike; nothing when false, and nothing when the coach cannot be read, as today.
4. The words: a Vitest reads every template or inline template this row adds or changes (the admin's panel and sheets, the coach's page, Me's card) and refuses "verified", "certified" and "qualified".

**Rules**

- Presentation of the server's answer only: nothing is decided in Angular; the hide switch writes the server's own setting and shows what it answers; the admin's screens show the server's reading and its refusals.
- The switch is the holder's, nobody else's; nothing is kept in local storage.
- The badge changes no route, no access and no number; a client whose coach has no badge sees the card exactly as today (pixel-identical against the current screenshots), and a space that is not founding sees nothing new on any page.

**Where** (under `src/web/src/app/`)

- The admin's: `features/platform-admin/platform-space.html:200-251` (the Discounts panel) and its sheet templates at 257-302, `platform-space.ts` (`discounts` 155-161 with `discountLastDay`, `discountStateLabel` 220-235, the load), `platform-sheets.ts` (`PlatformDiscount` 483-687 and `PlatformPlan` 691-929: the sheet pattern, `focusFirstProblem`), `platform-admin-api.ts` (`grantDiscount` 142, `revokeDiscount` 157, `publishPricePlan` 101), `platform-admin.html:141-167` (the list row with its `app-status-pill`) and `:175-200` (the Price plan panel), `platform-admin.models.ts`, `features/billing/billing.models.ts` (`AdminWorkspace` 98-114, `BillingDiscount` 116-124, `toAdminWorkspaceDetail`).

### N4a · Certificate record and admin page

**Follows**

- Decision 7 (5 Nov 2026): a certificate line shows whatever proof the coach sent (a certificate, owning a gym, having coached for a while, a degree); the trainer partner confirms it with the coach over WhatsApp and marks it done in a click; no issuer or university is asked and no "valid to" date is shown; nothing is forced, and a check never stops a sign-up. What the line says for a coach with nothing to show is still open in that record: this row shows nothing for them, no line and no "none" text.
- The plan's Point 2, checking coaches' certificates, the rules that still stand: one line per item, never a single badge; never the words "Verified", "Licensed", "Qualified" or "Approved", and "physiotherapist", "physio" or "PT" in its health sense only with a current Order check (which decision 7 does not make, so the titles stay out); a line shows only while the coach agrees and the coach can switch it off (N4b); never a copy or a screenshot of a document, text only (what it is, the day it was shown, who confirmed, who recorded); a keeping period the lawyer sets and an erase path on request, the record gone within the law's 10 days and the erasure kept without the data; "not confirmed" results are not kept.
- The plan put the record after go-live and gave the line's wording to the lawyer (the 5 Nov 2026 record's build 6 waits on that wording). The owner's word of 5 Nov 2026 was to build what is decided, and decision 7 is; so this row builds the record now and holds the sentence in one constant, a placeholder the lawyer's words replace in one edit, and nothing reaches a client before N4b.

**Builds**

Backend rule first. A new record in the Identity module, `CoachCertificateLine`, a `TenantEntity` (so `ITenantOwnedEntity`, with the global query filter and the write-scope guard every row with a `TenantId` gets): one row per proof, about one coach (`CoachUserId`) in one coaching space (`TenantId`, the space where the coach is staff and where the line will show). It holds text only: `What` (the proof in the coach's words as the admin typed it, one line, 1 to 120 characters), `ShownOn` (the day the coach showed it, a calendar date not after today in the space's time zone), `RecordedByUserId` (the platform admin who typed it), `ConfirmedAtUtc` and `ConfirmedByName` (who confirmed it with the coach, 1 to 80 characters, prefilled on screen with the signed-in admin's own display name; empty until confirmed), `CoachShows` (the coach's switch, N4b; true from the start, since the coach sent the proof to be shown), `TakenDownAtUtc` (the admin took it down) and the erasure fields `ErasedAtUtc`, `ErasedByUserId` (null when N4b's keeping sweep erased it), `ErasureReason` (`Request` or `KeepingPeriod`) and `RequestedOn` (the day the coach's request arrived). No issuer and no expiry: decision 7 asks for neither, so there is no box and no column. A line goes through three states: waiting (added, not confirmed: shown nowhere), confirmed (the only state a reader may ever see, N4b) and taken down. Erasing blanks `What` and `ConfirmedByName`, nulls `ShownOn` and keeps the ids and the dates, so the row itself is the history of the erasure without the data. The sentence a reader sees is one constant in one place, `CoachCertificateLineWording` version 1: "<what>, shown to TB Gym on <date>", a placeholder until the lawyer's words replace it, the date written in the coaching space's own language (`Tenant.DefaultCulture`; `Tenant.Arabic` is `ar-LB`); the API answers the finished `text` beside its parts, so a reworded sentence is one edit and one reference test.

API: a group `/api/platform-admin/coaches` under the `PlatformAdmin` policy, no workspace header, antiforgery on every write and the `SensitiveWrite` rate limit, as the legal documents and billing admin groups are. The admin service lists every space with `IgnoreQueryFilters` and sets the space with `IMutableTenantContext.SetTenant` before each write, as `PlatformBillingAdminService` does:

- `GET /api/platform-admin/coaches`: every active owner and coach of every coaching space, one row per membership (`userId`, `name`, `email`, `tenantId`, `spaceName`, `role`, `confirmedLines`, `waitingLines`, `takenDownLines`), plus any membership no longer active that still holds a line, so a departed coach's request can still be met.
- `GET /api/platform-admin/coaches/{userId}/spaces/{tenantId}/lines`: the lines, newest first, each with its state, `text` and parts, who confirmed, who recorded (the admin's display name) and `version`.
- `POST .../lines` (`what`, `shownOn`): adds a waiting line; the same words and day for the same coach and space answer the line that exists, so a double press adds nothing twice.
- `POST .../lines/{lineId}/confirm` (`confirmedByName`, `version`): marks it confirmed now.
- `POST .../lines/{lineId}/drop` (`version`): drops a waiting line outright, nothing kept; on a confirmed line 409.
- `POST .../lines/{lineId}/take-down` (`version`): a confirmed line stops showing and starts the keeping period (N4b).
- `POST /api/platform-admin/coaches/{userId}/spaces/{tenantId}/erase` (`requestedOn`): erases every line of that coach in that space (waiting ones dropped), the request's day kept; the answer carries counts only.

Screens: a third platform admin page, **Coaches** at `/admin/coaches`, in TB Gym's colours outside any workspace like Platform billing and Legal documents, linked from the account menu, from the Me page's admin rows and from Platform billing's top actions beside "Legal documents". The page: the heading "Coaches" with the lede "What TB Gym shows a coach's clients about them: one line per proof, confirmed over WhatsApp. Text only, never a copy of a document, and a coach can have it erased on request."; a search box ("Search by coach, email or space…"); the rows as faces: the coach's name, their email, the space with their role ("Owner · Atlas Performance") and the count ("2 lines · 1 waiting", "No lines yet"). A row opens a sheet named after the coach with the space under it, listing each line as its sentence with a pill: "Waiting for confirmation", "Confirmed 3 Nov 2026" (N4b turns this one into "Shown to clients" or "Hidden by the coach" once a client can see it), "Taken down 3 Nov 2026", "Erased 3 Nov 2026"; under a confirmed line "Confirmed by <name> · recorded by <admin>", or "Confirmed and recorded by <name>" when they are the same. Actions: "Add a line" (a sheet: "What they showed" with the hint "In the coach's own words: the certificate, the gym they own, the years they coached, the degree.", "Shown on" as the kit's date field, the button "Add"); on a waiting line "Mark confirmed" (a sheet with one box, "Who confirmed it with the coach?", prefilled with the signed-in admin's name, then "Confirm": one press for the person who did the confirming) and "Drop" (a dialog: "Drop this line? Nothing about it is kept." / "Drop" / "Keep"); on a confirmed line "Take down" (a dialog: "Take this line down? The coach's clients stop seeing it at once. TB Gym keeps the words for the keeping period, then erases them." / "Take down" / "Keep"); at the foot of the sheet "Erase on request" (a sheet: "The day the coach asked" as a date field, the sentence "Requests are met within 10 days: by <date>.", then the dialog "Erase every line of this coach in this space? The words are gone for good; the dates and who acted stay." / "Erase" / "Keep"). A refused save says the server's sentence under its field. The words "Verified", "Licensed", "Qualified" and "Approved" appear nowhere on the page, the refusal included.

What the coach and the client see: nothing yet; N4b builds their views. Until then no line reaches any screen but the admin's.

**Rules**

- Only the platform admin reads or writes the record: the `PlatformAdmin` policy re-reads the account and role on every request; an owner, a coach or a client of any space answers 403, anonymous 401, and no workspace header is read. No route takes a coach's or a client's id from a workspace member.
- A line can be added only for a user who is active staff (Owner or Coach) of that space at that moment; otherwise 404, as a missing coaching space answers on Platform billing.
- Tenant scope: the row is tenant-owned, so the global query filter hides it from any read inside another space and the write-scope guard refuses a write outside the space the admin set; the admin's list reads every space with `IgnoreQueryFilters`, and N4b's reads inside a workspace see their own space's lines alone.
- Append-only where it matters, in the database: once confirmed, `What`, `ShownOn`, `CoachUserId`, `TenantId`, `RecordedByUserId`, `ConfirmedAtUtc` and `ConfirmedByName` never change and the row is never deleted (a trigger with error code 55000, like `TR_LegalDocumentVersions_Protect`); the one later change allowed to those columns is the erasure, which blanks the text once together with `ErasedAtUtc`, and nothing changes after that. A waiting line may be deleted: nothing about a "not confirmed" result is kept. Checks: `What` and `ConfirmedByName` are empty and `ShownOn` is null exactly when `ErasedAtUtc` is set; `ConfirmedByName` is set exactly when `ConfirmedAtUtc` is; `TakenDownAtUtc` only on a confirmed line; `RequestedOn` only with reason `Request`; `ErasedByUserId` set exactly when the reason is `Request`; one not-erased line per coach, space, words and day (a unique index with that filter).
- Confirm, drop, take down and erase carry the `version` the admin saw; an older one answers 409 `certificate_line_changed`; an action that does not fit the line's state answers 409 `certificate_line_state`.
- The server refuses, as whole words in `What`, the four reserved words ("Verified", "Licensed", "Qualified", "Approved": the platform's promises, not a coach's proof) and the plan's protected health titles ("physiotherapist", "physical therapist", "physio"), which Point 2 allows only with an Order check that decision 7 does not make. The refusal is 400 on the field and never repeats the word: "This line holds a word TB Gym keeps out of a coach's line. Write what the proof is: the certificate, the gym, the years."
- No copy, screenshot or file is accepted anywhere: no upload, no media asset, no link field. The record is text only.
- Nothing of a line reaches a log, an exception, a notification or analytics (CLI-008): a refusal names the field, never the value; the erase answer and any log line say counts only.
- No line, or none, changes anything about a coach's account, trial, bill, clients or sign-up: access and money never read this table. It is a sentence and nothing more: no ranking, no points, no badge.

**Where**

- `src/backend/Modules/Identity/TB.Gym.Modules.Identity/`: `ApplicationUser.cs` (`DisplayName`, `IsPlatformBlocked`, `SystemRoles.PlatformAdmin`); `LegalConsent.cs` and `LegalConsentEndpoints.cs` (today's global identity records, and the admin group `/api/platform-admin/legal-documents` with `AuthorizationPolicies.PlatformAdmin`, `antiforgery.ValidateRequestAsync`, `RateLimitPolicies.SensitiveWrite` and its `ToResult`). New: `CoachCertificateLine.cs` (entity, states, wording constant, refused words), `CoachCertificateContracts.cs` (`ICoachCertificateAdminService`, views, requests, result statuses), `CoachCertificateEndpoints.cs`, mapped in `src/backend/TB.Gym.Api/Program.cs` beside `app.MapLegalConsentEndpoints()` (line 144).
- `src/backend/BuildingBlocks/TB.Gym.SharedKernel/AuditableEntity.cs:37` (`TenantEntity`, the base every tenant-owned row extends) and `ExecutionContext.cs:22` (`IMutableTenantContext.SetTenant`).
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/`: `LegalDocumentAdminService.cs` (an admin service in Infrastructure behind an Identity contract) and `PlatformBillingAdminService.cs` (the admin pattern for tenant-owned rows: `IgnoreQueryFilters` at lines 331 and 364, `tenantContext.SetTenant` before a write at 302, 340 and 373). New `CoachCertificateAdminService.cs`, registered in `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/DependencyInjection.cs`; it reads `TenantMemberships`, `Tenants` and `Users` for the list and the names.
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Persistence/`: `GymDbContext.Legal.cs` (an `identity` table with check constraints and `ConfigureAuditable`), `GymDbContext.cs` (`ConfigureLegalConsent(builder)` at line 342; `ApplyPersistenceRules` at 359 and 1041, with `RejectAppendOnlyMutations` and the write-scope guard over `ITenantOwnedEntity` at 1480-1487; `ConfigureTenantEntity` gives the filter), `Migrations/20261022132740_LegalDocumentsPublished.cs` (the function `identity.protect_legal_document_version()` and the trigger `TR_LegalDocumentVersions_Protect`, lines 77-114). New: `features/platform-admin/platform-coaches.ts/.html/.scss` and `platform-coaches.models.ts`, route `admin/coaches`. `core/api/generated/` regenerated with `npm run api:generate`, never by hand.
- Docs: `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` section 2 ends at CLI-020 (line 211), so the record is CLI-021; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md` section 3 (the Identity row) and section 6 (the platform admin paragraph, lines 390-398); `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` section 5's lines on the platform admin's screens (R5.2b, around line 2167); a one-page `docs/adr/0039-coach-certificate-lines.md` (the last ADR is 0038), since what TB Gym keeps about a coach and how it is erased is a business decision; it cites decision 7 and names no person.

**Tests**

- Domain (`tests/backend/TB.Gym.Domain.Tests`): add refuses empty words, 121 characters, a day after today, each reserved word and each protected title as a whole word (and lets "physiotherapy" through, a degree's name); the refusal's own sentence holds none of the refused words; confirm once, with a name, never twice; a confirmed line's words never change; drop only while waiting; take down only once confirmed; erase blanks the text, keeps the dates, and nothing changes after; the wording's fixed reference, "<what>, shown to TB Gym on 3 Nov 2026" for a fixed date in English and the form `ar-LB` gives.

Mark confirmed as a one-box sheet prefilled with the signed-in admin's name (default: a click for the person who confirmed, and the tech partner can still type the confirmer's name on a WhatsApp "done"); or the confirmer always the signed-in account with no box (other way, which makes the record wrong when someone else presses).

### N4b · Coach switch and client view

**Follows**

- Decision 7 (5 Nov 2026): the lines show what the coach sent and the trainer partner confirmed; nothing is forced, so a coach may keep any line from their clients.
- The plan's Point 2: a line shows only while the coach agrees to it, and they can switch it off; the keeping period is one the lawyer sets, the plan's example being "while the account exists plus 12 months after the last line expires" (there is no expiry since decision 7, so this row reads it as 12 months after a line is taken down); records are kept only as long as needed; the client sees the lines under "Your coach".
- N4a built the record and the admin page (run after it; this row changes N4a's entity, endpoints file, admin pills and ADR). This row shows the lines to the coach and the client and adds the keeping period.

**Builds**

Backend rule first. Two reads inside a workspace, one write, and a sweep:

- The coach's read: `GET /api/coach-certificates/me` (`TenantCoach` policy, the workspace header checked by the tenant handler): the caller's own lines in the open space, confirmed or taken down, each with its sentence, state, `coachShows` and `version`; a waiting line appears as "Waiting for TB Gym" with no switch. Never another coach's, never another space's: the row is tenant-owned (N4a), so the global query filter hides every other space, and the service also names the caller's space and user.
- The coach's switch: `PUT /api/coach-certificates/me/{lineId}/shown` (`{ shown, version }`), `TenantCoach`, the `RoutineWrite` rate limit, marked `AllowedWhileWorkspaceReadOnly` (a coach may always stop showing something about themselves, even while the space's bill is unpaid; `PlatformBillingOnlyTheReviewedRoutesStayOpenWhileAWorkspaceIsReadOnly` and its `ReviewedReadOnlyExemptions` list gain the route's name). A line not the caller's or not in this space answers 404; one not confirmed, taken down or erased 409 `certificate_line_state`; a stale version 409 `certificate_line_changed`.
- The client's read: `GET /api/client-profile/me/coach` keeps its route and policy, and its view `OwnCoachView` gains `lines`: the confirmed, not taken down, not erased, switched-on lines of the coach assigned to the client's current profile (`ClientProfiles.CurrentFor`) in that space, oldest first, each as the sentence (`text`) with its parts. A released client's read already answers 404, so they see nothing; a client in another space of the same coach sees only the lines recorded for their space.
- The admin's page (N4a) now says what a client sees: a confirmed line's pill reads "Shown to clients" or "Hidden by the coach" in place of "Confirmed <date>".
- The keeping period: `CoachCertificates:KeepMonthsAfterTakenDown`, an integer setting holding the plan's example, 12, until the lawyer sets the number; read at start and refused outside 1 to 120 the way `ProductionSettingsGuard` names a bad setting without quoting it; the Worker runs `CoachCertificateKeepingSweep` once a day (a `PeriodicTimer` worker like `PlatformBillingWorker`, with `SweepEnabled` and the interval in its options), which erases, with reason `KeepingPeriod` and no erasing user, every line taken down longer ago than the period, in every space (`IgnoreQueryFilters`, the space set before each write as the billing run does), and logs counts only. A line the coach switched off is not taken down: it stays, since the coach can switch it back on. "While the account exists": nothing in the repo deletes an account today, so the sweep looks at taken-down lines alone, and the ADR says so.

Screens. The coach: a page **Certificates** at `/account/certificates`, in the Settings frame, listed in Your account after "Password and security" for every owner and coach (`settingsGroups`), with the kicker "Your account" and the lede "What TB Gym shows your clients about you. Switch a line off and your clients stop seeing it at once. TB Gym keeps only these words, never a copy of a document." Each line: its sentence, a pill ("Shown to clients", "Hidden", "Waiting for TB Gym", "Taken down 3 Nov 2026") and, on a confirmed line, a switch labelled "Show to my clients" that saves as it is flipped; a refusal puts it back with "Couldn’t save your choice. Try again." under it. With no lines: "No lines yet. Send TB Gym your certificates, the gym you own or the years you've coached, and each confirmed one appears here." Nothing on the page adds, edits or asks for a file.

The client: on Me, under "Your coach" and the coach's name, the lines, each on its own row in a `<bdi>`, in quiet text and nothing else: no pill, no icon, no count. The card stays a link to Messages when messaging is in the plan, as today, and the lines sit inside it. With no lines the card is exactly what it is today.

**Rules**

- The server decides what a client sees, per line: confirmed, not taken down, not erased, `CoachShows`, the coach of the client's current profile, the client's space. Angular shows what the read carries and never filters.
- The coach's switch changes `CoachShows` and `UpdatedAtUtc` alone; the words, the dates and who confirmed never change (N4a's trigger). A coach can flip it while the workspace is read-only; a blocked account cannot sign in at all (`PlatformAccessMiddleware`).
- Wrong-tenant: a coach's read in space B never carries a line recorded in space A; a client of space B never sees it; the switch route answers 404 for a line of another space or another coach. The global query filter is the guard; the service names the space too, so a read without a tenant context carries nothing.
- The sweep's erasure writes the same blanked row as the admin's erase, with `ErasureReason` `KeepingPeriod`, `ErasedByUserId` null and no `RequestedOn`; it never touches a confirmed line that is still up, nor a waiting line.
- No line's words reach a log, a notification or analytics (CLI-008): the sweep logs counts; the client's and the coach's reads log nothing of their content.
- The client's read keeps using `ClientProfiles.CurrentFor(userId)`, so a returning client sees their new coach's lines and never the old profile's.
- Nothing here changes access, money or a sign-up: a line is a sentence and nothing more.

**Where**

- `src/backend/Modules/Clients/TB.Gym.Modules.Clients/ClientContracts.cs:141` (`OwnCoachView(string Name)`) and `ClientEndpoints.cs:241-250` (`GET /api/client-profile/me/coach` in the `TenantClient` self group at :225); `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/ClientProfileApplicationService.cs:468-480` (`GetOwnCoachAsync`: `CurrentFor`, `AssignedCoachUserId`, the coach's `DisplayName` from `Users`), which gains the lines read. The view's `lines` is a Clients-owned record; Clients references no other module, and Infrastructure composes the read.
- `src/backend/Modules/Identity/TB.Gym.Modules.Identity/CoachCertificateEndpoints.cs` (N4a) gains the coach's group; `src/backend/BuildingBlocks/TB.Gym.SharedKernel/WorkspaceReadOnly.cs` (`AllowedWhileWorkspaceReadOnly.Instance`, used as `TeamEndpoints.cs:90` does for a coach's own resign route) and `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Security/TenantAuthorization.cs:87-90` (where the exception is read); `tests/backend/TB.Gym.Api.IntegrationTests/PlatformBillingTests.cs:479-501` (the pinned list).
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Worker/PlatformBillingWorker.cs` (the timer pattern: `BackgroundService`, `PeriodicTimer`, `SweepEnabled`, `SweepIntervalSeconds`, `LoggerMessage` with counts), `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/PlatformBillingOptions.cs` and `PlatformInvoiceRun.cs` (`IPlatformInvoiceRun`, run directly by the tests), `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/WorkerDependencyInjection.cs` (how a worker and its options are registered), `Initialization/ProductionSettingsGuard.cs` (settings refused at start, named and never quoted). New: `CoachCertificateKeepingSweep` in Infrastructure/Application, `CoachCertificateOptions`, `CoachCertificateKeepingWorker` in the Worker.
- Web, `src/web/src/app/`: `core/api/api-client.ts:413` (`getOwnCoach()`); `features/account/client-account.ts:65-67, 173-181` (`coachName`, `loadCoach`) and `client-account.html:15-45, 195-201` (the coach card and the `#coachBody` template with "Your coach"), `client-account.scss`; `features/settings/settings-groups.ts` (Your account: Notifications, Appearance, Password and security; Team for a coach), `features/settings/settings-frame.ts`, `features/account/account-appearance.ts` (a staff account page that saves as chosen and puts the control back on a refusal), `app.routes.ts:290-306` (`account/security`, `account/appearance` with `[authGuard]`, `REDESIGNED`), `core/auth/auth.guards.ts:45` (`coachGuard`, true for an owner or a coach). New: `features/account/account-certificates.ts/.html/.scss` at `account/certificates`, with `canActivate: [authGuard, coachGuard]`. `features/platform-admin/platform-coaches.*` (N4a) for the pills. `core/api/generated/` regenerated with `npm run api:generate`.
- Tests to extend: `features/account/client-account.spec.ts:130-170` (the coach card), `features/settings/settings-groups.spec.ts`, `platform-coaches.spec.ts` (N4a), `e2e/me.e2e.ts:74` (the mock of `/api/client-profile/me/coach`), `e2e/live/client-me.e2e.ts`.
- Docs: CLI-021 (N4a) gains the showing and keeping rules; `docs/adr/0039-coach-certificate-lines.md` gains the keeping period; `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` section 5 a line each for the coach's page and the client's card.

**Tests**

- Integration: a coach reads their own lines in their space and none of another space or another coach, through the filtered set; the switch saves, a stale version 409, a waiting or taken-down line 409, another coach's line 404; the switch while the workspace is read-only (the unpaid-invoice setup of the billing tests) saves, and `PlatformBillingOnlyTheReviewedRoutesStayOpenWhileAWorkspaceIsReadOnly` gains the route; the client's `me/coach` carries a switched-on confirmed line and not a waiting, taken-down, erased or switched-off one; a client in the coach's other space sees none; a released client's read stays 404; a returning client sees the new coach's; the sweep, run directly as `IPlatformInvoiceRun` is in `PlatformBillingTests.cs:547-553`, erases a line taken down 13 months ago by the test clock, leaves one taken down 11 months ago and every line still up, and writes no words to the captured log; the full suite, since server code changed.

- Mocked e2e: `e2e/me.e2e.ts` with lines in the coach mock at 390, light and dark; an `e2e/account-certificates.e2e.ts` at 1440 and 390; axe clean, no sideways scroll, the switch by keyboard.

Checks: `format:check`, `lint`, the production build, Vitest, the backend build and the full backend suite with `scripts/TestPostgres.ps1`, `git diff --check`.

**Left out**

- The lines anywhere but Me and the coach's page: not on Today's coach card, in Messages, on the Clients table, in the Team roster or on any public page (Point 2's later stages: a public page per coach and a Find-a-coach list).
- The keeping period's length: needs the owner, since the lawyer sets it (Point 2); the setting holds the plan's example of 12 months until then, and changing it is one setting, no code.
- The owner of a coaching space seeing their coaches' lines: not in the row.
- Email or in-app notices to the coach when a line is confirmed or taken down: Point 2's later step, with the reminders.
- Deleting an account: nothing in the repo does it today; the keeping rule runs on taken-down lines alone, and the ADR says so.
- A client asking about a line, reporting one, or any mark beside the coach's name: never.
- The lawyer's wording and the privacy notice: needs the owner (N4a's placeholder constant stands).

**Open** (the chat settles each, builds the default unless a reason says otherwise, and adds its choice to section 3)

1. Where the coach's page sits: Settings › Your account › Certificates (default: beside the person's other account pages); or a row in the account menu (other way).
2. Waiting lines on the coach's page as "Waiting for TB Gym" without a switch (default: the coach knows what was recorded); or hidden until confirmed (other way).
3. The switch allowed while the workspace is read-only (default: consent can always be withdrawn, a personal setting as ADR 0028 lists them); or blocked like other writes (other way).
4. The client's card lists every switched-on line (default: a coach sends few); or folds after three (other way).
5. The keeping period enforced by a daily Worker timer (default: the law's point is that it happens without anyone remembering); or by a button on the admin's Coaches page (other way).
6. The coach's "off" does not start the keeping period (default: the coach can switch it back on); or counts as a removal after the period (other way).
7. Each taken-down line erased 12 months after its own take-down (default: at least as protective as the plan's example); or the whole record 12 months after the last line was taken down (other way, the plan's wording).
8. The sentence under the coach's name in quiet text with no label (default); or under a small "Shown to TB Gym" label (other way; a second place for the lawyer's words).

### N10a · Remove maxes to review · Today

**Follows**

- Decision 19's note (7 Nov 2026): the owner asked for maxes to review to go from every place in the app. The 7 Nov reading binds it: every place where the app tells a coach that a client's logged sets suggest a new training max and invites a review of it. The note sits on the card of the plan's Point 4 ("How these work" looks like plain text), whose statuses sheet explains the item; the plan's Point 6 ("+X kg" from what was logged) names the suggested max as the only thing logged sets feed today, and N6a to N6d are the progression that follows. The 7 Nov record builds N10 before N5.
- What exists. P4c (ADR 0036, 8 Oct 2026) made maxes to review an item of Coach attention v2 (CLI-018), `CoachAttentionKind.MaxesToReview`: ranked last, never making a client Needs attention, named with both numbers ("<name>'s logged sets suggest a new Deadlift max", "About 80 kg, up from 77.5 kg"), tagged "Maxes", its action **Review maxes** opening the client's Training at `?action=maxes`. A client's page and the client panel in Messages draw the same item; Clients has a "Maxes to review" chip while some client has one; the statuses sheet explains it under "On the chips too"; and a client between programs carries the latest program's suggestion on the No program item ("Last program's sets suggest Deadlift about 80 kg, up from 77.5 kg").
- The first of three parts. This one takes the item off Coach Today, a client's page, Messages and Clients, on the server and on screen. N10b takes the suggestion out of the client's Training and removes the rule; N10c takes it off the homepage.

**Builds**

Server first.

1. Coach attention v7, in the Clients module. `CoachAttentionKind.MaxesToReview` goes. Its number, 8, stays unused and no other kind's number moves; kinds travel by name (`JsonStringEnumConverter` with `allowIntegerValues: false`, `Program.cs:40-42`). `RankOrder`, `Flags` and `Status` lose it, so a client is Needs attention for anything but a plan's end or sessions running out. `ClientAttentionSignals` loses `MaxesToReview`. `CoachAttentionPolicy.Version` is 7, and the rule's summary says v7 removed maxes to review on the owner's word.
2. The reads. `CoachAttentionItemView.Maxes` and `CoachMaxSuggestionView` go. No program keeps `Date` (the day the client joined) and `SubjectId` (the client's latest program not cancelled, null when there was none) and loses `Maxes`. In `CoachOverviewReader`, `ReadMaxSuggestionsAsync` (two queries: every program's maxes in use with the coach's last keep, then the logged sets) gives way to one query for the latest program not cancelled of each client in scope, the program `lastProgram` names today; it may be folded into the open-blocks read (the chat's choice). `Suggestions`, `MaxViews`, `MaxSuggestionRow`, the suggestion on `ProgramMaxRow` and the state's two suggestion lists go. Coach Today, the client list and a client's page stay one set-based read each; their command pins are measured again and, if a count moves, pinned at the new number with ARCHITECTURE's lines on them.
3. Web. The API client is regenerated (`npm run api:generate`, from a scratch API, as N3a did). Coach Today's `attentionRow` and `attentionTarget` lose the MaxesToReview case; No program says "Joined <day>", as it does today when nothing was suggested; `carriedMaxDetail`, `suggestedMaxDetail` and `maxWeights` go. A client's page (`needRow`) the same, and the Messages client panel follows, since it draws those rows. Clients loses the `maxes` chip, its label, its three empty sentences and its reason text, with `CLIENT_FILTERS_WHEN_USED`, whose only member it was; an old `/clients?show=maxes` opens All, as an unknown chip does (`initialFilter`). The statuses sheet loses "Maxes to review" under "On the chips too". Two comments that count maxes to review in Today's greeting are corrected.
4. `DemoWorkspaceTests` stops asserting that the demo shows maxes to review; the demo's data does not change.

What the coach sees: Coach Today, a client's page, Messages and Clients never say that logged sets suggest a new max and never offer Review maxes; a client between programs reads "<name> has no program running" with "Joined <day>" and Assign program. Everything else on those screens is as it was. The client sees nothing new.

Docs. DOMAIN-RULES CLI-018 becomes v7 in a few lines: No program names the latest program alone, the last item goes, the status sentence loses maxes to review, and one line says they were removed on the owner's word, 7 Nov 2026 (decision 19's note). STR-012's last sentence loses Coach Today. ADR 0036 gains a short closing section, "Removed on the owner's word (7 Nov 2026)", with this part; N10b completes it. README's Today line loses "training maxes to review". `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` section 5 gains a sentence under C1. The notes plan's N6d, while not Done, takes No program's `?action=assign` as its pattern in place of Review maxes, and its new kind takes a new number, never 8. The changelog (What changed) and the dated docs (the critique, the audit, ROADMAP's P4 lines, the UI plan's P4 follow-ups) are history and stay.

**Rules**

- The server decides: the kind leaves the rule, so no read carries it, and Angular filters nothing.
- Every other kind keeps its rank, its effect on the status, its words and its action; a client whose only item it was keeps their status, since it never made them Needs attention.
- The reads stay one set-based query each over every client in scope, never a query per client.
- Scope as today: a Coach's own clients, the Owner's all (CLI-013, ADR 0026), never a former client; a client's page answers 404 for another coach's client.
- Nothing stored changes: no migration; maxes, keeps and logged sets stay as they are (strength observations and mesocycle working maxes are append-only, AGENTS.md).

### N10b · Remove maxes to review · Training

**Follows**

- Decision 19's note (7 Nov 2026), read as N10a reads it. After N10a, which took the item off Coach Today, a client's page, Messages and Clients.
- What exists, from P4b and P4c (ADR 0036, 8 Oct 2026). BestLoggedSet v1 (STR-012) picks the best logged set of the latest program week, and the Training maxes panel shows it while it moves the max by more than one rounding step: "Logged sets suggest about 80 kg, up from 77.5 kg.", the set it came from, How it is worked out, **Review the new weights** and **Keep 77.5 kg**. A keep (STR-013) is recorded in `strength.WorkingMaxKeeps`, and the panel then says "You kept 77.5 kg on …, when logged sets suggested about 80 kg.". The update sheet offers "Use the estimate from logged sets: …" and opens on a lift's suggestion. Assign program, for a client between programs, offers "<program>'s logged sets suggest 86.25 kg (…)" with **Use 86.25 kg** (STR-014). `?action=maxes` brings the suggestion into view. On the server: `suggestion` and `kept` on the panel's read, `suggestion` on the previous program's read, `POST /api/training/mesocycles/{mesocycleId}/training-maxes/keep`, a `loggedSetId` on a max change and on an assignment's max, and 409 `training_max_suggestion_changed`.
- What carried maxes are: STR-014, in `TrainingApplicationService.CarriedMaxes.cs` and `GET /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/previous-maxes`. Assign program starts each lift from the max the client's latest program uses now, unless a max recorded since is newer, and the new program's max names it (`CarriedFromMaxId`, source PreviousProgram, "Carried over from the previous program …", a composite key keeping it to the same client). It reads no logged set and suggests nothing: the coach sees a number in the box and may type another. It is not a review surface, so it stays; only the suggestion that rides on the same read goes.
- What else uses the suggestion's code once its surfaces are gone: one thing. The panel names the set behind a max applied from a suggestion before today (`LoggedSetMaxSuggestions.LoadSetsAsync`'s `includeSetIds`, read by `GetTrainingMaxesAsync` for `ToEntryView`). That read stays, as a plain read by set id with today's filter (finished workouts of the same program), so a max started from the last program's set still reads "logged in the last program". Its rows are `LoggedSetEvidence` today, the record `ToEntryView` (`TrainingApplicationService.Maxes.cs:706-728`) and `LoggedSetView` (`.LoggedMaxes.cs:196-197`) take, and that record lives in the Strength file this part deletes (`BestLoggedSetRule.cs:10-21`), so the read keeps a small record of its own in Infrastructure. Nothing else calls `BestLoggedSetRule`, `LoggedSetMaxEstimate`, `CaptureFromLoggedSet` or the keep.
- N6b: its rule reads last time through `LoadPreviousPerformancesAsync` (`TrainingApplicationService.Client.cs:790-864`) and the workout's snapshot, neither touched here. It names `BestLoggedSetRule.cs` and `LoggedSetMaxSuggestions.cs` only as patterns; with both gone its pattern is `TrainingAttentionPolicy.cs` (a named rule in Training with input records of its own, `AttentionSession` and `AttentionWeek`, fed by `CoachOverviewReader`) and `FoundingBadgePolicy` (`FoundingStatus.cs:280`, a named rule Infrastructure feeds), and its "or in Strength beside `BestLoggedSetRule.cs`" goes. N6c's pointer to ADR 0036's apply of a suggested training max becomes STR-011's update (preview, hash, apply, with provenance).

**Builds**

Server first.

1. The route and the contracts. `POST /api/training/mesocycles/{mesocycleId}/training-maxes/keep` (`KeepTrainingMax`) goes, with `KeepTrainingMaxAsync`, `KeepTrainingMaxRequest` and `TrainingCommandResult.TrainingMaxes`, which only the keep set. `TrainingMaxView` loses `Suggestion` and `Kept`; `TrainingMaxSuggestionView` and `TrainingMaxKeptView` go; `PreviousProgramMaxView` loses `Suggestion`; `TrainingMaxChangeRequest` and `WorkingMaxSelectionRequest` lose `LoggedSetId`; `TrainingMaxSuggestionChangedException` goes, with its 409 code on the preview and the catches in apply and assign. `TrainingMaxesView.LoadIncrement` stays (the program's rounding step), its comment no longer about suggestions. A request that still names a logged set: the API skips an unknown member, so with no number and no week 1's weight it is refused 400 and nothing is written.
2. The services. `GetTrainingMaxesAsync` reads no suggestion and no keep, and still reads, by id, the sets that stored maxes name. `PlanTrainingMaxesAsync` loses the logged-set branch (a number or week 1's weight, one of the two) and `PlannedLift.Logged` goes. The fingerprint that binds an idempotency key (`MaxRequestFingerprint`) and the preview hash (`MaxesHashLift`) keep their shapes, the logged set always null, so an update sent before this change and retried after it still answers its first result. `GetPreviousProgramMaxesAsync` keeps the program, its maxes in use, their names and days, and loses the suggestion; `MaxFromPreviousLoggedSetAsync` goes; `CarryMax` and `LoadPreviousProgramAsync` stay; the assignment's mapping and its replay check lose the logged-set case. `TrainingApplicationService.LoggedMaxes.cs` and `LoggedSetMaxSuggestions.cs` go, the read by set id moving into `.Maxes.cs` with a small set record of its own in place of `LoggedSetEvidence`, and `LoggedSetView` with it; `CoachMayReadLoggedSetsAsync`, which only the suggestion used, goes.
3. The Strength module. `BestLoggedSetRule.cs` (the rule, `LoggedSetEvidence`, `LoggedSetMaxEstimate`) goes, and `MesocycleWorkingMaxSnapshot.CaptureFromLoggedSet`. `WorkingMaxSource.LoggedSet`, `SourceSetPerformanceId` and the derived `Source` stay, so a max stored that way reads as it did. `WorkingMaxKeep` stays, an entity with its table, keys, checks, `RejectAppendOnlyMutations` and trigger, written by nothing now. The EF model does not change: no migration, and `dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes` says so.
4. The demo's comments (`DemoWorkspaceGenerator.Clients.cs`) stop naming suggested maxes; its logged RPEs and pushed sets stay, since records and share cards read them.
5. Web. The API client is regenerated. The panel (`program-maxes.ts`, `program-maxes.models.ts`) loses the suggestion box, Keep, the kept line, `keeping`, `reveal` and the lift id on `update`; each lift keeps its number, where it came from (a stored max from logged sets still "Estimated from <name>'s logged sets", with its set's day and week), its change and its earlier maxes, and the foot keeps **Update maxes for the coming weeks**. The update sheet (`program-maxes-update.ts`) loses the logged-sets box, its `suggested` input and the re-read on a newer set: a max is typed or worked out from week 1's weight, previewed, then applied. Training (`client-program.ts` and `.html`) loses `?action=maxes`, `revealMaxes` and `maxesSuggested`; an old address with the word opens Training as any address does. Assign program (`program-assign.ts`, `client-program-forms.models.ts`) loses the suggestion line, Use, the "Estimated from <program>'s logged sets" help, `withSuggestedMax`, the logged branch of the request and the 409's sentence; each lift still starts "Where <program> left it, set <day>". `client-program-api.ts` loses `keepTrainingMax`. `core/units/load-steps.ts` and its spec go: after N10a only the panel's suggestion used it.

What the coach sees: the Training maxes panel lists each lift with its number, its source and its history, and Update maxes types a new one or works it out from week 1's weight, shows every changed weight and applies it; Assign program starts each lift where the last program left it. No screen says that logged sets suggest a max. The client sees nothing new.

STR-014 loses the suggestion and `LoggedSetId` and keeps the carry; STR-007's last two sentences say the logged-performance suggestion was built and removed; STR-016's pointer to the panel's number becomes STR-011's week 1 reading. ADR 0036's closing section (N10a) gains what this part removed and what stays (P2's own maxes and preview, the carry, one history per lift, the max from a set they did), and that +X kg (N6a to N6d) is the progression from logged sets that follows. The notes plan's N6b and N6c, while not Done, lose their pointers to what this removes, as Follows says; N9c loses "a new max their logged sets suggest (STR-012) is accepted with one tap" and N9's Left out gains it (needs the owner, who removed suggestions); N9's Where drops the keep from its Training line ("training-maxes preview/apply/keep"); N9's tests list loses `P4bLoggedSetMaxesTests`.

### N10c · Remove maxes to review · homepage

**Follows**

- Decision 19's note (7 Nov 2026), read as N10a reads it: the homepage is one of the places. Its Sunday stage, "You both see it.", says in its second sentence that the client's logged sets suggest a new deadlift max and that the coach keeps the old one or reviews the coming weeks' weights; its screen-reader line says the same; and its coach's window, `maxes-dark`, is the Training maxes panel showing "Logged sets suggest about 80 kg, up from 77.5 kg.", **Review the new weights** and **Keep 77.5 kg**. The homepage's own rule: its words stay true to what its pictures show and to what the product does (`home-content.ts:3-8`).
- After N10b: the picture is taken of the panel as N10b leaves it. The other homepage and README pictures show no suggestion (Coach Today's crop ends above the old maxes item).

**Builds**

Web only.

1. The words (`features/home/home-content.ts`, the `see` stage). The second sentence of `body` goes, for one true of the panel now (Open); `screens` says what the new picture shows; the first sentence, the title, the numeral and the phone stay.
2. The picture. The window `maxes-dark` is taken again from the sample demo (`demo-workspace --cast sample`, in a throwaway database) through `scripts/home-pictures.ps1` with `-Only coach-client-training,maxes-dark`: the wrapper hands the one list to capture, which matches shot names, and to compose, which matches window names (`wanted`, `home-pictures.mjs:307-312`), so this takes the `coach-client-training-dark` shot and its 360 × 580 crop at 1050, 300 (`scripts/home-pictures.mjs:237-245`). Its four files in `src/web/public/images/home/` (`maxes-dark.avif`, `maxes-dark.webp`, `maxes-dark-1x.avif`, `maxes-dark-1x.webp`) are replaced. The crop was measured on the Training page of 13 Oct, and `client-program.html` has changed in seven commits since (audit A8 to critique B19): if the panel now ends inside the crop or sits elsewhere, the crop is shortened or moved to the panel and `home-pictures.ts` gives the new size. `home-pictures.ts:50` and `home-pictures.mjs:237` stop saying "with the suggestion". Compose asks for Apple's bezel and the status bars even for a window (`home-pictures.mjs:596-612`), and the wrapper refuses to compose without `-Bezel` (`scripts/home-pictures.ps1`); if the bezel is not on the laptop, compose asks for them only when a phone picture is made and the wrapper passes `--bezel` only when given, with a test in `scripts/home-pictures.test.mjs`.

What a visitor sees: Sunday's stage with the trend and the training maxes as the app now shows them; no sentence and no picture says that logged sets suggest a max.

Docs: `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` section 5, Signed-out and website (2383-2391), a sentence.
