### N5 · The help button

**Follows.** Decision 19 (7 Nov 2026), decided: the plan's option 4E, the app's underlined link look on all three Today footers, with an ⓘ on the help alone. Two of the three footers are buttons and that look is written for links, so it becomes a small option in the shared kit (about 1–1.5 hours). Point 4 of the plan, "How these work" looks like plain text: the words stay, and the app's other text buttons stay. The owners' note on decision 19 (7 Nov 2026) also takes "maxes to review" out of the app everywhere: that is N10, which runs before this row and takes the "Maxes to review" entry out of the same explanation sheet (under "On the chips too", `statuses-help.html:159-172` today), so the sheet's words may already have changed when this row runs; this row changes none of them.

**Builds.** No server, API, table or migration: web only. Three footers on Coach Today and the Clients copy of the first:

1. "How these statuses work": `features/clients/statuses-help.html`, a `button appButton variant="text"` that opens the explanation sheet, placed under Today's Needs you (`features/dashboard/coach-today.html:192-194`, `.queue-foot` in `coach-today-lists.scss:143`) and at the end of the Clients filter chips (`features/clients/clients.html:156`, `.help` in `clients-toolbar.scss:79-82`, pushed to the end with `margin-inline-start: auto`).
2. "See this month and last": `coach-today.html:270`, an `a appButton variant="text"` to `/month`, in `.month-foot` (`coach-today-month.scss:128`).
3. "Show all N" / "Show less": `coach-today.html:478-494`, a `button appButton variant="text"` with `class="feed-toggle"` that unfolds the feed (`coach-today-lists.scss:266`; inside the phone's disclosure at `coach-today-month.scss:212`).

Today a text button is the kit's `.tb-button--text` (`src/styles/_ui.scss:191-230`): the accent colour, no icon, no underline, a surface fill only on hover, which needs a mouse; in light mode its colour sits almost on the page's ink, so on a phone nothing says "tap me", and the coach's brand colour cannot fix it because every accent is forced dark enough for white text.

*The look (option 4E).* The kit's button directive gains a fifth look, `variant="link"`, on both `button[appButton]` and `a[appButton]` (`ui/button.ts`, `ButtonVariant` is `filled | accent | outlined | text` today), drawn in `src/styles/_ui.scss` beside `.tb-button--text`: the ink of the app's links (`--tb-accent-ink`, which the text look already takes through `--color-accent`, `_tokens.scss:124`; as `.panel-link` on a client's Overview, `features/clients/client-overview.scss:105-122`, the "Open progress" link), an underline at `text-underline-offset: 0.2em` (the base `.tb-button` sets `text-decoration: none` at `_ui.scss:125`, so the variant turns it back on), hover to `--tb-accent-ink-strong` with no surface fill, the 40 px minimum height kept (`min-block-size: var(--control-height-40)`, `_ui.scss:138`) so the tap target does not shrink, the kit's focus ring, the disabled and loading looks of the text variant. The help button alone gets an ⓘ before its words: a new `info` glyph in `ui/icon.ts` (there is none today; a ring with a dot and a short stroke on the 20 px, 1.5 px-stroke grid the other glyphs use), `aria-hidden`, so the accessible name stays "How these statuses work". The three footers and the Clients copy switch `variant="text"` to `variant="link"`; nothing else moves.

*The UI lab.* The UI lab (`dev/ui-lab/ui-lab.html`, its Buttons section from line 232) shows the new look beside Filled, Outlined and Text, in LTR, RTL and dark, and `e2e/ui-lab.e2e.ts`'s pictures are recorded again after looking at each. `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` gains a line where the kit's buttons are listed (its section 0, the R1.2 row, and section 4), nothing more.

What the coach sees: on Today, under the Needs you list, "ⓘ How these statuses work" underlined in the link colour; at the foot of This month, "See this month and last" underlined; under the feed, "Show all 12" underlined. On Clients, after the chips, the same help link. Each does what it did. Clients see nothing: all three are coach screens.

**Rules.** No business rule and no data: the server does not change. The accessible names stay the same strings, so the Vitest presses (`coach-today.spec.ts:498`, `clients.spec.ts:274`, `statuses-help.spec.ts:13`) and the role queries in `e2e/this-month.e2e.ts:143` keep passing. The link ink on the page surface passes 4.5:1 in light and dark and in each of the four brand looks and a coach's own two colours (the theme e2e and axe check it).

**Where.** `ui/button.ts` (the two directives and `ButtonVariant`), `src/styles/_ui.scss:110-230` (the kit's button rules), `src/styles/_tokens.scss:124`, `ui/icon.ts` (`GLYPHS`, no info glyph today), `features/clients/statuses-help.{ts,html,scss,spec.ts}`, `features/dashboard/coach-today.html` (lines 192-194, 269-271, 478-494), `features/dashboard/coach-today-lists.scss` (`.queue-foot`, `.feed-toggle`), `coach-today-month.scss` (`.month-foot`), `features/clients/clients.html:156` and `clients-toolbar.scss:79-82`, `dev/ui-lab/ui-lab.html`, `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md`. The global `a` rule (`src/styles.scss:139-141`) colours links `--tb-accent-ink-strong` and keeps the browser's underline; `.panel-link` on the Overview is a page-local class, not a kit piece.

The app's other text buttons (Cancel, Undo, Copy beside other buttons) stay as they are, as Point 4 says: they read fine beside their neighbours. The Overview's `.panel-link` keeps its own class (it could adopt the variant later). This month's "How these are counted" is a fold (`app-disclosure`, `features/month/month.html:136`), not a text button, so it is not touched. No new words, no change to the explanation sheet's text (N10 changes it first), no icon on the two other footers.

**Open.** Nothing waits: decision 19 picked 4E on 7 Nov 2026. The chat settles these itself and lists them in section 3: whether the Overview's `.panel-link` moves onto the variant (default: no); the ⓘ before the help's words (default) or after them.

### N6a · +X kg · the step

**Follows.** Decision 21 (5 Nov 2026), decided and binding: the coach applies the new weight, a step the app offers after the client logged the sets, which the coach reviews and applies each week; not automatic, not a client button (the plan's option C). Decision 22 (5 and 7 Nov 2026), decided: reps must be typed before a set counts, so the reps box starts empty on these sets; no rounding at first, the client types the nearest weight they have; the rule carries on from the last time the lift was done, and a logged weight more than 25% away from the one before holds the lift (both lines are N6b's). Decision 20 (7 Nov 2026), decided: the step is added only when every set reached its reps (N6b's too). With 20, 21 and 22 decided the +X kg build opens whole, N6a to N6d in order; none of the 7 Nov answers changes the set's shape, so this row is built as drafted. Point 6 of the plan, "+X kg from what was logged": the coach picks "Last time + step" for a set, types the step and, optionally, a starting weight; the reps box starts empty on these sets; "Build next block" and "Edit weeks" keep the step. ADR 0036, STR-007 and STR-009 keep their rules for training maxes; this row adds a weight choice beside them, not under them, in an ADR of its own (one page, at the next free number: 0038 is the newest today and N4a's certificate lines take 0039), which N6b and N6c amend. About 2–3 hours of chat, plus the backend suite.

**Builds.** Server first.

1. *The sixth weight choice.* `TrainingLoadStrategy.LastTimeStep = 6` (`src/backend/Modules/Training/TB.Gym.Modules.Training/TrainingBlueprints.cs:77-84`, beside None, Direct, PercentageWorkingMax, RpeBasedEpley and RpeChart). A set with it carries a step, `LoadStep` (more than 0 and at most 50 in the set's unit, two decimals at most), its unit (`LoadUnit`, required) and an optional starting weight (`DirectLoad`: with none, the session shows no weight and the client picks, as a set with no prescribed load does; with one, the session shows it). `SetPrescriptionRules.Validate` (`ProgramTemplates.cs:440-481`) gains the case, and a database check constraint pins it, as the two load-strategy checks ADR 0036's Consequences name. The set shape everywhere carries the step: `SetPrescriptionBlueprint` (`TrainingBlueprints.cs:30`) and `SetPrescriptionRequest` (`TrainingContracts.cs:315`; drafts, publish, assignment, Build next block, Edit weeks), `SetPrescription` (`TrainingMesocycle.cs:810-918`) and the workout's snapshot `WorkoutSetPerformance` (`WorkoutExecution.cs:326-433`, which copies the prescription at start), so a started workout keeps the step it was started with and history is never rewritten. One migration (`LastTimeStepSets`, on top of the newest when the row runs: `20261105223545_FoundingStatus` is the newest today, and N4a's `CoachCertificateLines` comes before this row). `SetPrescription.ApplyCalculation` (`TrainingMesocycle.cs:895-917`) keeps refusing these sets: a step set is never worked out from a max; its weight is the coach's starting weight or what the coach last applied (N6c). The program's rounding rule (STR-008, `TrainingMesocycle.LoadIncrement` and `LoadRoundingMode`) does not touch it (decision 22).
2. *The step survives every copy.* The builder's week copies (`WeekStepProgressionTransform`, `TrainingProgression.cs:58-220`, TRN-025): the weight step moves a step set's starting weight as it moves a typed weight; the step itself is copied as it is. Build next block (TRN-027) runs the same four steps through the same transform (`WeekStepProgressionTransform.Generate`, called from `TrainingApplicationService.Progression.cs`) and then works each calculated weight out again from the maxes in use and rounds it (`CalculateCopy`, STR-011, STR-008): a step set moves there as a typed weight does, its starting weight by the weight step and its step copied as it is, and is never worked out from a max or rounded. Edit weeks (TRN-028: `ProgramEditRules`, rule key `GridEdit`, and `ProgramEditPlanner` in `ProgramEdits.cs`) keeps a session sent as stored, and a changed session's step set is neither worked out from a max nor rounded. Tests pin all three. Assignment snapshots it and needs no max for it, so `features/training/training-max-needs.ts` and the "Needs a training max for" line (`needs-max-line.ts`, repeated by `program-publish-preview.ts`) leave step sets out.
3. *API.* The client's reads (`GET /api/training/me/today`, `/upcoming`, `ClientSetView` in `TrainingContracts.cs:933-953`) and the coach's reads (`GET /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/program`, TRN-022, and `GET /api/training/mesocycles/{mesocycleId}`) carry the set's weight choice and step, so every screen knows a step set. Nothing is written by this row beyond the program itself; the web's API client is regenerated (`npm run api:generate`).
4. *Screens.* The builder's set editor: the load menu (`features/training/program-sets-editor.html:40-46`, `loadItems` and `strategyChanged` in the `.ts`; its words come from `trainingLoadStrategyLabel` in `core/i18n/display-labels.ts:137-146`: "No prescribed load", "Direct load", "% of training max", "RPE · Epley curve", "RPE · RTS chart") gains a sixth, "Last time + step", with a "Step (kg)" box (the unit as the set's) and an optional "Starting weight" box, and under it "You add the step each week, after reading the logged sets."; `set-lines.ts` reads "3 × 10 · last time + 2 kg · start 5 kg" on the grid, the publish preview and Edit weeks; the week copies sheet (its "Weight a week (<unit>)" field, `program-week-progression.ts:109`) says the weight step moves the starting weight only. The player (`features/training/today-training.html`, the set rows from line 237 with the reps box at 344 and the Target line at 384; drafts in `workout-set-drafts.ts`, TRN-029): on a step set the reps box starts empty instead of at the prescribed minimum (TRN-029 gains the exception), the weight box starts as TRN-029 starts any set (the set's weight, else last time's on the same set in the set's unit, else empty; Open), ✓ with the reps box empty says "Type your reps first" and sends nothing, the way `saveSet` (`today-training.ts:336-372`) already answers a missing unit or an effort out of range before any round trip (CLIENT-5); the Target line reads "Target: 10 reps · 7 kg" as today, and Last time stays beside the set. The coach's client Training tab (`features/clients/client-program.ts`, its session sheet at `client-program.html:351`) and the logged workout (`features/training/coach-workout.html:33-66`, the Target line) read the set as the grid does: "7 kg · last time + 2 kg".

What the client sees: on Today's workout, the set's weight (or last time's, or none) and an empty reps box. What the coach sees: the sixth choice in the builder, and the step on every line that shows the set. Nothing steps yet: N6b reads where each lift stands and N6c applies it; until then the coach moves a weight with Edit weeks, as today.

**Rules.** Every table is tenant-owned already (`TenantEntity`, the global filters); the new columns ride on them. A coach acts on assigned clients only (ADR 0026: the routes keep `clientProfileId` and the mesocycle loader answers 404 for another coach's client); a released client is read-only (ADR 0027). The server keeps accepting a logged set without reps as it does today (`RecordActual` in `WorkoutExecution.cs:326-433`): the player's nudge is a convenience, and N6b's rule is what makes such a set never count. No rounding on step sets (decision 22). Kilograms and pounds are never converted. Nothing in this row changes a weight on its own; snapshots keep the step they were started with. STR-007 is untouched, since a step set is not a calculated load. `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` gains the sixth choice under section 4 (a TRN line: the shape, the copies, the empty reps box, and TRN-029's exception for step sets); the new ADR (one page): the sixth weight choice, why the coach applies it (decision 21), the three rules it sits beside (ADR 0036, STR-007, STR-009), and that N6b's rule and N6c's apply follow.

### N6b · +X kg · where lifts stand

**Follows.** Decision 20 (7 Nov 2026), decided: the step is added only when every set reached its reps; otherwise the weight holds and a line says why (line (a)). The wish on the same card, a bigger step for a client who can push further, got no answer: left out (N6a). Decision 22 (5 and 7 Nov 2026), decided: a set logged with no reps typed never counts, and no rounding; carry-on, yes: the rule carries on from the last time the lift was done, so a renamed or swapped session or a new program never drops the client back to the starting weight (line (d)); the typo guard stays at 25%: a logged weight more than 25% away from the one before holds the lift (line (e)). Decision 21 (5 Nov 2026), decided: the coach applies, so this row only reads and says where a lift stands; nothing moves. Point 6 of the plan: "the coach sees where each lift stands", the holds and their reasons, and the fixes the reviewers made part of version 1. Builds on N6a: the sixth weight choice and the step on every snapshot exist. About 2.5–3.5 hours of chat, plus the backend suite.

**Builds.** Server first.

1. *Where a lift stands: `LastTimeStep` v1*, a named, versioned rule with its inputs and reasons kept on every answer, in the Training module beside `TrainingAttentionPolicy.cs`, on its pattern: a named rule with input records of its own (`AttentionSession` and `AttentionWeek`, `TrainingAttentionPolicy.cs:58, 61`) that Infrastructure fills from the rows (`CoachOverviewReader`); `FoundingBadgePolicy` (`FoundingStatus.cs:280`) is the same pattern in another module, a named rule in PlatformBilling that Infrastructure feeds from other modules' rows (`FoundingStatusServices.cs`). For one client, one lift (the exercise actually performed, `WorkoutExercisePerformance.ActualExerciseId`, found the way "last time" is found today: `LoadPreviousPerformancesAsync`, `TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/TrainingApplicationService.Client.cs:790-864`, reads the latest finished workout holding the lift, from any program of the client's profile, by its scheduled day) and the lift's step sets in the coming session, it answers: last time's day and each set's logged weight and reps, and per set either a new weight (that set's last logged weight plus the set's step, exact) or a hold with a reason. The lines, each one the owner can change in a later version:
   - (a) every set of the lift last time, in the performance line (f) matches and whatever its weight choice was then, is logged (`IsCompleted`) with typed reps at or above the reps that workout asked for (the snapshot's `PrescribedRepetitionsMaximum`: the top of a range, a fixed target itself); otherwise the lift holds: "Same weight · set 3 reached 8 of 10 on Tue". With carry-on, line (d), last time can be a program that wrote the lift as a typed weight, so the line reads every logged set of it, never step sets alone (warm-ups: Open). A set logged with no reps typed never counts as reached (decision 22). [decision 20, 7 Nov 2026]
   - (b) last time is within the last 14 days (the workout's day in the program's time zone); otherwise: "Same weight · last done Tue 13 Oct, more than 14 days ago".
   - (c) not rated harder than the coach's target: where the set had a target effort and the client rated it, a rating above the target holds: "Same weight · set 2 felt harder than planned (RPE 9 of 8)"; no rating is not a hold.
   - (d) carry-on: last time is the last finished workout of this lift whatever its program or session name, so a renamed or swapped session, or a new program, never drops the client back to the coming set's starting weight: the answer starts from last time's logged weight, plus the step, or, on a hold of (a) or (c), that same weight named as the one to keep. The coming set keeps its own weight (its starting weight, or what the coach last applied) only when the lift was never done, when last time is more than 14 days old (b), or on a hold of (e), (g), (i) or the unit. [decision 22, 7 Nov 2026]
   - (e) typo guard: a set's weight logged last time more than 25% above or below the same set's weight the time before holds the lift: "Same weight · 55 kg logged on Tue looks like a typo (5.5 kg the time before)". A finished workout cannot be edited (`WorkoutExecution.EnsureMutable`: completed history is immutable), so the hold stands until a later finished workout logs the set within the line, and the coach can type the weight with Edit weeks meanwhile. A first log has nothing to compare and never trips it. [decision 22, 7 Nov 2026]
   - (f) a lift written twice in one session (a top set and a back-off) is matched by order: the first prescription of the lift to the first performance of it, then set by set by `Position`; never mixed.
   - (g) a forgotten Finish holds with a true reason: a workout in progress is not last time; the line reads "Finish Monday's workout to go up", naming the day.
   - (h) weeks whose reps step up never block for ever: (a) reads the reps that workout asked, never this week's.
   - (i) each set moves by its own number (5 → 7, 6 → 8); a coming set with no match last time (a week added a set) keeps its weight and says so.
   - A weight logged in the other unit than the set's does not count and holds ("logged in lb"); nothing is converted.
2. *API.* The coach's reads (`GET /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/program`, TRN-022, and `GET /api/training/mesocycles/{mesocycleId}`) and the logged workout's read (`GET /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/workouts/{workoutExecutionId}`, `GetCoachWorkoutDetail`, `TrainingEndpoints.cs:519`) carry, per step set of a coming session, the rule's answer, read-only, through the coach's training reads (TRN-023) and for the coach's own clients only (CLI-013). The logged workout's read answers with the client's own `ClientWorkoutView` and `ClientSetView`, so the answer rides the coach's read alone and never reaches the client's reads under `/api/training/me`. Nothing is written by this row; the web's API client is regenerated.
3. *Screens.* The coach's client Training tab (`features/clients/client-program.ts`, its session sheet at `client-program.html:351`) and the logged workout (`features/training/coach-workout.html:33-66`, the Target and Actual lines): each step set says where it stands, "Last 15 kg × 10, 10, 10 on Tue · next time 17 kg if every set is done" or the hold's reason. The client's screens do not change (decision 21: the coach moves the weight).

What the coach sees: on the client's Training and on a logged workout, the standing line per lift. What the client sees: nothing new.

**Rules.** The rule runs on the server, named and versioned, with its inputs and reasons on every answer; a set logged with no reps typed never counts as reached (decision 22); no rounding (decision 22); kilograms and pounds are never converted; nothing changes a weight. Tenant scope, a coach's own clients (ADR 0026) and a released client read-only (ADR 0027), as N6a. STR-007 is untouched: a step is a separate strategy from what was logged, which STR-007 itself keeps apart from a max. `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` gains the rule's lines under section 5 (a new STR number); N6a's ADR amended with the lines the owner can change.

**Where.** Backend: `src/backend/Modules/Training/TB.Gym.Modules.Training/TrainingAttentionPolicy.cs` (the pattern: training signals v1, `MissedLookbackDays` 14, its input records `AttentionSession` 58 and `AttentionWeek` 61, filled in `TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/CoachOverviewReader.cs`), `TrainingContracts.cs` (the coach's set views; `CoachWorkoutDetailResult`, whose `ClientWorkoutView` and `ClientSetView` the client's reads share), `TrainingEndpoints.cs` (the program read at 489, the mesocycle read at 111, the logged workout's at 519), `WorkoutExecution.cs` (`WorkoutSetPerformance`, `IsCompleted`, `PrescribedRepetitionsMaximum`; `EnsureMutable`), `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/TrainingApplicationService.Client.cs:790-864` (`LoadPreviousPerformancesAsync`), `.Reads.cs`, `.Mapping.cs`; `src/backend/Modules/PlatformBilling/TB.Gym.Modules.PlatformBilling/FoundingStatus.cs:280` (`FoundingBadgePolicy`, a named rule in one module that Infrastructure feeds from other modules' rows, in `TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/FoundingStatusServices.cs`). Web: `features/clients/client-program.{ts,html}`, `features/training/coach-workout.{ts,html}`, `training-read.models.ts`, `core/api/generated/`. Docs: `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` section 5, N6a's ADR.

**Tests.** Domain: `LastTimeStepDomainTests.cs` gains a fixed reference case for each line (a) to (i) and the unit hold. Integration (beside `Phase3TrainingWorkflowTests.cs` and `ClosedBetaTrainingReadTests.cs`): the coach's read says where the lift stands after every set done, after a missed rep, after a set logged without reps, after a typo and after the sensible log that follows it, after 15 days, after a swapped session name, after a new program (last time's weight, never the starting weight), after a new program whose lift was a typed weight last time (line (a) reads those sets), after a forgotten Finish, and for a lift written twice in one session, on the program, mesocycle and logged workout reads; a client on the coach reads 403, another coach's client 404, the wrong tenant 404, closed training reads (TRN-023) carry no answer, and the client's own today and upcoming reads carry none. Server code changed, so the full backend suite with `scripts/TestPostgres.ps1`, then the web checks and `git diff --check`.

**Left out.** Applying a weight (N6c); Coach Today (N6d); everything N6a left out. A "held 3 times in a row" line on Coach Today (the plan's optional extra, 2–3 hours). A line for the client: nothing is applied here.

**Open.** Nothing waits: decisions 20, 21 and 22 are decided (5 and 7 Nov 2026), and lines (a), (d) and (e) carry them. The chat settles these itself, with the default first, and lists them in section 3: where the rule lives (Training); the words of each hold reason (above); whether the coming session's extra set takes the last matched set's weight or keeps its own (keeps its own); whether the 14 days count from the workout's day or from its finish (its day, in the program's time zone); which log the typo guard compares with (the latest earlier log of the set that did not itself trip the guard, so one typo holds once and the next sensible log steps again; the other way: always the log just before); whether last time's warm-up sets count in line (a) (no, as no week step moves a warm-up, TRN-025; the other way: every set, warm-ups included).

### N6c · +X kg · applying a weight

**Follows.** Decision 21 (5 Nov 2026): the coach applies the new weight each week, not the app and not the client. Decisions 20 and 22 (5 and 7 Nov 2026), decided: the step only when every set reached its reps; reps typed first; no rounding; carry-on from the last time the lift was done; the 25% typo guard. All of them live in N6b's rule, which says per set the new weight or the hold and its reason; nothing here computes a weight. Builds on N6b as built (`LastTimeStepRule`, STR-019, `LiftStandingView` on the coach's program, mesocycle and logged workout reads). The chat that opened this row wrote this spec from that outline (10 Nov 2026).

**Builds.** Server first.

1. *What an apply writes.* `LastTimeStepRule.SetsToApply(answer)`: each set the answer moves to a weight it does not have yet, a step up (`StepUp`: last time's weight plus its step) or last time's weight kept on a set at another weight (`SameAsLastTime`: a hold of line (a) or (c), a set carried on, or a step past 2,000), so a new program's starting weight carries on (decision 22's carry-on, the row's default). A hold that keeps each set's own weight (never done, 14 days, a forgotten Finish, nothing to match, another unit, a likely typo) applies nothing, and a set already at the answer's weight is left alone. `LastTimeStepRule.Explain` says in words why a set holds its weight ("15 kg logged on 2026-11-03 plus the 2 kg step (LastTimeStep v1), applied by the coach.").
2. *The program stores it.* `TrainingMesocycle.ApplyLiftWeights` → `SetPrescription.ApplyLiftWeight`: only a "Last time + step" set of a session still open (`ProgramEditRules.IsOpen`, judged on the answer's own day), in the set's own unit, more than 0 and at most 2,000 to three decimals; the weight becomes the set's own, `DirectLoad` and the prescribed load together, as a typed starting weight is (TRN-032), with the rule's key, version and the words of (1) in the set's calculation fields; the step stays; the program's version moves on (`RegisterMutation`), so a start, an Edit weeks sitting or another apply read before it conflicts. Edit weeks keeps it (sent as stored, or rebuilt from the grid, which sends the set's weight back), Build next block copies it as a typed weight, and the workout's snapshot starts at it. The database already refuses a change to a started session's sets (`TR_SetPrescriptions_ProtectStarted`).
3. *The record.* One append-only row per lift and apply, `training."LiftWeightApplications"` (`LiftWeightApplication`, Training): the client, the program, the session (name and day), the writing (exercise prescription, lift, occurrence), the rule's key and version, the answer's outcome and reason, last time's workout and day, the day it was judged, the answer itself as read (JSON, last time's logged sets included), the sets it wrote (each set's id, place, move, weight before and after, unit and step), who applied it and when (the row's own stamps), and the idempotency key with the whole request's fingerprint (one row per key and lift, and one request per key: an exclusion constraint). Sets and sessions are named by id with no foreign key (Edit weeks replaces a changed session's rows); the row names its program. A trigger and the context's guard keep it append-only. One migration, `LiftWeightApplications`, whose Down refuses while any row exists.
4. *API.* `POST /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/lift-weights/apply` (`ApplyLiftWeights`): `{ lifts: [{ exercisePrescriptionId, sets: [{ setPrescriptionId, currentWeight, weight }] }], idempotencyKey }`, one lift or every lift of the client ("Apply all", up to 100 lifts of up to 20 sets). The server reads the answers again (`ReadLiftStandingsAsync`) and writes only when each lift's request names exactly `SetsToApply` of its answer now, each set from and to the weights the coach read, and each set still holds that weight when written (a weight of 0 is never offered); otherwise 409 `lift_standing_changed` (the first answer instead, when the same request already went through), and the coach reads it again. It answers the lifts it wrote (`LiftWeightsAppliedView`: per lift the session, its day, the sets before and after, when); a retry with the same key and request answers with the first answer, rebuilt from the rows; another request under the key answers 409 `idempotency_conflict`; a lost race 409 `concurrency_conflict` (or the first answer, when the winner was the same request). A coach acts on their own clients only (the tenant handler, 404), a released client is refused (409 `client_released`), the coach's training read must be open (TRN-023, else 403), and a workspace read-only for an unpaid bill refuses it (403 `workspace_read_only`): the route is a write like any other and is not on the reviewed list.
5. *The client's line.* `ClientExerciseView.WeightNote` on the client's reads of a workout still to do or running (`GET /api/training/me/today`, `/me/upcoming`'s unfinished workouts; the coach's logged workout read shares the view): the latest record of that writing, while every set it wrote still holds that weight and the lift is done as written (not swapped); one query, made only when a set of the read carries the rule's key. It carries the client's own numbers only (the move, each stepped set's rise, last time's day, working sets and reps asked, a hold's reason), never who applied it.
6. *Screens.* The coach's client Training tab: "Weights to apply", first on the tab while any lift's answer moves a weight (from the program read's answers, every program of the client), one row per lift with its name, its workout and day, N6b's line and an outlined "Apply 17 kg", and "Apply all" for two or more; after an apply the page says what changed and reads the program again; nothing is offered on a former client's record. The session sheet (`program-session.ts`) and the logged workout (`coach-workout.*`) put the same Apply under N6b's line, the sheet reading the workout again and saying what changed where the coach is. The client's workout player (`today-training.*`) says why under the lift's Last time, outside the sets: "Up 2 kg · all 3 × 10 done on Tue", or "Same weight as last time · set 1 reached 11 of 12 on Tue 3 Nov"; the Target line stays as it was, and typing another weight still wins (TRN-029).

What the coach sees: the weights waiting on each client's Training tab, Apply per lift there, in a session sheet and under a logged workout, and Apply all. What the client sees: the session at the weight the coach applied, with one line saying why.

**Rules.** Actuals never overwrite prescribed values; applying is a coach edit of the prescription with provenance, kept append-only, as Edit weeks keeps each edit. A coach acts on assigned clients only (ADR 0026, `clientProfileId` routes); a released client is read-only (ADR 0027). No automatic change when the client opens a session (decision 21). No rounding, no conversion (decision 22). Two coaches applying the same lift at once resolve by optimistic concurrency, the second asked to re-read. Applying is a state-changing staff write, so a space read-only for an unpaid bill refuses it (PBL-005): there is no preview route, the coach's reads already carry the answer. `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` gains STR-020 (and TRN-032, STR-019 point at it); ADR 0040 is amended with "Applying a weight"; ARCHITECTURE.md section 13 and the UI plan say so in a few lines.

**Where.** Backend: `src/backend/Modules/Training/TB.Gym.Modules.Training/` (`LastTimeStepRule.cs` `SetsToApply` and `Explain`; `TrainingMesocycle.cs` `ApplyLiftWeights` and `SetPrescription.ApplyLiftWeight`; `TrainingBlueprints.cs` `AppliedLiftWeight`; `LiftWeightApplications.cs`, new: the record, `LiftWeightTarget`, `AppliedLiftSet` and `LiftWeightNotes`; `TrainingContracts.cs` the request, the answer, `ClientWeightNoteView`, `ClientExerciseView.WeightNote`; `TrainingEndpoints.cs` the route); `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/TrainingApplicationService.LiftWeights.cs` (new: the apply, its replay, the client's note), `.Client.cs` and `.Reads.cs` (the note on the reads); `Persistence/GymDbContext.Training.cs`, `GymDbContext.cs`, `DatabaseConstraintNames.cs`, `Migrations/*_LiftWeightApplications.cs`. Web: `features/clients/lift-weights.ts` (new: what applies, the request, the words, `LiftWeightApplier`), `program-weights.ts` (new: the panel), `client-program.{ts,html}`, `program-session.ts`, `client-program-api.ts`; `features/training/coach-workout.{ts,html,scss}`, `standing-words.ts` (shared words), `weight-note-words.ts` (new), `today-training.{ts,html}`, `today-training-note.scss` (new); `core/api/generated/` (regenerated). Docs: `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md`, ADR 0040, `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`, `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md`.

**Tests.** Domain (`LastTimeStepDomainTests.Apply.cs`): what a step, a kept weight and each hold apply; the explanation's words; the set written as its own weight with its step and the rule's key; refusals (a session started or before today, another weight choice, another unit, nothing, past 2,000, finer than a gram, a set of another program, a cancelled program); Edit weeks keeps an applied weight; the record keeps the rule and refuses what is not an apply; the client's line from a step and from a kept weight. Integration (`N6cApplyLiftWeightsTests.cs`): apply as the client's coach, the program's sets, the record, the client's line on the session's day and once started, gone once finished; a new program's starting weight carried on; every stale or wrong request refused with nothing written; a retry and a reused key; two applies at once and the same key twice at once; who may apply (a client 403, another coach's client 404, another space 404 and 403 for its `X-Tenant-Id`, anonymous 401, no antiforgery 400, a blocked relationship 403, a released client 409); a read-only workspace 403; Edit weeks after an apply; append-only; the migration's revert and its refusal. Vitest: `lift-weights.spec.ts`, `program-weights.spec.ts`, `weight-note-words.spec.ts`, `client-program.spec.ts`, `coach-workout.spec.ts`, `today-training.interaction.spec.ts`. Mocked e2e: `e2e/client-training.e2e.ts` (the panel at 1440 and 390, dark and right to left, the sheet's apply and a conflict), `e2e/workout-progress.e2e.ts` (the client's line at 390 and 1440). Live pass on a throwaway demo; the full backend suite.

**Left out.** An automatic weight (the plan's option A) and a client button (option B): decision 21 chose the coach. Rounding (decision 22). Coach Today's line and its `?action=` opening (N6d). A bigger step for a client who can push further (decision 20's wish, unanswered on 7 Nov 2026). A notice to the client when a weight is applied (section 3). Undoing an apply other than by typing a weight with Edit weeks.

**Open.** Settled by this row's chat, each in section 3: "Apply all" per client (the row's default); a hold that names last time's weight on a coming set at another weight is offered (the row's default); the panel's place, title and words; the buttons' words; no confirm before Apply all; the client's line's words; where the client sees it; no notice; the words after an apply.

### N6d · +X kg · Coach Today

**Follows.** Decision 21 (5 Nov 2026): the weekly apply is the coach's homework, so Coach Today has to show it. Decisions 20 and 22 (7 Nov 2026) are decided and live in N6b's rule. Builds on N6c as built (`LastTimeStepRule.SetsToApply`, STR-020, and "Weights to apply", first on the client's Training tab). The owners' note on decision 19 (7 Nov 2026) takes "maxes to review" out of the app (N10), so this line is no suggestion of a new max: it counts what N6b's rule says N6c would write. The chat that opened this row wrote this spec from that outline (10 Nov 2026).

**Builds.** Server first.

1. *What counts.* Per client, one for each writing of a lift whose answer writes a weight (`SetsToApply` not empty: a step up, or last time's weight kept on a set at another weight, N6c's "Keep 25 kg"), exactly the rows "Weights to apply" lists; with how many of them step up (the answer's outcome) and the soonest of their coming workouts' days, each in its program's calendar. A lift leaves once applied, or once its answer writes nothing: held at each set's own weight (a likely typo, a running workout), its workout started, last done more than 14 days ago.
2. *The rule.* Coach attention v8 (`CoachAttentionPolicy`, CLI-018): `CoachAttentionKind.WeightsToApply = 12` (8 stays unused), raised from a count in `ClientAttentionSignals` (Clients sees no Training type), ranked last, after No program, and never Needs attention: alone it leaves the plan's own status. Within the kind the soonest workout comes first, then by name. Read only while training is in today's plan, as every training signal is (CLI-018).
3. *The read.* `LiftStandingsReader` (Infrastructure, new) takes N6b's per-client read and serves both: the Training tab's reads call it for one client with the whole history (two commands, as before), and `CoachOverviewReader` for every client in scope (`ReadRecentAsync`): the coming step sets (each lift's first open session on or after the UTC day after, and the few before it); those lifts' workouts of the last 15 days; and the workouts before them only of a writing whose last weeks cannot settle the typo guard, as `LastTimeStepRule.RecentSettles` (new, pure, in Training) says: a log within 25% of the weight its own workout asked stands whatever came before it, so a set with such a log, or with no recent log, folds alike either way, and an answer that ends before the guard needs none. Each history read takes exactly the clients coming to each lift. Three commands at most, whatever the client count; the rows grow with a client's history only for a lift given no weight its client logged near in those 15 days (audit BEB-2: Coach Today is read every 4 s while the coach is around; section 3).
4. *API.* `CoachAttentionItemView` gains `StepUps`; the kind travels by name; Today, the client list and a client's page carry the item, so the page never disagrees with the list. The web's API client is regenerated.
5. *Screens.* Coach Today's Needs you, last: "Maya Fakhoury has 3 lifts ready to step up" ("has a lift ready to step up"; "has 2 weights to apply" or "has a weight to apply" when one keeps last time's weight), "Next workout today", "tomorrow" or "Tue 17 Nov", the Weights tag in the share tone and Review weights, which opens `/clients/<id>/training?action=weights`. The Clients row's tag ("3 lifts ready to step up"), a client's Overview row and the chat's client panel say the same and open the same. The Training tab, arriving with `?action=weights`, brings Weights to apply into view with the focus on its heading, or, with nothing left, says "Nothing is left to apply for Maya now." with the focus on the program's title; the word leaves the address. "How these statuses work" gains "Lifts ready to step up".

What the coach sees: the weekly homework last in Needs you, one tap from the panel that applies it. What the client sees: nothing new.

**Rules.** The attention read stays a fixed number of commands (no per-client calls); a coach sees assigned clients only and the Owner every client (ADR 0026, CLI-013); a released client raises nothing (ADR 0027); nothing is applied from Today (decision 21). Coach attention v8 has its reference tests, as each version before it. `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md` CLI-018 (v8, the item, the read) and STR-020 (one line); ADR 0040 amended with "Coach Today"; ARCHITECTURE.md's query counts (Today's busiest case 32, the list 26, a client's page 24, 26 with a step lift coming) and section 13; the UI plan's C1 and C3.

### N7 · The Billing page's discount line

**Follows**

- Decision 11 (7 Nov 2026), decided: the founding offer is a free start, then two rates in a row, each for a set number of months, then the normal price (the free start is decision 12's note of the same day); the seat's included clients for life come on top of the discount and are N11's. The platform admin grants each stretch as a discount (`WorkspaceDiscount` takes more than 0 and up to 100%, to two decimals, for any dates; the free start is a 100% discount), so every figure is data the admin types, and none of the offer's figures is written in code, a test or the docs.
- Decision 12 (7 Nov 2026): the founding offer is for founding coaches only and opens with the free start before decision 11's two rates; coaches who are not founding get the free trial alone, at a length the owners chose (Left out). Its tick on "fixed dates in the letter" was a placeholder, so two details stay open for the letter and the admin's typing: whether a founder's free start follows the trial or replaces it, and, if the company opens late, whether the discount months start at the first paid bill or on the letter's dates. Nothing here waits on them, since the line shows the dates the admin typed either way.
- Decision 13 (5 Nov 2026): founding coaches pay nothing until the company's account exists; the first invoice waits for it, so the line is needed before that invoice. Decision 3 (7 Nov 2026) registers the company only once the founding coaches have said yes and the app shows real use.
- Decision 14 (7 Nov 2026): the other coaches on the list get the same early price if they join by a date, granted by hand the same way, so their Billing page reads the same.
- The plan's Point 8, founding coaches from your list: before the first founding invoice, the Billing page shows each discount's name and end date, in the shape "<name>: <percent>% off until <day>, then <percent>% off until <day>".
- What exists today: the owner's Billing page shows the month so far and each invoice with a "Discount (<percent>%)" line and its amount, nothing about the discount's name, its last day or what follows; the admin's space page lists the discounts with their dates, note and state (running, starts later, ended, revoked); a discount's note is typed by the admin as "What it is for" and the owner never sees it; discounts cannot overlap, and the largest one touching a month applies, so a 100% discount gives an invoice with nothing to pay (ADR 0028).

**Builds**

1. The owner's read, `GET /api/billing` (`WorkspaceBillingView`), gains `discounts`: the space's unrevoked discounts that are running or still to come, oldest first, each with its name, percent, first day and last day (the last day is `EndsOnExclusive` minus one, as the admin's page shows it). Ended and revoked ones are not sent; an issued invoice keeps its own discount line as today.
2. The Billing page shows one sentence in the hero under the status sentence: "<name>: <percent>% off until <last day>", and for each discount that starts the day after the last day of the one before (its `StartsOn` is the other's `EndsOnExclusive`), ", then <percent>% off until <last day>"; it ends with ", then the normal price." A 100% discount reads "free until <last day>" (Open), so decision 11's offer reads "<name>: free until <last day>, then <percent>% off until <last day>, then <percent>% off until <last day>, then the normal price." A discount still to come reads "<name>: <percent>% off from <first day> until <last day>"; two with a gap between them read as two sentences. No discount: nothing is added. The name is the discount's note; the admin's discount sheet says under the field "The owner sees this as the discount's name.", the way the void sheet says the owner sees the reason.
3. During the trial the sentence still shows, after the trial sentence, so the owner knows what follows the trial.

**Rules**

- Every number and date is the server's; the browser adds nothing up; dates are UTC days, which the page's note already says (BILL-6).
- A revoked discount never shows; a discount that ended shows only on the invoices it touched.
- No new money rule: what is billed does not change (PBL-003, the largest-wins rule of ADR 0028, the GiST non-overlap). A month two discounts touch takes the larger, so where a chain's day falls inside a month, that month's invoice can take off more than the sentence's next rate, never less; the sentence states the days as granted, and each invoice keeps its own discount line.
- The owner alone reads it (`/api/billing` stays on the owner policy); a coach still gets 403.

**Where**

- `src/backend/Modules/PlatformBilling/TB.Gym.Modules.PlatformBilling/PlatformBillingContracts.cs` (`WorkspaceBillingView` at 168-176, which carries no discounts today; `WorkspaceDiscountView` at 214-221), `PlatformInvoice.cs:235-333` (`WorkspaceDiscount`, `ValidatePercent`, `ApplicableTo`), `PlatformBillingEndpoints.cs:14-19` (`GET /api/billing` on `TenantOwner`).
- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/WorkspaceBillingService.cs:22-42` (`GetForOwnerAsync`; `StateAsync` already reads `state.Discounts` and the owner's view drops them), `PlatformBillingReader.cs:169-178` (`DiscountsAsync`) and `:388-395` (`ToView(WorkspaceDiscount)`).
- Web (under `src/web/src/app/`, line numbers with N3b's changes in): `features/billing/billing.models.ts` (`WorkspaceBilling` 89-102, `BillingDiscount` 125-133, `toWorkspaceBilling` 230, and `billLines` at 381 with its "Discount (<percent>%)" line at 412-417), `billing.html:24-46` (the hero) and `:143-176` (the month so far), `billing.ts:149-167` (`statusSentence`), `features/platform-admin/platform-space.html:281-332` and `platform-space.ts:181-187, 246-259` (how the admin's page states a discount), `platform-admin.models.ts:209-218` (`discountState`, `discountLastDay`), `platform-sheets.ts` (`PlatformDiscount` from line 559, the note field at 617-628 with `noteLabel` "What it is for" at 673; the void sheet's "Kept on the invoice. The owner sees it too." at 505).
- Tests to extend: `tests/backend/TB.Gym.Api.IntegrationTests/PlatformBillingTests.cs` (`PlatformAdminPublishesPlansVoidsAndReissuesRecordsExactPaymentsAndGrantsDiscounts` at 378), `tests/backend/TB.Gym.Domain.Tests/PlatformBillingDomainTests.cs` (`TheLargestUnrevokedDiscountTouchingTheMonthApplies`, a 100% discount then a lower one, as the model), `billing.models.spec.ts`, `billing.spec.ts`, `platform-sheets.spec.ts`, `src/web/e2e/settings.e2e.ts` (the `BILLING` mock at 135, served at 220; the `/billing` screens at 246 and 398), `src/web/e2e/live/unpaid-read-only.e2e.ts`.

**Tests**

- Integration (PostgreSQL): the owner reads a running and an upcoming discount with name and days, and a chain of three in a row starting with a 100% one, not a revoked or an ended one; a coach gets 403 on `/api/billing`; another space's owner reads none of them (a second workspace and the wrong `X-Tenant-Id`).
- Vitest: the sentence for none, one, two in a row, three in a row with a 100% start, two 100% stretches in a row, two with a gap, one still to come, and during the trial; the sheet's new help line.
- Mocked e2e: Billing at 1440 and 390, light and dark, axe clean, no sideways scroll, 200% text inside the page.
- Live: on a scratch demo (the owner's databases and ports never), grant a chain of three as the admin (a 100% start and two rates) and read the owner's page; stills at 1440 and 390 in light and dark. Server code changes, so the backend tests of section 1 run.
- Test figures are the chat's own, never the owners' offer.

**Left out**

- The offer's figures: the admin types them when granting (decisions 11 and 12); the page shows what was granted.
- Included clients for life (decision 11's other half, on top of the discount): N11.
- Dates the server works out, such as months counted from the first paid invoice: not built. Whichever way decision 12's open detail goes, the admin grants each stretch with its dates once they are known (after the first paid bill, if the months count from it), and the line shows what was granted.
- The trial's length for coaches who are not founding (decision 12's note): a field of the published price plan (`TrialDays`), so the admin publishes a new plan version; no code. A version that changes the trial alone moves no price; one that changes a price waits for N8, which the plan puts before any new price plan.
- The printable invoice (ADR 0028: needs TB Gym's legal name).

**Open** (the chat settles each, builds the default and adds the line to section 3)

- A 100% discount's words: "Free until <last day>" (default; "free" in lower case inside the sentence). Other way: "100% off until <last day>".
- Two free stretches in a row (decision 13's free months until the company's account exists, then the free start): one clause, "free until <the later last day>" (default). Other way: a clause each.
- A chain whose discounts carry different names: one sentence named after the first (default). Other way: a new sentence at each new name.
- Whether the name is the note or a new field the admin types: the note, with the sheet's help line changed (default). Other way: a "Name" field beside "What it is for".

### N11a · Included clients, the server

**Follows**

- Decision 11 (7 Nov 2026): a founding coach's seat includes more clients than the price plan's seat, for life, on top of the founding discount. The number is the owners'. The platform admin enters it as a published founding terms version, and it is never written in code, a migration, a test name or the docs. N7 left this out as a price rule for the owner; the decision now settles it.
- Decision 17 (7 Nov 2026): for each coach a founding coach brings, extra included clients for a limited period. This replaces the thank-you that the plan's Point 8 proposed. The admin enters the clients and the months with each grant, so neither is written in code or the docs. When a grant is earned (the brought coach signs up, or pays) and any cap are the admin's call, so nothing in code depends on them.
- The plan's Point 8, founding coaches from your list: the founding offer, and the thank-you for bringing a coach. "On top of the discount" means the included clients are counted first, and the discount still comes off the whole subtotal.
- Decision 12's reading and decision 4 (7 Nov 2026) need no code: the founding offer's discount months, the trial for coaches who are not founding, and the trainer partner's own space. They are discounts the admin grants and a price plan version the admin publishes. This row touches none of them.
- What exists today:
  - A seat includes the plan's `IncludedClientsPerSeat` (0 to 1,000), pooled across seats by `platform-invoice-v1`.
  - An invoice snapshots `PlanIncludedClientsPerSeat` and `IncludedClients`, and the check `CK_Invoices_Quantities` pins `IncludedClients = Seats × PlanIncludedClientsPerSeat`.
  - A `WorkspaceDiscount` is granted with UTC days, revoked once and kept. It never overlaps another (a GiST constraint), and the largest one touching a month applies.
  - N3a's `FoundingStatus` allows one open status per space, and the global, immutable `FoundingTerms` versions are in force latest first.
  - Their docs say founding status changes no bill. This row changes that in one place only: the included clients.
  - A terms publish refuses a number left out rather than read it as 0, since a version is immutable (`PublishFoundingTermsRequest`'s summary; `PublishTermsAsync`, line 305).
- Order: built after N3b is committed, since this row adds one field to N3b's terms request, and before N11b, which draws every screen.

**Builds**

Server only, as N3a was: no screen changes. N11b draws every screen on this API.

1. The founding seat's number. `FoundingTerms` gains `FoundingSeatIncludedClients`: the clients a founding space's owner seat includes. It is a whole number from 0 to 1,000 (the plan's range), or none.
   - The migration adds the column empty. Version 1 keeps none and no version is inserted, so no figure lives in the code, a migration or the docs.
   - Until the admin publishes a version with a number, a founding seat includes what the plan's seat includes.
   - `PublishFoundingTermsRequest` and `FoundingTermsView` gain the number. `PublishTermsAsync` checks it beside the two badge thresholds and, like them, refuses a request that leaves it out (400), never reading it as none. None is sent on purpose, the key present and null, and means the plan's seat. So an admin page still running N3b's code, a hand call or a later row that adds a terms field never drops every founding seat from the next invoice.
   - The badge's rule reads its thresholds as before. N8 adds its months to the same table later, the same way.
2. Extra included clients. A new tenant-owned row in the module, `IncludedClientGrant` (`billing."IncludedClientGrants"`), modelled on `WorkspaceDiscount`.
   - Its fields: `Clients` (1 to 1,000); `StartsOn` and `EndsOnExclusive` (UTC days, half-open, the end after the start, each the 1st of a month, so a grant is whole calendar months: Open 7); `Note` (required, 200 characters, saying what it is for, such as which coach was brought; only TB Gym's admin reads it); `GrantedAtUtc`, `GrantedByUserId`, `RevokedAtUtc` and `RevokedByUserId`.
   - It is granted only to a space with an open founding status (Open 6). A grant already made is not undone when the status later ends; the admin revokes it if it should go.
   - It is never edited or deleted. It is revoked once and the revocation is kept, guarded by a trigger `billing.protect_included_client_grant()` like `protect_workspace_discount` and by the context's guard like the discount's.
   - There is no overlap constraint, since grants that touch one month add up (Open).
   - The same clients, days and note for the same space, while unrevoked, answer the grant that already exists, so a double press grants once. A partial unique index keeps two presses at once safe.
3. The calculation, named and versioned: `platform-invoice-v2` in `PlatformInvoiceCalculation`. Included clients are the sum of three parts:
   - the seats times the plan's per-seat number;
   - for a billed window with a founding seat, whatever the founding number adds over the plan's per-seat number, for the owner's seat alone. The larger of the two applies, so a founding number at or below the plan's adds nothing;
   - the clients of every unrevoked grant that touches the window.

   Extra clients, the amounts, the discount (a percentage of the whole subtotal, rounded half away from zero to the cent) and the total follow as in v1. The calculation returns the included clients by source. Invoices already issued keep `platform-invoice-v1`.
4. Where the numbers come from.
   - A billed window has a founding seat when one of the space's founding statuses touches it, as a discount touches a month, and the founding terms in force have a number. A status runs from UTC midnight of its `FoundingSince` to its end, or on while it is open.
   - The latest terms version is in force when an invoice is issued, as the latest plan is.
   - A grant counts when it touches the window the same way.
   - `BuildInvoiceAsync` reads both over the billable window, as it reads the discount. `EstimateAsync` reads them over the rest of the month, as it reads the discount at line 308.
   - A reissue keeps the voided invoice's plan, as today, and counts the founding seat and the grants again now, as it counts the discount again (ADR 0028). So a late founding mark or a late grant can be put right by a void and reissue.
5. The snapshot.
   - `PlatformInvoice` gains `FoundingSeatIncludedClients` and `FoundingTermsVersion`, both empty when no founding seat applied.
   - It gains `FoundingStatusId`, the status that made the space founding, as `DiscountId` names its discount.
   - It gains `GrantedIncludedClients`, the grants' total (0 when none), and the ids of the grants it counted.
   - `CK_Invoices_Quantities` becomes the v2 rule: `IncludedClients` = `Seats` × `PlanIncludedClientsPerSeat` + GREATEST(0, COALESCE(`FoundingSeatIncludedClients`, 0) − `PlanIncludedClientsPerSeat`) + `GrantedIncludedClients`.
   - Further checks: the three founding columns are set together, the grants' total is never negative, and a v1 invoice holds no founding seat and no grant.
   - Every invoice already issued meets the new check unchanged. The new columns go in with constant defaults and rewrite no row, so `TR_Invoices_AppendOnly` fires on nothing and keeps freezing the whole row, new columns included.
6. The admin's API, in the existing `/api/platform-admin` group (the platform-admin policy, antiforgery, `SensitiveWrite`):
   - `POST /workspaces/{tenantId}/included-client-grants` with `{ clients, startsOn, endsOnExclusive, note }` → the grant; 404 for an unknown space, 400 by field (a day that is not the 1st of a month among them), 409 `space_not_founding` when the space has no open founding status. Name `GrantIncludedClients`.
   - `POST /included-client-grants/{grantId}/revoke` → the grant; 404, or 409 `grant_revoked`. Name `RevokeIncludedClientGrant`.
   - `GET /workspaces/{tenantId}` (`AdminWorkspaceDetail`) gains `includedClientGrants`, newest first with the revoked ones kept, as `discounts` are.
   - `AdminFoundingView` gains the founding seat's number in force for the open status. It is null when there is none, or when it does not raise the plan's seat.
7. The owner's read and every bill.
   - `GET /api/billing` (`WorkspaceBillingView`) gains `foundingSeatIncludedClients`: what the owner's seat includes now, while the status is open and the number raises the plan's seat, the trial included; null otherwise.
   - It also gains `includedClientGrants`: the unrevoked ones running or still to come, oldest first, each with its clients, first day and the day after its last. Never the note.
   - `PlatformInvoiceView` gains `foundingSeatIncludedClients`, `foundingTermsVersion` and `grantedIncludedClients`.
   - `BillingAmountsView`, which an invoice and the estimate share, gains the included clients by source: `seatIncludedClients`, `foundingIncludedClients` and `grantedIncludedClients`, adding up to `includedClients`. So the screens add nothing up.
   - `CurrentMonthEstimateView` gains `foundingTermsVersion`.
8. Regenerate the web API client (`npm run api:generate`).
   - The generated publish request makes every field a required key. So the owned founding terms model and its request gain the number: `features/billing/founding.models.ts`, `platform-founding-api.ts` and `toPublishTermsRequest` in `platform-founding.models.ts`, plus the `foundingTerms()` fixture.
   - N3b's terms sheet sends the version in force's number unchanged (null for none), so thresholds published before N11b keep it. The tests that pin that request's exact body gain the number: `platform-admin.spec.ts:437`, `platform-founding-sheets.spec.ts:280` and the mocked `e2e/platform-admin.e2e.ts:712`.
   - The new fields are required keys of the generated `BillingAmountsView`, `PlatformInvoiceView`, `CurrentMonthEstimateView`, `WorkspaceBillingView` and `AdminWorkspaceDetail` too, so the API literals `billing.models.spec.ts` builds with those types gain them, as N3a's fields were added there (0718b0c).
   - No screen shows anything new, and the web build stays green.
9. Docs, a few lines each:
   - PBL-003 says the included clients come from the plan, the founding terms and the grants, under `platform-invoice-v2`.
   - PBL-009's "It changes no access, no bill and no discount" names the one thing it now changes on the bill.
   - A new PBL-011 states the rule above (PBL-010 is N7's discounts).
   - ADR 0028 is amended: the Price bullet, the reissue line, and the mechanism (the grants table, its trigger and the invoice check).
   - ARCHITECTURE.md's module row (line 80) names v2 and the grants, and `FoundingStatus`'s summary says the same.

What the coach and the client see: nothing yet. N11b draws the admin's and the owner's screens. A client never sees any of this, since the bill is the owner's alone.

**Rules**

- Only the platform admin grants, revokes and publishes founding terms (PBL-006: the global role, re-read from the database on every request, no workspace header). The owner reads their own space's founding seat and grants on `GET /api/billing`. A coach gets 403 there, as today, and a client reads nothing of it.
- Tenant-owned rows carry `TenantId`, the global query filter and the write guard through `ConfigureTenantEntity`, a tenant-aware index and a foreign key to the space. The admin enters the space with `tenantContext.SetTenant`, as `GrantDiscountAsync` does. Every read names its space, as `PlatformBillingReader` does. A grant on one space never reaches another space's invoice or estimate.
- Exact money: every amount is a decimal in USD from the published plan, and included clients are whole numbers. The discount still comes off the whole subtotal, after the included clients are counted.
- Append-only history:
  - A grant is never edited or deleted. It is revoked once and kept.
  - Founding terms versions stay immutable.
  - An issued invoice never changes when terms are published, a grant is revoked or a founding status ends. It says which plan number, which founding number and version, which status and which grants it counted, so every bill can be explained.
- No figure in code: the founding number comes only from a published terms version, and a grant's clients and months only from the admin's request. None is seeded, defaulted or written in the docs. Test data and the live pass use made-up numbers.
- The server keeps a grant to a founding space and to whole calendar months, and refuses a terms publish that leaves the founding number out, so none of these lives only in N11b's screens (AGENTS.md).
- Nothing else changes: no access, no trial, no discount, no badge rule, and no money between a coach and their client. A space that is not founding and has no grant gets the bill that v1 would give it.
- The two grant routes are admin routes outside the tenant handler, so the read-only allow-list does not change.

**Tests**

- Domain (`tests/backend/TB.Gym.Domain.Tests/PlatformBillingDomainTests.cs`): v2 reference cases beside v1's at lines 24-92.
  - A space that is neither founding nor granted gets v1's amounts.

  - A grant adds its clients, and two that touch one month add up.
  - A revoked grant adds nothing, nor does one ending on the month's first day (the month before, half-open) or one starting after the month. A grant of two whole months counts in each of them.
  - The founding seat for a status founded mid-month, for one ended mid-month (that month keeps it), and for one ended before the month (none).
  - The discount still comes off the whole subtotal, rounded half away from zero to the cent, in USD.

  - The refusals: a grant of 0 or 1,001 clients, a first day or an end that is not the 1st of a month, an end not after its start, an empty or 201-character note, a second revoke, and a terms number of −1 or 1,001.

- The figures: the founding number and each grant's clients and months are the admin's to type.
- The founding offer's discount months, the trial for coaches who are not founding, and the trainer partner's own space (decisions 12 and 4): discounts the admin grants and a price plan version the admin publishes, with no code.
- Referral tracking: no record of who brought whom, no automatic grant when a brought coach signs up or pays, and no cap in code (decision 17: the admin's call).
- The discount line (N7), the price guarantee (N8) and the printable invoice (ADR 0028).

**Open** (the chat builds the first way, the conservative one where money is concerned, and adds a line to section 3; a money rule stays the owner's)

1. A founding space with several seats, such as a gym: the owner's seat alone takes the founding number, and every other seat the plan's. Decision 11 names a founding coach's seat, and N3a's founding coach is the owner (conservative). The other way: every seat of a founding space.
2. A founding number at or below the plan's: the larger of the two applies, so a founding seat never includes fewer clients than a plain one (conservative). The other way: the founding number always.
3. A founding status ended for any of the three causes, the coach's own request included: the founding seat ends with it, from the first month after the one it ended in (conservative). A coach who only wants the badge gone can hide it instead, and N11b's end sheet says so. The owners chose this on 7 Nov 2026, over keeping the seat for life once marked, as they chose for N8's guarantee.
4. Two grants touching one month: they add up, since decision 17 gives clients for each coach brought; an invoice then never bills a client the admin's grants included (conservative). The other way: the largest applies, as with discounts.
5. A later terms version that lowers the founding number: each founding space keeps the highest number it has had while its status stays open, since decision 11 gives the founding seat its clients for life; a higher number applies from the next invoice. The other way: the lower number applies from the next invoice to every founding space, as a new plan version does (PBL-003).
6. A grant on a space with no open founding status: refused (409), since decision 17's thank-you is a founding coach's and no answer gives extra included clients to any other space (conservative). The other way: allowed on any space, as a discount is, with the note saying why.
7. A grant's days: whole UTC calendar months, its first day and its end each the 1st of a month, other days refused (400). So a grant counts in exactly the invoices of the months it names, and the server keeps it so, not N11b's sheet (conservative). The other way: any days, a grant that does not sit on a month's edges touching two months and adding to both, as a discount does.
8. A reissue counts the founding seat again with the terms in force now. The other way: it keeps the voided invoice's founding terms version, as it keeps its plan.
9. The chat's own technical choices:
   - how a publish says none on purpose: the key sent as null, with the key required (the default), or a flag of its own; a publish that leaves the number out is refused either way (item 1);
   - how the invoice holds the grants' ids: a `uuid[]` column, or an append-only child row per grant;
   - whether `FoundingStatusId` carries a composite foreign key.

### N11b · Included clients on screen

**Follows**

- Decisions 11 and 17 (7 Nov 2026), as N11a built them: the clients a founding space's owner seat includes, a number the admin publishes in the founding terms; and extra included clients the admin grants a founding space for whole UTC calendar months.
- N11a built the server, and this row builds after it and changes no server code. N11a added:
  - `foundingSeatIncludedClients` on the founding terms and their publish request;
  - the grant and revoke routes, a grant only on a founding space and for whole calendar months;
  - `includedClientGrants` on the space's detail, and the founding seat's number on its founding view;
  - `foundingSeatIncludedClients` and `includedClientGrants` on the owner's `GET /api/billing`;
  - the included clients by source on every invoice and estimate;
  - the regenerated web API client.
- N3b's founding screens say "Marking changes no access and no invoice", and that ending changes only the badge. Once a founding number is published that is no longer true, so their words change here.
- N7 (the Billing page's discount line) puts one sentence in the hero once it is built. This row's sentences come after it.

**Builds**

Web only, in this order: the admin's page first, so a grant can be made before the owner's page is tried.

1. The admin's space page (`/admin/billing/:tenantId`).
   - A panel "Extra included clients" after Discounts, on a founding space or one that has grants. A space never founding and never granted shows no panel.
     - With none it says "No extra clients. A grant adds its clients to the invoice of each month it covers."
     - Otherwise it shows one row per grant: "<n> clients", "<first day> to <last day>", the note, and a pill like the discounts' (Running now, Starts later, Ended, Revoked).
     - A running or coming grant has **Revoke**, which asks "Revoke these <n> extra clients (<first day> to <last day>)? Invoices already issued keep them; invoices from now on will not have them." with **Revoke clients** and **Keep them**.
     - **Grant extra clients** opens the sheet. It shows while the space is founding, since the server refuses a grant otherwise.
   - The grant sheet, `PlatformClientGrant` in `platform-sheets.ts`, built like `PlatformDiscount`:
     - "Clients": a whole number from 1 to 1,000.
     - "First day": the 1st of a month, the 1st of next month by default.
     - "Last day": a month's last day, that month's by default, with the presets "One month", "2 months" and "3 months" (whole calendar months). A day off a month's edge is marked on its field, and the server refuses it too.
     - "What it is for", with the help "Only TB Gym’s admin sees it. Say which coach was brought."
     - The sentence "Adds its clients to the included clients of the invoice of each month it covers, on top of the seats’ and a founding seat’s. Two grants in one month add up."
     - The button **Grant clients**. A refusal stays on screen in the server's words.
   - The Founding panel (N3b's) gains a line while the status is open: "Its owner’s seat includes <n> clients while it stays founding." With no number published, it reads "Its owner’s seat includes what the price plan’s seat includes: no founding number is published yet."
   - The mark sheet's "Marking changes no access and no invoice." becomes "Marking changes no access. While the space stays founding, its owner’s seat includes <n> clients (the terms in force).", or the no-number line.
   - The end sheet's notice gains "From next month’s invoice, the owner’s seat includes what the price plan’s seat includes, and extra clients already granted stay until you revoke them. To take only the badge away, the coach can hide it."
   - The month so far and every invoice show where the included clients came from (item 4). The invoice row's usage line (`invoiceUsage`) adds "founding terms v<n>" when the invoice used them.
2. The list page (`/admin/billing`).
   - N3b's "Founding coach badge" panel becomes "Founding terms". It holds the badge's line and "A founding space’s owner seat includes <n> clients.", or "…what the price plan’s seat includes."
   - The terms sheet gains a third number, "Clients a founding owner’s seat includes", with the help "The price plan’s seat includes <m>; the larger applies." It is prefilled with the version in force (empty for none), and appears in What changes when it changes.
3. The owner's Billing page (`/billing`). In the hero, after the status sentence, or after N7's discount sentence once N7 is built:
   - "As a founding coaching space, your own seat includes <n> clients.", while the server sends the number;
   - one sentence per grant: "<n> more clients included until <last day>.", or for one still to come "<n> more clients included from <first day> until <last day>.";
   - nothing when there is neither.

   The sentences show during the trial too.
4. The bill lines (`billLines` in `billing.models.ts`), used by the owner's month so far, the owner's invoice sheet and the admin's space page.
   - When a founding seat or a grant counted, "Clients with a plan: <b>, <i> included with your seats" becomes "Clients with a plan: <b>, <i> included: <s> with your seats, <f> more as a founding space, <g> more granted".
   - Each part shows only when above zero, and the "<x> more × <price>" tail stays as today.
   - Every number is the server's, from the amounts by source.
5. The words: N3b's words test (`founding.words.spec.ts`) also reads every template this row adds or changes, and refuses the five words of section 1, "verified", "certified", "licensed", "qualified" and "approved", as its `NEVER` pattern already does.

What the owner sees: on Billing, what their seat includes as a founding space and any extra clients with their last day; on each invoice, where its included clients came from. Coaches and clients see nothing new.

**Rules**

- Presentation of the server's answer only: nothing is decided in Angular. Every number and day is the server's, and the browser adds nothing up. Dates are UTC days, as the page's note says (BILL-6).
- The admin's sheets work as the discount sheet does: a CSRF refresh before the write, the dirty flag and the ask before discarding, and a refusal in the server's words.
- The owner never sees a grant's note, a founding status's note or an end's reason.
- A space that is not founding and has no grant looks exactly as before, pixel-identical against the current screenshots. A founding space's page gains the new panel, empty until a grant.
