### N2 · The website link

**Follows.** Point 5 of the plan ("the homepage feels separate from the app"), its recommended option: the homepage at a second address that works while signed in, with a Back to Today button, and a "TB Gym" group in the coach's account menu with "TB Gym website" and "What changed", with the fixes the plan lists (the cancel on unsaved work, the sign-up-style links hidden when signed in, clients and former clients not sent there, a tag for search engines, 200% text and Arabic, the installed app). Decision 23 (7 Nov 2026): the owners picked the address `/about`, the plan's recommendation, over the same address with a tag on the end; owners and coaches see the link, for now; the words stay the plan's; the WhatsApp line waits for the number. Follows N1: no page counts unless it asks to, and `Home` counts on `/` alone, so `/about` never counts; the sign-up page now asks to, on the owners' word (7 Nov 2026).

**Builds.** No server rule and no API change: the homepage is public and the menu is presentation; the API authorises every request as it does today.

The address. A route `about` in `app.routes.ts` loads the same `Home` component (today matched at `''` for a visitor alone, by `publicHomeGuard`), open to anyone, waiting for the session's answer first (`sessionKnownGuard`, as the not-found page does) so it never draws the visitor's header and then swaps it; it keeps the homepage's title and is client-rendered (not added to `app.routes.server.ts`, which `app.routes.spec.ts` pins), so nginx's `location /` answers it with the shell with or without the sign-in cookie and nothing in the cookie maps changes.

How it sits when signed in. The page fills the window as a visitor sees it, with its own header and no coach sidebar, top bar or client tabs around it: a route data flag beside `FULL_SCREEN` in `core/theme/redesigned-route.ts`, read by `App` as `wantsFullScreen` is, so `app.html` draws none of its three shells on that route and brings them back on the next (the other way, inside the coach frame as `/changelog` is drawn, goes in section 3).

The way back. Where the header shows "Sign in" (the wide header's `links__sign` link and the phone menu's row) a signed-in person sees "Back to Today", a link to `/`, which is Today for anyone signed in; the finale's footer "Sign in" reads "Back to Today" too. "Start your coaching space" (the header's phone menu, the hero, the who-it's-for half, the finale) and the six rows' links to `/auth/register` ("Build your first program", "Log a first workout", "Set a client's targets", "Write your check-in", "Open your inbox", "Follow a client's trend") are hidden when signed in, so one tap during a pitch never opens the real client list or the sign-up form; the quiet "Talk to us about your gym" links, the form and "What changed" stay. Every in-page link must stay on `/about` when the page is there: today the header's section links (the wide nav's three, `home-header.html` lines 9–27, and the phone menu's three, lines 60–62), the wordmark (line 3), the hero's, the gym half's and the finale's `fragment="talk"` links (`home-hero.html` line 28, `home-who.html` line 48, `home-finale.html` line 12) are all `routerLink="/"`, which signed in would leave for Today; the chat makes them relative to the page's own address (`routerLink="."` with the fragment, or an address `Home` passes down) and checks the pre-rendered homepage's hrefs stay `/#talk` and the rest (`home.e2e.ts` line 43 counts them).

The menu. In the coach shell's account menu (`coach-shell.html`, the deferred `#account-menu` panel), after Dark mode and before the platform admin's rows and Sign out, a group headed "TB Gym" (the panel's `menu-heading` style, as the workspace panel's "Coaching spaces") with two rows: "TB Gym website", to `/about`, and "What changed", to `/changelog` (which, signed in, already draws inside the coach frame with no website header, `changelog--embedded`). The menu closes on the navigation as every menu does (`NavigationEnd` closes everything). Owners and coaches see it because the coach shell is theirs alone (`App.shell()` is `coach` when `tenants.canCoach()`); the client's Me page and the former client's member bar gain nothing, so clients and former clients are not sent there.

Unsaved work. A link to `/about` runs the page's leave guard as any navigation does (`confirmBeforeLeaving` and `confirmSheetLeave` on the routes that hold work); Cancel leaves the person where they were with their typed work, the menu closed; Leave goes. The chat proves it and adds no new guard.

Search engines. `/about` says noindex two ways. In the browser the page adds `<meta name="robots" content="noindex">` while on `/about` and removes it on leaving (the `NotFound` pattern, which keeps a tag the document already has), never on `/`: the public pre-rendered HTML must carry no robots tag (`check-prerender.mjs`, `home-prerender.e2e.ts` line 52). nginx sends `X-Robots-Tag: noindex` on `/about` through a map at server level, like the cache-control map (an `add_header` inside a location would drop the server's security headers); the map keys on `$request_uri`, since after `try_files` `$uri` is the shell's file name, which the cache map relies on. `robots.txt` stays as it is: a Disallow would stop a crawler from reading the noindex. The test copy's Dockerfile copies `src/web/nginx.conf`, edits it with sed and checks it with grep (lines 69–87); it already adds its own server-level `X-Robots-Tag "noindex, nofollow"` after `X-Content-Type-Options`, so on the test copy `/about` would carry two such headers, both noindex, which is harmless; the change keeps those checks green. `scripts/serve-built.mjs` mirrors the header if cheap, so the built tests can read it.

The sign-up page's count. The owners said yes on 7 Nov 2026 (section 3, N1's line): the sign-up page (`/auth/register`, `features/auth/register-coach.ts`) counts itself once each time it opens for a visitor who is not signed in, as What changed does: `/auth/register` joins `COUNTED_PAGES` in `core/website/website-analytics.ts` and the page calls `WebsiteAnalytics.pageview` the way `features/changelog/changelog.ts` does, so `countedAddress` sends its path and only the tags N1 already keeps, never the rest of its address. Nothing else in N1's rules changes.

Words on screen: "TB Gym" (the group), "TB Gym website", "What changed", "Back to Today", the plan's words, which decision 23 kept. Arabic: the app has no Arabic translation (`angular.json`'s i18n has the source locale alone), so "in Arabic" is the right-to-left check the repo does (`document.documentElement.dir = 'rtl'`, `home.e2e.ts` line 467, `coach-shell.e2e.ts`'s `rtl` option), at 390 and 1440. 200% text: `font-size: 32px` on the root, as `home.e2e.ts` line 459 does. The installed app: the manifest's scope is `/` and its start is sign-in, so `/about` opens inside the installed window, where there is no browser Back: "Back to Today" is the only way back; the chat proves the button at 390 and the owner tries the installed app (the plan's own step for Point 5).

**Rules.** Presentation only: no role check moves into the browser, nothing is granted or withheld (AGENTS.md); `/about` reads no tenant data; the website's settings read (`GET /api/website/settings`, anonymous) happens as on `/`, for the WhatsApp link, and may load the analytics script where the deployment names it, but nothing is counted on `/about` (N1: `Home` counts on `/` alone and the script counts nothing on its own). The Talk-to-us form keeps its stamp, antiforgery token and allowances. No cookie, no storage, no migration, nothing on the server. `docs/UI-REDESIGN-PLAN.md` gains a sentence where the account menu is described (around line 1426) and in section 3; `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`'s R6.2g paragraph a line on `/about` and the nginx paragraph a line on the header.

Tests to extend: `shell/coach-shell.spec.ts` (the account menu tests, lines 175–247), `app.spec.ts` ("takes the tabs and the page margin away on a full-screen route"), `features/home/home-header.spec.ts`, `home.spec.ts`, `home-hero.spec.ts`, `home-who.spec.ts` (no `home-finale.spec.ts` exists today), `e2e/coach-shell.e2e.ts` (`openShell`, the account menu test at line 265), `e2e/home.e2e.ts`, `e2e/edge/edge-headers.e2e.ts` (a real nginx through `scripts/edge-proof.ps1`), `e2e/built/home-prerender.e2e.ts`.

**Tests.** No domain or integration test: no server code changes. ### N8 · The founding price guarantee

**Follows**

- Decision 16 (7 Nov 2026), decided: once their discount ends, founding coaches keep today's prices for a set number of months, the plan's number; the platform admin enters it as a founding terms value, never a number in code, a migration seed or the docs. The build must exist before any new price plan is published, since the placeholder prices are expected to change.
- Decision 16's money details, answered by the owners on 7 Nov 2026: (a) a founding space with no discount at all is held too, N months from its founding day; (b) while the guarantee runs, a founding space pays whichever is lower, its held version or the latest. And, on 7 Nov 2026 too: (c) an ended founding status ends the guarantee from the first month after the one it ended in; (d) the month the guarantee's last day falls inside is billed at the held version, for the whole month.
- Decision 9 (5 Nov 2026): the partners fix the real prices between them before the demo calls. The plan's Point 8 adds that the letter freezes those prices for the founding year, so a rise never touches a founding coach while the discount runs: that part of the hold is in this rule too, not only the months after.
- Decisions 11 and 12 (7 Nov 2026): a founding space's discounts are a free start and two rates in a row, each one the admin grants (N7 shows them), so "the discount ends" is the last day of the last of them.
- The plan's Point 8, founding coaches from your list: keep founding coaches on today's prices until N months after their discount ends, so the first full-price month does not jump.
- What exists today: every invoice and estimate uses the latest published price plan version (`plans.MaxBy(VersionNumber)` in `PlatformInvoiceRun.IssueOneAsync` and in `PlatformBillingReader.EstimateAsync`); an invoice snapshots its version and terms and is never changed; a reissue keeps its voided invoice's version; `TrialPolicy.PlanAt` already finds the version in force at an instant, for the trial length. ADR 0028 and PBL-003 say new invoices use the latest version. N3a's founding status says which spaces are founding and since when, and its `FoundingTerms` (`billing.FoundingTerms`: immutable versions, the latest in force, version 1 seeded with the badge's two numbers) hold the badge's thresholds; `PublishFoundingTermsRequest` refuses a missing number rather than read it as 0, and N3b's terms sheet publishes the next version against the version the admin saw.

**Builds** (once the owner opens the row)

1. The rule, named and versioned: `FoundingPricePolicy` v1 in the platform billing module. A founding space (an open founding status) is priced by the plan version in force on its founding day (`TrialPolicy.PlanAt` at `FoundingSince`) until its guarantee ends, unless the latest version bills less: while the guarantee runs, the invoice and the estimate are worked out at both versions and the lower total is billed, the version used written on the invoice as today, equal totals keeping the held one (answer (b); every plan is in USD, so no exchange rate comes in). The guarantee ends N months after the later of the founding day and the last day of the space's last unrevoked discount, a UTC day, with the badge's calendar-month arithmetic (31 January plus one month is the last day of February), so a space with no discount, or one whose discount ended before its founding day, is held N months from its founding day (answer (a)); after it the latest version applies, as for every other space; the month that day falls inside is still held, for the whole month (answer (d)), the way a discount covers every month it touches. An ended founding status ends the guarantee too: the month it ended in is still held, and from the first month after it the latest version applies (answer (c)), the month N11a's founding seat ends as well. N is a term the admin publishes beside the badge thresholds: `FoundingTerms` gains `PriceGuaranteeMonths`, a nullable column with a range check like the badge's two, so the versions already published, version 1 among them, stay as they are. Its value is decision 16's: the admin types it in a new terms version once the row is built and before any new price plan; the chat builds the rule reading N from the terms in force and never writes the number. While the terms in force carry no N, nothing is held and the latest version applies, as today.
2. Where the version is chosen: the invoice run and the estimate ask the policy for the version instead of taking the latest; a reissue keeps its voided invoice's version as today; the trial length still comes from the plan at the space's creation.
3. Screens: the owner's Billing page says, after N7's discount sentence (or alone when N7 is not built), "Your prices are held until <day>." while the guarantee runs (the owner's page names no plan version today, and does not start to); the admin's space page says under "Where it stands" "Prices held at plan version <n> until <day>.", and its month-so-far note already names the version in use; the price plan sheet's sentence gains "Founding spaces keep their version until their guarantee ends."; the founding terms panel and sheet (N3b's) gain the guarantee's months as a third number, so the admin enters N there; N3b's mark sheet (its notice, `platform-founding-sheets.ts:92-101`, "The discount is separate: grant it on this page too.") gains that a founding space keeps its plan version until its guarantee ends, and its end sheet (`platform-founding-end.html:35-38`, "The discount is not changed: revoke it separately if it should end.") gains that the held prices end with the status, from the next month (answer (c)).

**Rules**

- The guarantee holds a plan version, never a price typed by hand; every number still comes from a published version (PBL-003 amended to say which one).
- Invoices stay immutable snapshots; the version chosen is written on the invoice as today, so every bill is explainable.
- No FX, credit, refund or recurring billing; nothing changes for a space that is not founding; nothing changes between a coach and their client.
- A revoked discount is out of the count: the later of the founding day and the last unrevoked discount's last day starts the N months. The plan's way to end a founding price early is the admin's two steps, revoke and grant again to the month's end, and the guarantee then counts from that end. An ended founding status ends the guarantee from the first month after the one it ended in (answer (c)); a coach who only wants the badge gone can hide it instead (PBL-009), and that changes nothing here.

**Where**

- `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/PlatformInvoiceRun.cs:109-139` (`IssueOneAsync`, the plan taken at 135), `PlatformBillingReader.cs:281-318` (`EstimateAsync`, the latest at 291) and `:183-209` (`BuildInvoiceAsync`), `PlatformBillingAdminService.cs:249-266` (the reissue keeps its plan), `src/backend/Modules/PlatformBilling/TB.Gym.Modules.PlatformBilling/PlatformBillingRules.cs:61-93` (`TrialPolicy`, `PlanAt` at 70, the lookup to reuse), `PlatformInvoice.cs:79` (`Issue` snapshots the version and terms), `PlatformBillingContracts.cs` (`CurrentMonthEstimateView.PricePlanVersion`, `WorkspaceBillingView`, `AdminWorkspaceDetail`, `PricePlanView.IsCurrent`, `FoundingTermsView`, `PublishFoundingTermsRequest`), `PlatformBillingAdminService.cs:78-121` (`PublishPricePlanAsync`).
- N3a's founding terms: `FoundingStatus.cs` (`FoundingBadgeTerms` and its `Validate`, `FoundingTerms.Publish`, `FoundingBadgePolicy.WholeMonthsSince`), `src/backend/TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/FoundingStatusServices.cs` (`FoundingReader.CurrentTermsAsync` and `StatusesAsync`, `FoundingAdminService.PublishTermsAsync`), `PlatformBillingEndpoints.cs:259-280` (`GET` and `POST /api/platform-admin/founding-terms`), `Persistence/GymDbContext.PlatformBilling.cs` (`FoundingTermsVersions`, the `billing.FoundingTerms` table) and `Persistence/Migrations/20261105223545_FoundingStatus.cs` (its checks, its append-only trigger and version 1's INSERT); a new migration.
- Web (under `src/web/src/app/`, with N3b's changes in): `features/billing/billing.html:143-176` ("An estimate." at 171-174), `features/platform-admin/platform-space.html:246-279` ("An estimate at plan version" at 275-276) and `:49-79` ("Where it stands"), `platform-sheets.ts:757-765` (the plan sheet's sentence "Invoices issued from now on use the new terms; invoices already issued keep theirs."), `platform-admin.html:178-243` (the Price plan panel) and `:246-280` (the Founding coach badge terms panel), `platform-founding-sheets.ts` (`PlatformFoundingTerms`; the mark sheet's notice at 92-101) with `platform-founding-terms.html`, `platform-founding-end.html` (the end sheet's notice at 35-38) and `platform-founding.models.ts` (`termsFields()` at 77), `features/billing/billing.models.ts`, `features/billing/founding.models.ts`.
- Docs: PBL-003 in `docs/DOMAIN-RULES.md:501-505` and PBL-009 at 532 (whose "It changes no access, no bill and no discount" now names the one thing founding status does change: the plan version a founding space is billed at), and ADR 0028 ("new invoices use the latest one", line 28) amended in a few lines.
- Tests to extend: `tests/backend/TB.Gym.Domain.Tests/PlatformBillingDomainTests.cs` (`AnInvoiceSnapshotsItsPlanAndFallsDueAfterThePlansPaymentDays` at 295, `APlanPublishedLaterNeverShortensATrialAlreadyGiven` at 123 as patterns), `tests/backend/TB.Gym.Api.IntegrationTests/PlatformBillingTests.cs` (`PlatformBillingIssuesOneInvoicePerWorkspaceAndMonthFromSeatsAndCoveredClients` at 25, `PlatformAdminPublishesPlansVoidsAndReissuesRecordsExactPaymentsAndGrantsDiscounts` at 378), `billing.models.spec.ts`, `platform-admin.models.spec.ts`, `platform-founding-sheets.spec.ts`, `src/testing/platform-admin-fixtures.ts` (`foundingTerms()`), `src/web/e2e/settings.e2e.ts`, `src/web/e2e/platform-admin.e2e.ts`.

**Tests**

- Domain: the founding-day version while the guarantee runs and the latest after; the end day from the last discount's last day plus N, at month ends; terms with no N hold nothing; a space with no discount, and one whose discount ended before its founding day, held N months from its founding day (a); a cheaper latest version billed while the guarantee runs, equal totals keeping the held one (b); an ended status: the month it ended in held, the next at the latest (c); the month the guarantee's last day falls inside held for the whole month (d); a space that is not founding unchanged.
- Integration (PostgreSQL): mark a space founding with a discount, publish terms with N, publish price plan version 2, issue a past month by hand (`POST /api/platform-admin/invoices/issue` with `periodStart`, the test clock moved as the billing tests do): the founding space's invoice and estimate carry version 1 and a plain space's version 2; after the guarantee's end day, version 2; a version cheaper than version 1 published while the guarantee runs bills the founding space at that version (b); an ended founding status: the month it ended in at the held version and the next at the latest (c); a reissue keeps its version; a terms publish against a stale version answers 409 as N3a's does; the owner reads the held-until day, a coach gets 403, another space's owner reads nothing of it; the admin routes stay admin-only.
- Vitest: the sentences on both pages, the plan sheet, the mark and end sheets, and the terms sheet's third number.
- Mocked e2e: Billing and the admin's space page at 1440 and 390, light and dark, axe clean.
- Live: on a scratch stack of the chat's own; the demo space is created 77 days before the build day (`DemoWorkspaceCast.WorkspaceCreatedDay = -77`), so its trial is over and at least one ended month can be issued by hand without touching dates: mark founding, grant a discount, publish terms with N, publish a new version, issue a past month, read both pages; never the owner's databases or ports. Server code changes, so the backend tests of section 1 run.
- N in the tests is the chat's own number, never decision 16's.

**Left out**

- N's value in code, a migration seed or the docs: the admin publishes it as a terms version (decision 16, 7 Nov 2026).
- Any change to what a seat includes (N11's included clients), to the trial, or to coach-to-client money.
- The printable invoice (ADR 0028: needs TB Gym's legal name).

**Open** (what the owners answered, and what the chat settles)

- Decision 16 is decided: the admin publishes N as a terms value; the rule is the same whatever it is.
- Answered by the owners on 7 Nov 2026 and built above: (a) a founding space with no discount at all is held N months from its founding day; (b) while the guarantee runs, a founding space pays the lower of its held version and the latest.
- Answered on 7 Nov 2026 too and built above: (c) an ended founding status, for any of N3b's three end causes, the coach's own request included, ends the guarantee from the first month after the one it ended in; (d) the month the guarantee's last day falls inside is billed at the held version, for the whole month.
- Which day fixes the version: the founding day (`FoundingSince`). Other way: the space's creation day.
- Whether the held-until day shows while the discount still runs: yes, so the owner sees the whole path. Other way: only once the discount has ended.

### N9 · Trainees without a coach

Point 1 of the plan of 3 Nov 2026, "trainees who sign up without a coach". This row is an outline: when the test has run and the decisions below are settled, the owner opens its parts (N9a to N9e, in that order) as rows of their own, each sized for one chat. No part is Open now, and no code is written for the test itself. On 11 November 2026 the owner changed Free (an exercise pool, no programs) and confirmed what Max is, and a critique the owner had asked for was checked against this row, the code and Hevy's own pages; the lines that changed say "11 Nov 2026".

**The tiers in plain words** (the owner's reading, 11 Nov 2026)

On Free and Pro a trainee is their own coach. On Pro+ and Max they follow the trainer partner.

| | Free | Pro | Pro+ | Max |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| In one line | Train on your own from TB Gym's exercise pool | Still on your own; the app works out your weights | Follow the trainer partner's programs | TB Gym coaches you |
| Pays | Nothing, with no end date | TB Gym | TB Gym, monthly | TB Gym |
| Gets | The exercise pool with its demo clips; picks exercises and logs workouts of their own, typing their own weights; their history and records | Free, plus the app's maths: their own max, weights as a % of it, the effort chart, "+X kg next time" | The trainer partner's programs, one at a time, with weights worked out from their own maxes | A program the trainer partner picks for them, a meal plan, check-ins and Messages: the four things a coach's client gets |
| Who does the work | Nobody for each person: the pool is filled once | Only the app | The trainer partner writes each program once, for everyone | The trainer partner, for each trainee, every month |
| Does anyone watch their training | No | No | No (a monthly check is not placed yet: here or on Max, 29's note) | Yes |
| Opens | First | Later, with own routines (28, 32) | Once the company's Whish account exists (3, 13) | The same, and after the owner privacy split (N9d) |

- Free runs underneath for good, and a trainee holds one paid plan at a time on top of it: a paid plan that ends, or one whose money has not come yet, leaves them on Free and never locked out.
- Pro+ sells the program. Max sells the program and the trainer partner's time, which is why Pro+ grows with no extra work and Max has a limited number of places.
- Max is what a coach on TB Gym sells, sold by TB Gym: the house is an ordinary coaching space whose Coach is the trainer partner, and Max switches on the four features a coach switches on for a client. It is lighter than one-to-one coaching: the program is one of the trainer partner's, picked for the person, not written for them alone, and one-to-one sessions stay the trainer partner's own to sell (18). The owner confirmed on 11 Nov 2026 that this is what was wanted.
- The health questions are asked only on Max (33).

**Follows** (the decisions of 5 and 7 Nov 2026, and the owner's word of 11 Nov 2026)

- Decision 24: TB Gym takes trainees straight after the first major test, as soon as both partners feel it can go up, and not after go-live, the company or a long test; what counts as that test is still to fix (its note), and the company and the consumer terms exist before any trainee pays (its note). The trainer partner fills the house from their own gym and former clients.
- Decision 3 (7 Nov 2026): the company is registered later, once the founding coaches have said yes and the app shows real use; until then no trainee money is taken, as decision 13 already fixes for the founding coaches (they pay nothing until the company's Whish account exists).
- Decision 2 (7 Nov 2026): the platform goes live without the health consent published, accepting that risk. As built, a member who joined before a version was published is not asked for it later (CLI-010), so a tester invited before a health consent exists is not asked for it. Decision 2 does not settle the consumer terms, which 24's note keeps before any trainee pays.
- Decision 25: the house, a TB Gym coaching space inside the app, titled "TB Gym coaching space"; a person who signs up becomes its client and is handled as their own coach: self-directed, not coached. What that means on the screens (the coach card on Today, check-ins, attention) is noted on the card, not settled.
- Decision 26: Free never ends. Its limits: no calculations (that is Pro, 32), later only a few own routines (28), a cap on kept workouts and plans (27); a non-paying trainee gets the house's standard programs (30). Changed by the owner on 11 Nov 2026: Free gets no programs. It is an exercise pool the trainee picks from to log workouts of their own, maybe with the 3D motion library (C20's); the house's programs start at Pro+.
- Decision 27: the cap works like Hevy's: what is over it is kept but locked until the trainee pays, never erased. Count or window, and the numbers, are not set. Checked on 11 Nov 2026 against Hevy's own pages: they put no limit on logged workouts (a user scrolls back with "no limit", and "the free version provides access to all the above information, though you can only load graphs from the last three months"); what the free plan limits is saved routines, four at a time, and how far back its graphs load. Hevy's page on leaving Pro could not be read that day. So "like Hevy's" is read as Hevy is: every logged workout stays readable, and the limit falls on how far back Progress reaches and, once own routines exist (28), on how many a trainee keeps. Locking older workouts, this row's first reading, is the other way (Open).
- Decision 28: own routines only after the test, and only if testers ask; until then the house's coach types routines into the builder. 11 Nov 2026: a workout a Free trainee puts together from the pool is not a routine. It is logged once and kept in their history; a routine is a workout saved to be started again, and that still waits for this decision.
- Decision 29: a monthly Pro+ with the trainer partner's programs one at a time, access for the paid period only. Whether a monthly check of the trainee belongs to Pro+ or to Max is noted, not settled. 11 Nov 2026: with no programs on Free, Pro+ is the first tier with a program.
- Decision 30: Max is a plan matched to the person, by hand at first, the money to TB Gym. The later paid consultation, more coaches, call-takers and automation are not in this row. The rule for which standard program fits whom comes from the trainer partner. 11 Nov 2026: the owner confirmed Max as TB Gym coaching the trainee (the tiers above). With no standard programs on Free, the trainer partner's rule now says whom each Pro+ program suits and guides the pick for a Max trainee.
- Decision 31 (5 and 7 Nov 2026): Max ships a meal plan of recipes with their calories counted; no dietitian, and nothing sold as health advice. The health and legal questions are worked out later; the lawyer's question, whether a calorie-counted meal plan needs a licence, stays on the lawyer's list.
- Decision 32: Pro goes on sale once trainees can build their own routines; the order of 28 and 32 is to settle.
- Decision 33: the house runs under a TB Gym company account, the tech partner as Owner and the trainer partner as Coach; both see the trainees. The owner privacy split (C8 in docs/CONCERNS-BRIEF.md, decided 14 Oct 2026) comes before Max opens, and the Health step is hidden for Free and Pro+ trainees.
- Decision 34: the house refuses nobody who is another coach's current client, and such a client may buy the house's plans while staying with their coach. The plan's sign-up refusal and its "keeps what they paid for, no renewal" rule fall with it.
- Decision 35 (7 Nov 2026): the plan stands. Until a Find a coach list exists, the tech partner passes a trainee who asks TB Gym for a coach to the next checked coach who agreed to be recommended, in turn, with a log and no commission; no build now.
- Decision 4 (7 Nov 2026): when a house trainee asks TB Gym for a coach without naming one, the trainer partner is not in that turn. Its two other answers, on the trainer partner's own space and certificates, touch no part of this row.
- Decision 18 (7 Nov 2026): the founding letter carries none of the plan's fair-play promises; the trainer partner will sell one-to-one sessions.
- Decision 36: health details only in the app, never on WhatsApp; no AI reads them until a lawyer says otherwise.
- It replaces C20 (docs/CONCERNS-BRIEF.md, 26 Oct 2026): one TB Gym space, starter and paid programs, training only, 3D demos. The house keeps C20's one-space design and its bought 3D demo library (a one-time licence that allows a paid app and keeping the files, tested on the free sample first; the trainer partner picks the clips) and adds what the plan's reviewers added: consumer terms, an age check, account closing, a weekly sign-up cap and plans that follow each other.
- The owner's word of 11 Nov 2026, in chat after reading the critique below: the tiers read as in the table above; Free is the exercise pool, with no programs; the 3D motion library is a maybe; Max as TB Gym's own coaching is what was wanted.
- A critique of 11 Nov 2026, asked for by the owner and checked against this row, the code and Hevy's pages before anything changed here. Kept: asking for a paid plan ended Free while the money was still to come, and a plan's days run from its first day however late it is paid (SUB-018), so a trainee who asked lost their training and paid days at once (N9c books a plan when its money is recorded); a program had to fit inside a monthly pass, so one of eight weeks could never start (N9c's blocks); the cap's reading of Hevy (27 above); Max is the trainer partner's hours and needs a limit on its places (N9c); the test had no pass mark (Step 2). Not kept: that four tiers open at once (they never did: the test takes no money, Pro waits for Step 5, Pro+ and Max wait for the company), and American apps' prices as the yardstick (the house's prices are the owners' to set, and what people at the trainer partner's gym pay today is the nearer comparison).

**Builds.** Nothing until the test has run. The order is the plan's "Steps":

*Step 1, now, no code.* The founding calls ask each coach how they feel about TB Gym serving trainees before anything is posted (the plan's Step 1; it holds nothing up, 24), and a waitlist is collected on the TB Gym WhatsApp number.

*Step 2, the test, with none of the first version's code.* Decision 24 puts it straight after the first major test, as soon as both partners feel it can go up, and ties it neither to go-live nor to the company; what counts as the test is still to fix (24's note). No tester pays before the company's Whish account and the consumer terms exist (24's note, C17), and decision 3 registers the company only once the founding coaches have said yes and the app shows real use, so until then the test takes no money. Everything it needs exists today: the house is an ordinary coaching space registered as a coach's (`POST /api/auth/register/coach`), named "TB Gym coaching space", with a company email; the platform admin gives it a full discount with a far-off end date (`POST /api/platform-admin/workspaces/{tenantId}/discounts`, a `WorkspaceDiscount` has a percent, a start and an exclusive end, so it must end); the trainer partner joins as Coach by a team invitation (ADR 0026); testers come from the trainer partner's own gym and former clients (24), reach the partners through a WhatsApp link or a QR code, are asked their age, are sent the consumer terms by email once the lawyer's text exists, and each one is then invited by email as a client (ADR 0002), so they get the workout player, Progress, notices, My plan and Whish receipts on day one; the house's Coach builds the programs as templates, assigns them by hand and sets each tester's training days so every session opens on a day they train; a wish for a routine of one's own is typed into the builder (28); a request to close an account is handled by hand, as docs/LAUNCH-CHECKLIST.md already allows for the founding cohort. The plan asks that no stranger is invited before the age question, the terms and the closing routine are ready; whether the free part of the test may start on the platform's published documents alone, before the consumer terms exist, is the owner's and the lawyer's (decision 2 settles only that the platform goes live without the health consent). The test counts how many testers pay, pay again, ask for a coach, and would otherwise have hired one (while no tester can pay, see Open); what it must show before the first version is built is the partners' call (24), the plan's counts a guide; the plan says that if coach requests outnumber payments, Find a coach (35) comes before the rest. Since 11 Nov 2026 two things are said plainly. What the test can show: on today's app a tester trains only on a program the house's Coach assigned, so the test tries, at no charge, what the paid tiers sell (the trainer partner's programs, and by hand Max's picked program, check-ins and Messages), not Free's pool, which needs N9b's code; whether N9b is built before strangers are invited, so that a tester starts where a stranger will, is the owner's (Open). And its pass mark: before the first invitation the partners write down, outside this file, who is invited and how many (people beyond friends and former clients among them), how long the test runs, and the counts that mean it passed (testers who start, who still train in its last week, and who ask for a paid plan once shown its price). A test with no pass mark cannot fail, and a test that takes no money cannot show that people pay; an ask at a named price is the nearest count until the company exists.

*Step 3, the first version, only if the partners judge the test passed.* The parts a later chat splits this row into, each a row of its own with the full Follows / Builds / Rules / Where / Tests / Left out / Open:

- **N9a · Sign-up without an invitation, into the house.** Follows 24, 25, 33, 34, 36 and the consumer terms (24's note; decision 2 does not settle them). Server first: one workspace is marked as the house (default: a column on the tenant row set by a `platform-admin` command beside `grant`, since that command is already the only way a global role is granted; the other way: a configuration key naming the tenant, which a wrong deployment setting could point at a coach's space). A public `POST /api/auth/register/trainee` beside `register/coach`: anonymous, antiforgery, `RateLimitPolicies.PublicAuthentication` plus a limit of its own, the Talk-to-us guards (a honeypot a person never sees and a server-signed stamp refused inside three seconds, WEB-002) and an age check before any account is stored (adults only, as section 11 of DOMAIN-RULES already says; the age line and its wording are the lawyer's, so it is a setting the owner fills). Sign-up opens in groups: a cap on new house sign-ups per calendar week in the workspace's time, a setting the owner fills, since payments and plans are recorded by hand (the plan's reviewers' fix); over it, `/train` reads "Sign-up is full for this week. It opens again on Monday." and stores no waitlist (the other way: no cap, and the partners watch the count). It creates the global account unconfirmed (email confirmation as for a coach, ADR 0002), a Client membership and a `ClientProfile` in the house, assigned to the house's Coach (ADR 0026 needs exactly one, until N9d makes it at most one), marked self-directed (a new flag on the profile), and records the acceptances of every document in force for a house trainee in the same transaction (CLI-010), the consumer terms included: a new `LegalDocumentKind` asked only at a house sign-up, published by the platform admin from the lawyer's text and never seeded. Sign-up asks nothing about other coaching spaces (34), so an email that already has an account signs in first and gets only the new membership and profile, as an invitation does today; a current client of another coach joins the house the same way, and neither space learns of the other (CLI-001). A Worker sweep removes house sign-ups never confirmed after a set number of days (no such sweep exists today; it touches house sign-ups alone). Self-directed trainees raise nothing on Coach Today (`CoachAttentionPolicy`, CLI-018, counts them out) and their workout notes and leavings reach no staff inbox, while money asks do (an ask for a plan and I've paid, N9c, since the house's staff record payments by hand); both partners keep them on the Clients list (33). Screens: a public `/train` page in the signed-out frame and TB Gym's own colours (`HOUSE_BRAND`), with the house's offer in a few lines, "Train with TB Gym" and one button, "Create your account", and the line that training days follow the house's clock (Beirut; a trainee abroad is told); the sign-up form on the model of `features/auth/register-coach.ts` (name, email, password, date of birth, the documents' boxes with the lawyer's sentence beside each); the confirmation screen; the homepage's client strip, which today reads "Clients join by invitation from their coach, never from a public sign-up.", loses its second half and gains no link, since the plan's card 34 keeps the trainee offer on its own page instead of the coaches' homepage and 24 fills the house from the gym and WhatsApp (the other way: one quiet link to `/train`); the "You are not part of a coaching space right now" screen stays as it is and shows no paid offer (the plan's rule under 34). On the trainee's Today the coach card reads the house, not a person: default "Self-directed · TB Gym coaching space" with no Message link, Training's "Your coach will put your program here." replaced by "Start a workout" opening N9b's pool (the other way: keep the coach card naming the house's Coach, which invites messages the house does not answer daily).
- **N9b · Free: the exercise pool and a workout of one's own.** Follows 26 and 27 as the owner changed and checked them on 11 Nov 2026, 28, 30, 32 and 34. When it opens it is split again: N9b1 the plan that never ends and the pool's read (server), N9b2 a workout of one's own (server and player), N9b3 the pool's page, the looping clips and the limit. The plan that never ends: a Free product in the house (an ordinary `CoachingProduct` with a zero-price `ProductOffer` covering Training; a free offer activates at once, SUB-008) that the server starts by itself the day a sign-up is confirmed (`ClientEnrollment.Assign`, which makes a zero-price enrollment Active at creation; idempotent on the profile). Free never ends but an offer is a fixed length (SUB-013; recurring is reserved): default, a long fixed length that a Worker job renews before its last day as a new linked row (SUB-003, never an edit); the other way, an open-ended enrollment, which touches the half-open periods and platform billing, so it needs a domain decision. Free stays under a paid plan (11 Nov 2026): default, Free's offer lets Training be covered beside another plan (`AllowsConcurrentCoverage`, SUB-004's stated exception, the one session packs use), so Free is never ended or started again around a purchase, a paid plan waiting for its money never locks Training, and the GiST exclusion stays exactly as it is, still keeping two paid plans apart; the other way, this row's first design, ends Free the day a paid plan starts and starts it again the day after that plan ends, two more moving parts and a locked trainee whenever one of them is late. Free raises no plan notices (activated, ending soon, expired). The pool: the house's exercise library, which the house's Coach fills (a new workspace starts with none, and no catalogue is seeded); a new client read lists its exercises in use for a house trainee who has Training access, with each one's name, type, equipment, movement, muscles, instructions and demos, searched as the coach's library is (LIB-003), never an archived one (LIB-004) and never another workspace's; this is the library grant MED-002 already names, built for the house alone, and a coach's client still reads no library (`B9ExerciseLibraryTests` keeps its refusal). A trainee writes nothing to the pool. The demos: an exercise's media as today (an uploaded picture, MP4 or WebM, or a link that opens on YouTube or Vimeo, MED-016), which a house trainee may now open from the pool and not only from a program they were given (ADR 0011 and the media grant, which are amended); a clip plays like a GIF, muted, looping and inline, in the pool and in the player (C20's fourth slice, built here; today a demo opens in an overlay with play controls). The 3D motion library (the owner's maybe, 11 Nov 2026): the pool works with or without it; once the owner has bought it and its licence is read (C20: a one-time licence that allows a paid app and keeping and converting the files, tried on its free sample first; the trainer partner picks the clips), default, a command beside `platform-admin` loads a folder of converted clips and a sheet of names, types, equipment and muscles into the house's library alone, since its clips are counted in hundreds (LIB-001: under its licence, into no other workspace); the other way, by hand through the library's upload, which exists. A workout of one's own: a trainee starts a workout with no program behind it, adds exercises from the pool, logs each set (weight, unit, reps, effort, a note) and finishes; it then stands in their history, counts for their records (TRN-019), feeds "last time" in the player (TRN-029) and can be shared (TRN-030), as a program's workout does. Today no workout exists without a program session: `WorkoutExecution.Start` takes a `TrainingSession`, `MesocycleId` and `TrainingSessionId` are required and immutable, every exercise and set row names a prescription, no row is ever deleted, and every client read joins a session or a program. Default, the same workout tables with no session behind the row: the two ids and the prescription ids may be empty together and only together (a CHECK), the exercise is its own "prescribed" one so the swap CHECK stands, exercises and sets are added while the workout is in progress (the triggers refuse an insert only once it is finished), and history, records, "last time" and the player keep one source, which already reads by client and exercise; the other way, tables of its own, which leave the protected rows untouched and pay for it with a second copy of every read and of the player. Either way TRN-008 and TRN-012 gain the case, the protections stay (a finished workout frozen, nothing rewritten, Training access asked on every write as `ClientExecutionRefusalAsync` does), an exercise added by mistake leaves before the workout is finished without any row of a program's workout becoming deletable, and ADR 0007 is amended. No calculations: a workout of one's own holds typed weights only, so nothing on Free is worked out from a max, an effort chart or "Last time + step"; that is Pro's (Step 5), and no gate by plan exists in the code today for it to borrow. The limit (27, as checked on 11 Nov 2026): every logged workout stays readable; a setting, empty by default, caps how far back a Free trainee's Progress reaches (today 84 days by default and 366 at most, for every client), and with 28 another caps the routines they keep; what is over a limit is kept and opens again under any paid plan, with one sentence saying so (the words are the owner's); the numbers are the owner's, read from the settings, never a constant; the other way, this row's first reading, locks older workouts. Today for a self-directed trainee: "Start a workout", or "Carry on" while one is unfinished, where a coach's client reads their session; the week's counts take their own workouts in. Progress's training part counts finished workouts for a trainee with no program, where it counts sessions done against sessions planned for a coach's client. The house's own storage allowance: today one `MaxWorkspaceStorageBytes` covers every workspace, and the per-client photo allowance alone would let a few dozen trainees fill it; default, a per-workspace override the platform admin sets (the house's bigger, with room for the clips); the other way, photos only on paid plans. Daily tracking (weight, measurements, photos) exists and changes nothing.
- **N9c · Pro+ and Max.** Follows 29, 30, 31, 34, 36, the owner's word of 11 Nov 2026, and 3 and 13 (no money before the company's Whish account exists, and decision 3 registers the company only once the founding coaches have said yes and the app shows real use; a personal Whish account cannot take it, C17). The plans: Pro+ and Max as house products with immutable offers (Pro+ covers Training, and Check-ins too only if 29's note puts the monthly check there; Max covers Training, Nutrition, Check-ins and Messaging; a monthly length, 29; a new price or length is a new offer, SUB-002). Asking and paying (changed on 11 Nov 2026): a plan is booked when its money is recorded, never when it is asked for. "Ask for a plan" stores an append-only ask naming the offer, on the model of `RenewalRequest`, and books nothing; the screen shows the offer's price, the house's Whish number (a setting of the house's own beside `PlatformBilling:WhishNumber`) and "I've paid", which asks for the Whish name and number used; the ask and "I've paid" each tell the house's staff in-app, and they are the only staff inbox notices a self-directed trainee raises. When the money is there, the house's staff book the plan from that workspace day and record its receipt in one step (default: one staff route doing both in one transaction, idempotent on its key, the exact price only; the other way: today's two steps, `POST /api/commercial/clients/{clientProfileId}/enrollments` then `POST /enrollments/{enrollmentId}/payments`, which leave a booked plan waiting, its days running, if the second is forgotten). The receipt is append-only, the exact price grants access, same currency (SUB-007, SUB-008), and access follows `ICoachingFeatureAccessService` as for any client (SUB-009). Until then the trainee is on Free, with nothing locked and no paid day spent; this row's first design let the trainee book a plan waiting for payment, which ended Free at once and started the pass's days before it granted anything (a plan covers from its first day however late it is paid, SUB-018). The next pass is asked for the same way and booked from the day after the running one ends, or from the day its money is recorded when that is later; the coaching spaces' Ask to renew (SUB-014, ADR 0029) reads a plan that has run out, which a trainee with Free underneath never has, so the house's ask is its own. A trainee who later joins a coach keeps their house plans running to their end, since 34 dropped the no-renewal rule. Pro+, the programs: the programs page lists the trainer partner's programs for every trainee, each with a line on whom it suits (the trainer partner's words, 30) and a sample week, and Start only under a paid pass; a trainee on Free reads "Ask for Pro+" there, the way from Free to a program. Start is a new client route: it copies a published program as the trainee's own snapshot with every week showing (`RevealAllWeeks`, TRN-006), inside the pass's dates through `TrainingCoveragePolicy` (TRN-011), one at a time (the primary-program overlap constraint stays), with no "program assigned" notice (NOT-035) since they started it themselves; the trainee types their own maxes before the first week when the program works its weights out from them (a client route beside the coach's `POST /api/strength/clients/{clientProfileId}/maxes`, checked for sensible values and kept append-only, STR-015). A program longer than a pass (11 Nov 2026): default, the trainer partner writes it as blocks, each no longer than the pass, in order; a trainee starts block 1, and the next block opens once the one before is finished or its pass has ended, under the next pass, their maxes and history carried on, so what a second month buys is the next block; the other ways: a pass as long as the program (a second offer, SUB-002) or a program running across passes (a domain decision: TRN-011 keeps a program inside one plan). A block fits a pass only if it ends inside it, so the page says the last day a block can still be started and Start is refused after it with the reason; a pass exactly as long as a block leaves no day to start late, and the pass's length against the blocks' is the owners' (Open). With Free underneath, Training is always granted, so a program's sessions ask for more: they open only while the plan the program was started under still grants (today the client routes ask whether the client has Training, whatever plan gives it). A trainee may start any session of the current week, not only on its day: TRN-020's 409 `session_not_due` is relaxed for self-directed trainees to the workspace week (the other way: keep the day, as in the test, where the house's Coach sets each tester's training days). When a pass ends the trainee is on Free again, and what they logged stays theirs and readable. Max, by hand: the goal call is Point 3's (health answers typed into the app only, 36); the house's Coach assigns the picked program through the builder as for any client (exists), builds the trainee's meal plan from the house's recipes and assigns it as for any client (31: the meal-plan builder and `POST /api/nutrition/clients/{clientProfileId}/plans` exist, and the plan becomes the trainee's own `ClientNutritionPlan` snapshot; assigning it already takes a calculation of the trainee's targets, `AssignNutritionPlanRequest`'s `CalculationSnapshotId`, so the trainee sees the counted calories and the targets beside it, with no new code), assigns a monthly check-in form (the Check-ins module), and answers questions in Messages; the trainee's Health step opens only on Max (33), and the intake's five steps skip 'health' for a house trainee who is not on Max (CLI-004 still keeps birth date, height and a goal). Max's places (11 Nov 2026): Max is the trainer partner's hours, so the house holds a limit on Max trainees at a time, a setting the owner fills from those hours and the server keeps; when it is reached an ask for Max is still stored, in order, and the page says a place is not open yet (the words are the owner's), since asks are the count that says whether Max is wanted. What a month of Max holds (how often the program is looked at again, how soon a message is answered, what the monthly check asks) is the partners' to write before Max is sold, and the screen says only what they wrote. The consumer-law cancellation window the plan names needs the lawyer's wording: left out, needs the owner.
- **N9d · The owner privacy split (C8, slice 1).** Follows 33 and C8's decision of 14 Oct 2026. Before Max opens. `CoachClientScope` answers two questions, may manage and may coach, and every client route is sorted into one: the Owner sees the business side of every client (name, coach, plan and payment status, renewals, activity counts) and the private side (training and food logs, check-in answers, weigh-ins, measurements, progress photos, intake answers, the health fields on `ClientProfile`) opens only to the client's own coach; an owner who coaches is simply the coach of their own clients. A client's coach may be empty: ADR 0026's "exactly one coach" becomes "at most one", its database guards change with it, a departing coach's clients wait for a coach instead of moving to the owner (their programs keep running, nobody opens their private side), Coach Today lists "clients waiting for a coach" first, and every change stays in the append-only coach history with the client told. Coach Today, notifications, search, share cards, exports and the Team page's sentence follow. ADR 0026 is amended, a business decision already taken. C8's slice 2, the front-desk role, and its two small choices stay open and are not in this row.
- **N9e · Closing and anonymising an account.** Follows the plan's first-version list (built before sign-up opens) and the launch checklist's privacy item, by hand for the founding cohort. A trainee closes their account from Me: today a client can only leave a workspace (`POST /api/client-profile/me/leave`, ADR 0027 keeps the record read-only for the owner). Closing ends every membership, anonymises the person (name, email, phone, the health fields, intake answers; photos tombstoned and purged through the media purge, ADR 0014) and keeps the facts that may not be deleted under the anonymous profile: payments, enrollments, finished workouts and records (SYS-004; the append-only triggers refuse a delete, so this is a defined operation, never a row delete). What is kept for how long is the lawyer's list; the retention periods are a setting: left out, needs the owner. The platform admin can do the same on a request by hand.

*Step 4, later.* Find a coach (decision 35, 7 Nov 2026: the turn by hand until then; plan Step 4; C15's opt-in coach page): a list inside the app of checked coaches who opted in, in a neutral order, no commission. *Step 5, later.* Own routines and Pro (28, 32; the order to settle): a trainee saves a workout of their own as a routine to start again, simple and with no weeks, Free keeping only a few (26); Pro adds their own max, % of max, the effort chart, "+X kg next time" and "turn this routine into a block", refused by the server without Pro; the coach's full builder stays a coach tool. *Step 6, later.* "Share my TB Gym log with my coach", read-only and revocable.

**Rules** (server-side, for every part)

- The house is one ordinary workspace, marked as the house; every trainee is a `ClientProfile` in it with `TenantId`, the global query filter, the write guard, tenant-aware indexes and cross-tenant negative tests (ARCHITECTURE section 5). No new tenancy model, no second database.
- A trainee reaches only the client self-service routes (`/api/training/me`, `/api/client-profile`, `/api/client-plan/me`, `/api/client-renewal/me`, `/api/client-access/me`, progress, nutrition, check-ins, messages), every lookup through `ClientProfiles.CurrentFor(userId)`; nothing staff-only opens to a trainee, and the coach's builder, templates, assigning, Build next block, Edit weeks and every write to the exercise library stay staff-only (TRN rules; the plan counts about 45 such operations); the pool is a read of its own, for the house's trainees alone (N9b).
- Feature access stays `ICoachingFeatureAccessService` (SUB-009, ADR 0006): Free grants Training by its zero-price enrollment, for good and beside any paid plan (N9b); Pro+ grants its programs by the pass; Max what its offer covers. Which tier a trainee is on is read on the server from the house's plans that grant today. What Free leaves out (programs, calculations, what is over the limit of 27), a program's sessions once the plan it was started under no longer grants, Max's places and the weekly sign-up cap are refused by the server, never only hidden in Angular.
- The money objects never collapse: `CoachingProduct` → immutable `ProductOffer` → dated `ClientEnrollment` → feature coverage and append-only `PaymentRecord`. A renewal is a new enrollment; a new price, length or currency a new offer; the GiST exclusion on tenant, client, feature and date range and `Phase2CommercialWorkflowTests.ConcurrentOverlapAndWorkspaceScopedBlockingRemainTenantSafe` stay intact; receipts match the enrollment's currency and a part grants nothing; no FX, credit, refunds, waiver or recurring billing without an approved domain decision (AGENTS.md). Prices live in the house's offers, set by its staff in the app, never in code or docs.
- A paid plan is booked when its money is recorded, never when it is asked for (11 Nov 2026): an ask is an append-only request and books nothing, so nothing a trainee taps ends, locks or shortens what they have, and no paid day passes before it is paid for.
- A workout of one's own is the trainee's record (11 Nov 2026): tenant-owned with the global filter and the write guard, reached only through `CurrentFor`, written only with Training access, holding typed weights only. Nothing is prescribed in it, so nothing prescribed can be overwritten (TRN-012, TRN-015), and a finished one is as frozen as a program's.
- Append-only: payments, enrollment history, strength observations and maxes, legal acceptances, the coach history. Legal wording is never invented: the consumer terms, the privacy wording and the age line are the lawyer's, published by the platform admin (CLI-010).
- Health data (`Allergies`, `Medications`, `PreviousInjuries`, intake answers, photos) never reaches logs, notices, analytics or exceptions (CLI-008, NOT-006); no AI reads it (36); the Health step opens only on Max (33).
- Who may do what: the house's Owner and Coach see every trainee (33) until N9d narrows the Owner to the business side; the platform admin's role stays global and separate (PBL-006) and the house's marker is set only by its command. The house's staff are read-only while the house's own platform invoice is unpaid (PBL-005); that invoice counts every trainee as a billable client (PBL-002), which the test's full discount settles; whether the house is ever billed is the owner's.
- Time: the house runs on its workspace time zone (ADR 0003's default), notices in tenant time; `/train` says so.
- A trainee who is also a coach's client holds two memberships on one account (ADR 0002, CLI-001); neither space learns of the other.

**Where** (what exists today, and what each part extends)

- Joining: `src/backend/Modules/Tenancy/TB.Gym.Modules.Tenancy/TenancyEndpoints.cs` (`POST /api/auth/register/coach`, anonymous, antiforgery, `PublicAuthentication` rate limit, `legal_acceptance_required`) and `TB.Gym.Infrastructure/Application/WorkspaceApplicationService.RegisterCoachAsync` (user, tenant, Owner membership, acceptances, confirmation mail in one transaction); clients join only by `POST /api/invitations/accept` (`Modules/Invitations/.../InvitationEndpoints.cs`, `Infrastructure/Application/InvitationApplicationService.cs`, which sends an existing email to sign in first), ADR 0002. Angular: `features/auth/auth.routes.ts` (sign-in, register, forgot, reset, confirm), `features/auth/register-coach.ts`, `features/invitations/accept-invitation.ts`; the no-space screen `features/dashboard/dashboard.html` ("You are not part of a coaching space right now"); `features/home/home-content.ts` (the invitation sentence); `core/theme/redesigned-route.ts` (`HOUSE_BRAND`). No trainee sign-up, no `/train` route, no sign-up cap and no house marker exist.
- Spam guards: `Modules/Website/TB.Gym.Modules.Website/ContactRequestRules.cs` (honeypot, stamp, lengths), `WebsiteEndpoints.cs` (`PublicContact` policy), `src/web/nginx.conf` (`tb_gym_contact` limit zone), WEB-002, ADR 0037; `RateLimitPolicies` in `TB.Gym.SharedKernel/ExecutionContext.cs`.
- Tenancy and scope: `Modules/Tenancy/.../Tenant.cs` (`Tenant`: Name, Slug, TimeZoneId, DefaultCurrencyCode, IsActive, brand; `TenantMembership`: Owner, Coach, Client), `Infrastructure/Security/TenantAuthorization.cs` (`X-Tenant-Id`, `clientProfileId`/`clientId` route checks, `AllowedWhileWorkspaceReadOnly`), `Infrastructure/Application/CoachClientScope.cs`, `ClientProfileQueries.CurrentFor`.
- Clients: `Modules/Clients/.../ClientProfile.cs` (`AssignedCoachUserId`, `BirthDate`, the health fields, `IsCoachBlocked`, `ReleasedAtUtc`), `ClientEndpoints.cs` (`/api/client-profile/me`, `/me/intake`, `/me/leave`, `/me/complete-onboarding`; `/api/coach-today`), `CoachAttentionPolicy.cs` (CLI-018). No self-directed flag and no account closing exist.
- Money: `Modules/Subscriptions/.../CommercialDomain.cs` (`CoachingProduct`, `ProductOffer.CreateFixedDuration`, `ClientEnrollment.Assign`, Active at once when the price is zero, `Activate` for a pending one, `EnrollmentEntitlement.BlocksOverlap`, `PaymentRecord`), `CommercialEndpoints.cs` (staff products, offers, enrollments, payments, renew, pause, resume, cancel; client `GET /api/client-access/me`, `GET /api/client-plan/me`, `POST /api/client-renewal/me/requests`), `OwnPlans.cs`, `RenewalRequests.cs`; SUB-001 to SUB-018, ADR 0005, 0029, 0034; Angular `features/account/client-plan.ts` (My plan), `features/commercial/products.ts` (Plans you sell). No client route books a plan or says "I've paid". An offer's features carry `AllowsConcurrentCoverage` (SUB-004; session packs use it, `SessionPacks.cs`), `EnrollmentEntitlement.BlocksOverlap` is what the GiST exclusion reads, and `Infrastructure/Application/CoachingFeatureAccessService.cs` grants a feature when any plan covering the day is active and paid in full, so Free beside a paid plan keeps Training granted; `RenewalRequests.cs` is the model for an append-only ask.
- Training: `Modules/Training/.../TrainingCoveragePolicy.cs` (a mesocycle inside its enrollment, training included, not terminated), `TrainingEndpoints.cs` (`/api/training/me`: today, upcoming, week, program, personal-records, `sessions/{sessionId}/start` with `session_not_due`, set saves, complete; coach: templates, mesocycles, training-maxes preview/apply, progression, visibility), `TrainingBlueprints.cs` (`TrainingLoadStrategy`: None, Direct, PercentageWorkingMax, RpeBasedEpley, RpeChart), `Infrastructure/Application/TrainingNotices.cs` (ProgramAssigned, NOT-035); TRN-001, 003, 006, 011, 019, 020, 023, 029, 030; Angular `features/training/today-training.*` (the player), `training.routes.ts` (`/training/program`, `/training/today`; coach `/training/programs`, `/training/exercises`), `features/dashboard/client-today.html` (the coach card, "Your coach will put your program here.", "Training is not available").
- The exercise pool (11 Nov 2026): `Modules/ExerciseLibrary/TB.Gym.Modules.ExerciseLibrary/Exercise.cs` (`Exercise`: name, instructions, equipment, movement pattern, type, archived; its muscles, tags, alternatives and media links), `ExerciseEndpoints.cs` (`/api/exercises`: search, read, create, update, archive, every route `TenantCoach`), `Infrastructure/Application/ExerciseLibraryApplicationService.cs`, `Infrastructure/Persistence/GymDbContext.ExerciseLibrary.cs` (schema `exercise_library`, a name unique in its workspace); LIB-001 to LIB-005, MED-002 ("A coach may grant a client explicit full-library access per tenant", not built: "today no client reads the library"), ADR 0007; `B9ExerciseLibraryTests` pins that a client reads none of it. No catalogue is seeded: a new workspace has no exercises, and only the development demo carries a list of its own (`DemoWorkspaceCast.Exercises`). Angular: `features/training/exercise-library.*`, `exercise-sheet.ts`, `exercise-demo.ts`. A client's reads carry an exercise's name, the coach's notes, approved swaps and media ids (`ClientExerciseView` in `TrainingContracts.cs`), never its instructions, muscles or equipment, though the coach's exercise sheet says of Instructions "Clients read this in their workout, beside the demo."
- Demos: `Modules/Media/TB.Gym.Modules.Media/MediaAsset.cs` (purpose `ExerciseMedia`: an uploaded JPEG or PNG up to 15 MB, an MP4 or WebM up to 500 MB, or a YouTube or Vimeo link that opens on the provider's page, MED-015, MED-016), `MediaEndpoints.cs` (staff upload, link and delete; `POST /api/media/{assetId}/access` for any member), `Infrastructure/Application/MediaApplicationService.cs` (a client is granted exercise media only with Training access and only when their own prescription or workout snapshot holds it, ADR 0011); media is copied into a program at assignment and into a workout at its start (`ExercisePrescriptionMediaSnapshot`, `WorkoutExerciseMediaSnapshot`); the player's "Watch demo" opens a video with play controls in an overlay (`today-training.html`, `external-video.ts`). Nothing plays muted, looping and inline, no GIF is accepted, and nothing in the code knows a 3D library (C20).
- A workout (11 Nov 2026): `Modules/Training/TB.Gym.Modules.Training/WorkoutExecution.cs` (`WorkoutExecution.Start` takes a `TrainingSession`; `WorkoutExercisePerformance` an `ExercisePrescription`; `WorkoutSetPerformance` a `SetPrescription`, with `RecordActual`'s limits), `Infrastructure/Persistence/GymDbContext.Training.cs` (`training."WorkoutExecutions"`: `MesocycleId` and `TrainingSessionId` required, one workout per session; the triggers `protect_workout_execution`, `protect_workout_exercise` and `protect_workout_set`: no delete, a finished workout frozen, the snapshots immutable, an insert refused only once the workout is finished; `CK_WorkoutExercisePerformances_Substitution`), `Infrastructure/Application/TrainingApplicationService.Client.cs` (the one caller of `Start`; Training access asked again on every client write, `ClientExecutionRefusalAsync`, for the client and the feature, not for the plan a program sits under); TRN-008, TRN-012, TRN-015; the client's reads join a session or a program (`/me/today` and `/me/week` by session, `/me/history` by the program for its name, ten to a page with no window, `/me/personal-records` the newest twelve); `WorkoutPersonalRecordRule` (TRN-019), "last time" (`LoadPreviousPerformancesAsync`) and `LiftStandingsReader` read by client and exercise. No workout exists without a program session, no client adds an exercise or a set, no client starts a program themselves (`POST /api/training/clients/{clientProfileId}/mesocycles` is staff-only), and no load strategy is gated by a plan.
- Progress: `Modules/Progress/TB.Gym.Modules.Progress/ProgressEndpoints.cs` (`GET /api/progress/me/dashboard`), `Infrastructure/Application/ProgressApplicationService.cs` (a window of 84 days by default and 366 at most; its training part counts sessions done against sessions planned); no client reads a graph of one lift.
- Strength: `Modules/Strength/.../StrengthEndpoints.cs` (`/api/strength/clients/{clientProfileId}/maxes`, `/maxes/from-set`, the estimates; all coach-side), STR-011 to STR-015, ADR 0036. No client-side max route exists.
- Nutrition (Max's meal plan, 31): `Modules/Nutrition/TB.Gym.Modules.Nutrition/NutritionDomain.cs` (`Recipe` and its versions, `MealPlanTemplate` and `MealPlanTemplateVersion`, `ClientNutritionPlan`, `NutritionCalculationSnapshot`), `NutritionEndpoints.cs` (coach `/api/nutrition`: recipes, meal-plan drafts and publish, `clients/{clientProfileId}/calculations` and `clients/{clientProfileId}/plans`; client `/api/nutrition/me`), `NutritionContracts.cs` (`AssignNutritionPlanRequest`, which carries the `CalculationSnapshotId` whose targets the client sees), DOMAIN-RULES section 6; Angular `features/nutrition/` (`meal-plan-builder.*`, the recipe screens, `client-nutrition.*`). Nothing in it is house-specific.
- Legal: `Modules/Identity/.../LegalConsent.cs` (`LegalDocumentKind`: TermsOfService, PrivacyPolicy, HealthIntakeConsent; `LegalConsentContext`: Platform, Workspace), `LegalConsentEndpoints.cs` (current, to-accept, consents, the admin's publish and withdraw), CLI-010, ADR 0006; Angular `features/legal/*`, `features/platform-admin/legal-documents.ts`, `legal-publish.ts`. No kind for consumer terms exists.
- Intake: `features/intake/intake-steps.ts` (five steps, one of them 'health'), `features/profile/client-profile.ts`; CLI-004, 006, 008.
- Media: `Modules/Media/.../MediaContracts.cs` (`MediaStorageOptions.MaxWorkspaceStorageBytes`, one value for every workspace; `MaxClientProgressPhotoBytes`), MED-009, ADR 0014. No per-workspace allowance exists.
- Platform billing: `Modules/PlatformBilling/.../PlatformBillingEndpoints.cs` (`/api/platform-admin/workspaces/{tenantId}/discounts`, `/discounts/{discountId}/revoke`), `PlatformInvoice.cs` (`WorkspaceDiscount`: Percent, StartsOn, EndsOnExclusive, Note), `PlatformPricePlan.cs`; PBL-001 to 009, ADR 0028; `PlatformBillingTests.PlatformBillingOnlyTheReviewedRoutesStayOpenWhileAWorkspaceIsReadOnly` pins the read-only list (`ReviewedReadOnlyExemptions`); the `platform-admin` command in `TB.Gym.Api/Program.cs`.
- Notices: `Modules/Notifications/.../NotificationTemplates.cs` (ProgramAssigned, RenewalRequested, ClientLeft, ClientWorkoutNote, EnrollmentActivated, EnrollmentEndingSoon, EnrollmentExpired, EnrollmentRenewed, PaymentRequired, WeeksChanged, WeeksShared), NOT-006, NOT-035.
- Worker: `src/backend/TB.Gym.Worker` sweeps action mail, the notification outbox and platform billing; no sweep of unconfirmed accounts exists.
- Demo: `Infrastructure/Initialization/DemoWorkspaceGenerator.cs` registers its workspace through `RegisterCoachAsync`; `scripts/demo-workspace.ps1`; `DemoWorkspaceTests`. The demo gets a house with a few trainees only in the part that first needs one (N9b).
- Docs: C8, C15, C17 and C20 in `docs/CONCERNS-BRIEF.md`; ADR 0002 (clients invite-only in Phase 1) and DOMAIN-RULES section 11 (items 3 and 6) need an amendment, a new ADR of one page for the house (a business decision and a module boundary: which module owns the house marker and the trainee sign-up; default Tenancy for both, since the coach's sign-up endpoint lives in `TenancyEndpoints.cs` and the marker is a fact about a tenant, with the orchestration in `Infrastructure/Application` beside `RegisterCoachAsync`, which may compose Clients, Identity and Subscriptions as it does today; the other way: a module of its own for the house, if it grows rules no other module owns); `docs/ROADMAP.md` gains the house as a step after go-live, since it is outside the agreed order today.

**Tests** (each part, as AGENTS.md asks)

- Domain: the age check, the house marker, the weekly sign-up cap over the workspace's week, Free's renewal dates (half-open, each row following the last), the tier the house's plans that grant today make, a workout of one's own (an exercise in use in the house's pool, typed weights only, frozen once finished), the limit's window, one Pro+ program at a time, a block that would end past its pass refused, the day a plan is booked from when its money is recorded, Max's places, anonymising keeps the facts; fixed reference tests for any rule with a version name.
- Integration against PostgreSQL: an anonymous sign-up with the guards (the honeypot kept nowhere, the stamp, the limits, the weekly cap), antiforgery refused, the confirmation link, the consumer terms asked only there, the sweep; unauthorized (an anonymous caller on client routes, a trainee on every staff route, a trainee on another trainee's rows); wrong tenant (a sign-up cannot land in a coach's workspace, a trainee's reads under another workspace's `X-Tenant-Id`, a coach's offer cannot be booked by a trainee, a coach's workspace never sees a house profile); constraints (the GiST overlap untouched by Free beside a paid plan and still refusing two paid plans at once, a booking racing the Free renewal, the append-only triggers on payments, maxes and asks, the workout triggers still refusing a delete or a rewrite of a program's workout); concurrency (two sign-ups with one email, two asks at once, one ask booked twice, the last Max place asked for twice, idempotency keys bound to payloads); the read-only allow list unchanged; health fields absent from every log line (`CapturedTestLog`). Extend `A11aSignUpTests`, `A16aLegalConsentTests`, `Phase1WorkflowTests`, `Phase2CommercialWorkflowTests`, `Step3RenewalRequestTests`, `Phase3TrainingWorkflowTests`, `P2TrainingMaxesTests`, `ReleaseClientTests`, `CoachDepartureAndClientReturnTests`, `PlatformBillingTests`, `R62gTalkToUsTests`, `Phase5B5MediaQuotaTests`, and `Phase4NutritionHardeningTests` for Max's meal plan; architecture `ModuleDependencyTests` and `TenancyModelInvariantTests` for any new module reference or tenant-owned table. Each part that changes server code runs the backend tests of section 1, and the runner's final check runs the whole suite.
- Integration, for what changed on 11 Nov 2026: the pool (a house trainee reads the house's exercises in use and opens their demos, never an archived one and never another workspace's, and writes none; without Training access nothing is read; a coach's client still reads no library, `B9ExerciseLibraryTests` unchanged); a workout of one's own (started, an exercise added, sets saved, finished, then read in history, records and "last time"; another trainee's is 404; refused without Training access; frozen once finished); Free beside a paid plan (Training granted while an ask waits and after a pass ends; a program's sessions refused once its pass no longer grants); an ask books nothing, and one step books the plan and records its receipt.
- Vitest: the `/train` page and its full-week line, the sign-up form and its reasons, Today's self-directed card with Start a workout and Carry on, the pool's page and its looping clip, the player for a workout of one's own (add an exercise, log, finish), the limit's sentence, the programs page with Ask for Pro+ and Start, Ask for a plan and I've paid, Max's places, the Health step skipped, Me's Close account.
- Mocked Playwright: `e2e/auth.e2e.ts`, `legal.e2e.ts`, `home.e2e.ts`, `client-today.e2e.ts`, `client-training.e2e.ts`, `me.e2e.ts`, `client-service.e2e.ts` extended; a new `train.e2e.ts`.
- Live, on a scratch demo with a house: `e2e/live/register-and-confirm.e2e.ts`, `invite-and-onboard.e2e.ts`, `sell-and-pay.e2e.ts`, `update-maxes.e2e.ts`, `security-tenancy.e2e.ts` and `unpaid-read-only.e2e.ts` as models; a trainee signs up, confirms, opens the pool, logs a workout of their own, asks for Pro+, keeps training on Free until the house's staff record the payment, then starts a block with their own max; stills at 390 and 1440 in light and dark, axe clean, no sideways scroll, 200% text.

**Left out** (and why)

- One-to-one coaching: not a tier of the house. Decision 18 (7 Nov 2026) puts no promise about it in the founding letter, and the trainer partner will sell one-to-one sessions; how they are sold, and the later paid consultation in 30, are later steps, not this row. The trainer partner is not in the turn when a trainee asks for a coach (decision 4).
- Own routines and Pro (28, 32), the Find a coach list (35, plan Step 4), the shared log (Step 6): later steps, each a row of its own when the owner opens it.
- A new max a trainee's logged sets suggest, accepted with one tap: needs the owner, who took suggested maxes out of the app (decision 19's note, 7 Nov 2026; N10a and N10b took them out, N10b with their rule, BestLoggedSet v1, which git and ADR 0036 keep).
- A readiness screen for people training without a coach: only if the lawyer says so; TB Gym's own questions, written with a qualified reviewer.
- The front-desk role (C8 slice 2) and its two small choices.
- Any price, discount, refund, cancellation-window or retention rule, and every legal wording: needs the owner and the lawyer; never written in code or docs.
- Buying the 3D motion library: the owner's step, after its free sample is tried and its licence read (C20); N9b builds the looping clip and the command that loads the library, and the pool opens with or without it.
- Saved routines and a routine builder for trainees: Step 5 (28). A workout of one's own is logged, not saved to be started again.
- The pool or a workout of one's own in a coach's space: a coach's clients train on what their coach gave them, and MED-002's library grant is built for the house alone.
- A trainee's own exercises: nobody but the house's staff adds to the pool.
- A graph of one lift for a trainee: no client has one today.
- Point 6's self-guided drop rule for programs nobody watches, Point 3's in-app goal form and AI suggestions, Meta's API (decision 38, 7 Nov 2026: later, once 3 coaches ask), Whish automation (Step 5), card payments, holding money for coaches (C17).
- Code for the test itself: Step 2 runs by hand on what exists.

**Open** (what this row waits on, and what each would change)

- Decision 24's note, what counts as the first major test: fixes when the test is done and N9a may open; the test itself needs no code and runs on an ordinary workspace with a platform-admin discount. Its pass mark (Step 2: who is invited and how many, how long, and the counts that mean it passed) is the partners' to write down before the first invitation.
- The company and the consumer terms (13 fixes the rule: no money before the company's Whish account; decision 3, 7 Nov 2026, registers the company only once the founding coaches have said yes and the app shows real use; the consumer terms are the lawyer's text, 24's note): N9a cannot ask for terms that do not exist and N9c cannot take money before the account; N9e's closing routine is ready before the first stranger joins; whether the free part of the test may start before the consumer terms is the owner's and the lawyer's (decision 2 settles only the health consent at go-live).
- What the test counts while no tester can pay: with decision 3 the test may run with no tester paying, so "how many testers pay" can be counted only once the company exists; what the test must show meanwhile (asks for a paid plan, say) is the partners' call with 24's note.
- Decision 25's note, what "their own coach" means on screen: the coach card, check-ins and attention for a self-directed trainee (N9a builds the defaults above and lists them in section 3 unless the owner says otherwise first).
- The limit on Free (27, checked against Hevy on 11 Nov 2026): N9b's default keeps every logged workout readable and limits how far back Progress reaches and, with 28, the routines kept, each a setting that stays empty until the owner fills it; the other way locks older workouts, as this row first read the decision. The owner's: which way, the numbers, and whether any limit is switched on before Pro exists to lift it.
- Decision 29's note, whether the monthly check is Pro+'s or Max's: Check-ins on Pro+'s offer or not; the trainer partner's time per trainee.
- Decisions 28 and 32, the order: whether own routines and Pro come before N9c or after it.
