- **N2, the group in the menu.** "TB Gym" heads its two rows after Dark mode, in the heading style of "Coaching spaces", with a hairline above and below so that Sign out does not read as one of TB Gym's pages. The other way: first in the menu, or no hairlines. Recommendation: keep.
- **N2, what is left where sign-up is hidden.** Signed in, the hero and the closing line keep "Talk to us about your gym" alone, the six rows have no link, and the coach's half of Who it's for has no button; on a wide screen its screen is held a button's height lower so the two screens stay in line. The Talk-to-us form stays and can be sent, with its stamp and allowances as for a visitor. The other way: hide the form too, or put another button in the coach's half. Recommendation: keep.
- **N2, the clients strip's "Sign in".** The row did not name it. Signed in, the strip reads "Here as a client? Your coach sends the invitation." without "Already joined? Sign in.", since that link would open Today under another name during a pitch. The other way: leave the sentence whole. Recommendation: keep.
- **N2, who can open `/about`.** Anyone: a client or a former client who types it sees the page with Back to Today and no tabs or member bar, and a visitor sees the homepage with Sign in and Start, its links on `/about`, never counted and marked noindex. The other way: send a client to Today and a visitor to `/`. Recommendation: keep; nothing leads either of them there.
- **N2, the title.** `/about` keeps the homepage's title, "TB Gym: coach both sides of the week". The other way: "About TB Gym". Recommendation: keep.
- **N2, the sign-up page's count.** To be sent, the count needs the script's name, so the sign-up page now reads the website's settings as What changed does (one anonymous read, a visitor's alone); the count carries the page's path and campaign tags and never the rest of its address. Nothing counts on `/about`, signed in or not. The other way: none that sends the count for someone who lands on the page directly. Recommendation: keep.
- **N2, analytics for someone signed in.** The row says `/about` may load the analytics script and count nothing. As built, someone signed in gets the website's settings there (for the WhatsApp link, once there is a number) and no script at all: it is started for a visitor alone, as What changed already did. The other way: load it for everyone and count nothing. Recommendation: keep; a signed-in page has no use for it.
- **N2, the installed app.** `/about` is inside the manifest's scope, so it opens in the installed window, where Back to Today is the way back; the browser checks prove the button at 390 px, and the installed app itself is yours to try on a phone (the plan's own step for Point 5). Not verified here.
- **N2, outside the row: focus after "Stay".** The two new rows close the menu and hand focus back to the account button, so after "Stay" on a page with typed work the keyboard is where it was. The menu's older rows (Password and security, the platform admin's three) still leave focus on the page's body after "Stay", as before. Not changed here. Recommendation: the same hand-off for them, a few lines with a test.
- **N2, outside the row: What changed in dark mode.** The menu's second row opens What changed inside the coach frame, and that page is still drawn light whatever the person's mode: with Dark mode on it is a cream page between a dark sidebar and a dark top bar (the still `changelog-owner-1440-dark.png`). It was so before; until this row nothing in the app led a coach there. Not changed here, since the same page is the public one the build renders ahead of time. Recommendation: a small row that marks it rebuilt, lets the copy inside the app take the app's ink in light and dark, and keeps the public page as it is.
- **N2, outside the row: What changed is a month old.** The page the new row opens, and the date `/about` and the homepage print ("Last change 13 Oct 2026"), come from `src/web/src/content/changelog.md`, whose newest day is 13 Oct 2026: nothing built since is on it (Dark mode, the critique and motion rows, this plan's rows, the TB Gym group itself), because no row was told to add its line. Not changed here: they are public words, and a month of them is more than this row. The other way: add this row's line alone, which would print today's date over a list that misses a month. Recommendation: one row that writes the missing days in a coach's words for you to read, and a line in every later row's checks to add its own.
